# UI/UX review — 2026-10-04

Multi-agent review of the web page (`web/`, HEAD `281af6e`) on the target screen: a
maximized browser on a QHD monitor, 2560x1305 CSS px at 100% scaling. QHD at 125%
(2048x1040, DPR 1.25) is the secondary case. The brief was that the page feels clunky
in places, the new Refine dialog in particular, and that the UI has never been much
liked. No code in the repository was changed. Every prototype was injected into a
running page. This file records the findings and a recommended direction.

**Method.** Eleven area finders drove headless Edge over the DevTools protocol at
2560x1305. Most also ran at 2048x1040 DPR 1.25. Each worked against its own
`tools/fake_server.py` instance with a throwaway gallery. The areas were:

- the visual system and use of the screen
- the generation panel
- running and watching a generation
- the gallery and lightbox
- Refine as a workflow
- the Refine dialog itself
- grid search and the contact sheet
- the prompt check, critic and comparison
- tag-checkpoint prompting (Animagine)
- a task walkthrough as a regular user
- microcopy, help and keyboard

They used real mouse and key events where it mattered and measured with
`getBoundingClientRect` and computed styles.

A merge step grouped their **156 raw findings** into **108 clusters (M1–M108)**. Each
cluster went to a skeptical verifier, who:

- reproduced it on a fake server of their own;
- checked it against CLAUDE.md, `docs/design-notes.md` and the smokes;
- where possible, tried the proposed fix as in-page CSS or JS;
- wrote a final verdict, severity and recommendation.

In parallel, three designers each prototyped a redesign direction as an injected
stylesheet and script. Each took before/after screenshots at 2560x1305,
2048x1040@1.25 and 1400x900.

A completeness critic then named four areas nobody had reached:

- failure and recovery
- destructive actions end to end
- klein's references and LoRA
- Refine on an SDXL tag checkpoint

Four gap finders covered them. Their **34 findings (G1–G4)** went through the same
verifier gate. Finally a judge scored the three directions and wrote a plan.

Of 142 candidates, **141 are kept**: 39 confirmed as reported, and 102 adjusted in
severity, description or recommendation. Some of the adjusted ones reproduced only in
part. One was set aside as a duplicate, and none was left without a verdict. One
verifier's run was briefly hijacked by another agent's driver on a colliding DevTools
port; that run was discarded and redone (see U83).

The gate refuted no whole cluster. Its verifiers corrected where earlier reviews'
refuted: a cluster whose problem reproduced stayed, with its wrong claims and
unworkable fixes struck. Section 5 collects every claim refuted that way, so a
reader can see what the 102 adjustments removed.

One process slip touched the checkout. The results finder's first patched fake
server imported `backend.config` before setting its `LOCAL_DIFFUSION_*` paths. For
about two minutes it wrote `local-diffusion.db` (with `-wal`/`-shm`), four files in
`outputs/` and `tools/__pycache__` into the repository. The finder stopped it and
deleted them. Their creation times and the database's two planted rows identify that
run as their only writer. `git status` was clean afterwards, and nothing tracked
changed.

**Reading the results.** Severity is the verifiers' recalibration. After
verification there are **10 high, 68 medium and 63 low**.

- *High* means wrong output, lost or destroyed work, a state that misleads about a
  job, or a main control that cannot be reached.
- *Medium* means recurring friction in a core flow, or a clear waste of the QHD screen.
- *Low* means polish, copy and rare paths.

Several finders' highs came down to medium (for example U11, U37 and U40), and a few
lows went up.

Frequency is the most frequent estimate among a cluster's sources: every-session,
common, occasional or rare. Where the verifier disputed it, the entry says so.
Entries are ordered by severity, then by frequency.

The recommendations are the verifiers' final ones. Many were tried in the page and
measured, but none is a tested patch, and none has been run against pytest or the
smokes. The three directions are CSS/JS prototypes, not patches. They wrap the page's
global functions from an injected script, which a real change must not do.

U-numbers are this record's own. Each entry ends with the workflow's M or G id, and
the appendix maps the two.

**Paths.** Two bases, told apart by extension:

- **Prototypes and scripts** (`.css`, `.js`, `.py`, `.mjs`) are copied into
  `docs/review-2026-10-04-ui/`, and their paths here are relative to it: the three
  directions' `direction-*/proto.css` and `proto.js`, `verify-analysis-1/variant-C.css`,
  `verify-refine-dialog-1/sigma.py`, and `shot.mjs`, the review's copy of
  `tools/browser.mjs` with a `VIEWPORT`/`DPR` setting and full-page, element, hover,
  typing and modifier-key steps. To see a direction, start a fake server, open the
  page, and put the stylesheet in a `<style>` element and run the script once, in that
  order (`shot.mjs` does it from an `eval` step).
- **Screenshots** (`.png`) are relative to the session scratchpad the review ran in,
  `%TEMP%/claude/C--VSProjects-ImageGeneratorLocal/e3764fe6-d48a-47dd-8366-d08234020524/scratchpad/ux/`.
  The 5,020 shots (559 MB) are not in the repository, and that directory is not kept.
  Each entry states its measured values in the text, so it stands without them.

`docs/review-2026-10-04-ui-findings.json` is the machine-readable version: every kept
entry under its U-number, with the verifier's verdict, rule and smoke notes and the
finders' observations.

## Implementation follow-up — 2026-10-05

All 141 findings have been worked on the branch `ui-review-2026-10-04`, cut from
`main` at `ecbb79a` (the reviewed `281af6e` plus this record), in the 46 commits from
`cf591ca` to `9ec6783`. The final verifiers judged **140 fixed and 1 partly
fixed** (U61). Of the fixed findings, 19 leave an optional or secondary part of
their recommendation undone by decision. Those parts and U61 are listed below, each with its
reason. The original findings below this section are unchanged and remain the historical
review of `281af6e`/`ecbb79a`. `review-2026-10-04-ui-findings.json` gains a `remediation` block that
gives each finding's outcome, change, packages and commits.

**How it was done.**

- *Phase 0* (`cf591ca`, `4cb5a76`, `c58cee0`). `tools/browser.mjs` takes a viewport, a
  device pixel ratio and a port offset, so a smoke can assert the QHD layouts and worktrees
  can run side by side. The page's sizes moved onto tokens on `:root` with no visible change:
  56 screenshots matched pixel for pixel. The panel's sticky top is measured (U1), and
  `tools/smoke-wide.json` holds the wide layouts.
- *Wave 1: seven packages.* Each was implemented in its own worktree, reviewed twice
  (against CLAUDE.md's rules, and for coverage of its findings), fixed, then merged in this
  order:
  - `ui/vis`: the wide tier, the focus ring and the panel's copy
  - `ui/galdel`: deletes, Reuse, and what starred images were made from
  - `ui/grid`: the grid preview and Ctrl+Enter
  - `ui/sheet`: the contact sheet
  - `ui/tag`: the tag chips, autocomplete, the Tags helper and Examples
  - `ui/ana`: the docked prompt check
  - `ui/ref`: the Refine dialog

  Three integration reviews followed (consistency, layout, regressions). Their fixes are
  `f77bb7a`, `e9a0735`, `5a00282` and `c616336`.
- *Wave 2*, done the same way: `ui/gen` (the job flow and the failure states), `ui/galcard`
  (the gallery's cards and the Runs picker) and `ui/lb` (the lightbox). The integration
  fixes are `daacc9e`, `077ac4b` and `69613b5`.
- *Wave 3*: `ui/res`, the results viewer, merged as `d0528f2`.
- *Docs* (`bd8f318`). CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md, which now say the same, plus README and the
  design notes, were brought up to the code. Each pending rule change was checked against
  the code first.
- *Final verification.* Four verifiers re-checked every finding on the integrated tree at
  the review's viewports, reproducing each original observation. Alongside them ran three
  reviewers: one for the documentation, a bug hunter, and a designer who walked a whole
  session at 2560x1305 and 2048x1040@1.25 on FLUX.1 Krea [dev], FLUX.2 [klein] and
  Animagine fakes. They raised 40 issues, taken in `4aeaee7`: 38 fixed and 2 partly, both
  partial by decision (see U5 and U12 below). An independent re-check of that round raised
  one more, the delete dialog's raw red, which `9ec6783` put on a token. The re-check after
  it found nothing open.

**Backend changes the page needed:**

- `POST /api/prompt/measure` (U7)
- ranked suggestions and a reason code in `backend/tags.py` (U52, U113)
- one keep predicate in `storage` for starred images' sources and references (U10)
- `jobs.forget_images` and `deleted_count` (U76, U117)
- `Job.failed_cells`, `stopped_by "failures"` and `Job.comparison` (U74, U75, U70)
- mapped load failures with `ModelStatus.detail_kind`, `detail_url` and `started_at` (U56,
  U73)
- `GridPreview.in_gallery` (U74)
- `parse_axis`'s range handling (U128)
- `refine_measured` (U119)
- `RunSummary.axes`, `refine` and `strengths` (U90)
- the job snapshot's prompt, size, refine and grid fields (U39)

| --- | --- | --- |

| Panel | U14, U15 | A wide tier from 1800 px widens the panel to 400-480 px, ramps the type up (body 15.5 px at 2560, a third of the step at 2048x1040@1.25) and lets the prompt grow to 12 lines. |

**Not fully fixed.**

- **U61, partial.** Part A is in. klein's edit Example is marked "needs a reference
  image", Check adds a heuristic note for an edit prompt with no reference attached, and a
  file dropped on the page no longer navigates away. Part B is not built: importing one's
  own photo or sketch as a reference, which is the problem in the finding's title. It is an
  L-sized feature, and the recommendation itself proposed splitting it off.

The findings below are fixed, but one part of each recommendation was left undone, by
decision:

| Finding | Left undone, and why |
| --- | --- |
| U5 | Kept by decision: beside a long run's strip the docked check keeps the viewer's 36rem minimum (in the first column a comparison's sheet would open at half width); the chips are not kept across both columns, so on a tag checkpoint the image moves down by their extra lines (a 40-tag prompt at 2048x1040@1.25 takes its bottom 8 px past the fold); a `.many` batch is not excluded from the dock. |
| U6 | No live start sigma: for FLUX.1 without a Mu the shift comes from `calculate_shift` inside the pipeline's `__call__`, so reading it would mean re-deriving it outside the pipeline (which `schedule.forced_mu` exists to avoid) or running the pipeline on the gate; the measured note covers the warning. |
| U12 | The optional L-sized two-column stage was not built; the empty width it aimed at is served by the result column (U28) and `#recent` (U34). The live preview of a run of five or more stays the size of the cell its image will take: a larger one would bring back the reflow U26 removed. |
| U16 | The optional Fit/Fill toggle was not built. |
| U27 | The optional ticker in the viewer's placeholder was not built; the progress line, the tab title and the topbar carry the elapsed time. |
| U38 | Not extended to a finding's Apply (optional): an Apply fixes one rule's complaint rather than offering a version to compare. |
| U45 | At 1400x900 the panel's Seed box still cannot show all 16 digits, which the recommendation allowed. |
| U48 | The optional hover-only star on sheet cells was not built. |
| U50 | The optional outline flash was not built. |
| U62 | The optional link from a finding's 'image 3' to its chip was not built. |
| U66 | Part (d), tag autocomplete in the dialog's prompt, was not built (optional). |
| U72 | The verifier's separate note on random-seed iterations (a sheet note pointing to Compare) was out of scope. |
| U79 | A day divider left with no cards is not removed: deleted cards stay as tombstones, so it never ends up empty, and removing it would move cards under the pointer. |
| U85 | The optional server-supplied order slot was not built: it needs per-guide order data, and the review rejects client-side slots. |
| U87 | The optional qualifier on FLUX's CFG label was not added. |
| U96 | The optional S/M density control was not built. |
| U100 | The optional press on the greyed box was removed in review: it stopped a long greyed negative from being scrolled or read. |
| U102 | 'Export all pins' (optional, M-sized) was not built. |
| U108 | The suggested guard ignoring `e.detail > 1` on the arrows was rejected: it would drop every other press of a quick click-walk. |

These took a documented way round the recommendation's letter, which the verifiers judged
sound:

- **U3.** The toggle is a labelled 'Compare with source' button with an S hint rather than the settings line; that line gave way to the facts list, whose '#N' links the source.
- **U15.** The plan's 1800 px gate with a ramp (a third of the increase at 2048x1040@1.25) rather than the verifier's 2200 px gate; height-safe because the panel is held (U1).
- **U35.** The toggle keeps one name, 'Reference image', with its number in the title and `aria-description`.
- **U41.** No `aria-pressed`: the button's words are its state (Pause / Pausing… / Resume).
- **U43.** Inverted mechanism: the user's own closes call `dismissLightbox`, and programmatic closes move no focus.
- **U124.** Up and Down take the nearest card in the next row, measured from layout, since a day divider starts a row of its own.

Two things outside the findings remain:

- *A flaky smoke.* During the fix rounds `smoke-delete` failed intermittently. First, two
  new count-dialog checks failed in full parallel runs; they now compare the server's
  count. Then an older check after Delete unstarred's wrong-count dialog failed twice in a
  row. It has passed every run since, this one included, but the cause was not found.
- *A race that predates this work.* `watchNext` checks `busy` before it awaits
  `/api/jobs`, so a job submitted during that await can be watched twice. It is left as it
  was, and `smoke-grid` spaces its two renders around it.

**Validation**, on the final tree (`9ec6783` plus this record):

- `uv run --group dev pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider` gave
  **1,929 passed, 4 skipped** and 31 dependency deprecation warnings in
  76.41 s. `main` had 1,880 passed.
- **All 32 browser smokes passed**: 1,370 expectations, none failing. Each
  smoke ran on a fresh fake server with its own throwaway gallery, started by the session's
  runner (`run_smokes.py --offset 3500 --jobs 4`, a script outside the repository), which
  found no smoke file it did not know. The 12 new with this work:
  `smoke-analysis-tags`, `smoke-delete`, `smoke-gallery`, `smoke-grid`, `smoke-jobs`,
  `smoke-lightbox`, `smoke-refine-sdxl`, `smoke-results`, `smoke-sheet`,
  `smoke-sheet-klein`, `smoke-tags`, `smoke-wide`.
- The other 20, which were there before:
  `smoke`, `smoke-analysis`, `smoke-anima`, `smoke-animagine`, `smoke-comparison`,
  `smoke-comparison-batch`, `smoke-conditioning`, `smoke-ernie`, `smoke-flux`,
  `smoke-illustrious`, `smoke-kohaku`, `smoke-netayume`, `smoke-newbie`, `smoke-paging`,
  `smoke-preview`, `smoke-refine`, `smoke-refine-unavailable`, `smoke-rewrite`,
  `smoke-wai`, `smoke-z-anime`.
- The smokes run at 1400x900. Some also assert other sizes:
  - at 2560x1305 and 2048x1040@1.25: `smoke-wide`, `-analysis`, `-analysis-tags`,
    `-gallery` and `-results`
  - at 2560x1305: `-grid`, `-lightbox`, `-refine`, `-sheet` and `-sheet-klein`
  - at 2048x1040@1.25 and 800x900: `-delete`
- The final verifiers checked in the browser at the review's two screens, 2560x1305 at DPR
  1 and 2048x1040 at DPR 1.25, with real mouse and key events. Their fake servers claimed
  FLUX.1 Krea [dev], FLUX.2 [klein], Animagine XL 4.0 and Krea 2 Turbo, and one fake was
  killed mid-run and restarted.

**No GPU validation run.** Everything above ran on the tests' fake backend. Nothing in this
work changes the pipeline, the schedule or what reaches the card. No measurement in
`docs/verified.md` was added or changed: the refine tight lines (U6, U119) quote its
existing rows. These remain for the card, and belong in `docs/verified.md` once run:

- the layouts against real images and real timings: the strip and its reserved height over
  a long run, the live preview, and the SDXL pass's stand-in (U12, U26, U67)
- the job flow's words against a real worker:
  - Pause at a real cell boundary
  - Cancel during a real first load, and the elapsed ticker
  - the budget's ETA and the preview's budget line against real step times
  - a failure stop from a real exception (U27, U41, U74, U75, U120, U121)
- the gated-load card against the real Hub error on a machine without access (U56)
- the sizes the Refine dialog now opens at, on each profiled checkpoint. Measured tight

  generic line (U6, U119).
- the critic's first-use line and clock against the real llama-server download (U131)

## Leftovers — 2026-10-05, second pass

The follow-up above left five things open:

- U61 part B;
- the parts of 19 fixed findings left undone by decision;
- the remainders of six documented alternatives;
- two issues outside the findings;
- a list of checks for the card.

A check of this record against `e63f004` (main, with the follow-up merged) listed 87
items: those parts and remainders, the plan's steps and grafts that had not been taken,
the outside issues, the GPU list, and a critic's corrections of the record.

The work on them is on the branch `review-2026-10-04-leftovers`, cut from `e63f004`, in
the 179 commits from `7212b32` to `bde3bc2`. It went in five rounds:

- *Wave 1*: eight worktree packages (jobs, lightbox, prompt, refine, sheet, gallery,
  stage, tags). Each was implemented, reviewed, fixed and rechecked, then merged at
  `f342854`-`91b4caa`. All 34 items were done.
- *A review of seven areas this review never reached*, run against `e63f004` alongside
  wave 1. It kept 85 findings; its record is `docs/review-2026-10-05-areas.md`.
- *Wave 2*: ten packages. Nine took those 85 findings, and one (`w2-integ`) the 52 minor
  points wave 1's rechecks left. They were merged at `2bedf55`-`aef7022`, with four fixes
  after the merges.
- *A final pass*: an integration review of the merged branch, whose 18 findings were all
  fixed in `936ba28` with five leftovers of wave 2. The docs followed (`c4515b9`,
  `f9de976`, `a3ab988`, `564f94d`), and a checker went over them.
- *The card*: the GPU list, run on the RTX 5080, and what it found (`d814fc8` to
  `bde3bc2`).

The findings below and the follow-up above are unchanged. `review-2026-10-04-ui-findings.json`
gains a `leftovers` block beside `remediation`.

**Corrections to this record.** The check found four errors in the follow-up:

- **smoke-delete's flake did recur, and it was the app's.** The follow-up said the smoke
  had passed every run since and that the cause was not found. In six fresh runs, one
  failed on the two server-count checks after the lightbox-of-one delete.
  - The cause: the lightbox's `close` event is queued, not fired by `close()` itself, and
    its listener reset whatever lightbox was open when it came. The smoke closes a
    lightbox and at once opens a lightbox of one to delete from. The late event shut the
    reopened lightbox under the delete in flight, so the next count was read before the
    delete answered.
  - Fixed in `24b7abe`: the listener resets only a lightbox that is still closed.
  - In the smoke, the fixed waits became conditions (`d95589e`). Its viewer-tile delete
    waits for the picture to load (`6ffe73b`), since on Windows a PNG still being
    streamed cannot be removed.
- **U72's sheet note was part of the published recommendation** (its item 5). It was not
  the verifier's separate note, out of scope. Built in `6dfa8f3`: a sheet of prompt
  versions whose images each have their own seed says so, and points to Check's Compare on
  the same seeds.
- **U43 had one path left.** With the Tags helper or the Examples menu left open by the
  keyboard, Escape in the lightbox went to the menu's branch. The dialog's own cancel then
  closed the lightbox without `dismissLightbox`, so focus went to the image the lightbox
  had opened on, not the one the walk ended on. Fixed in `5c05195`: the lightbox's Escape
  comes first.
- **The profiles with no measured refine line were more than Animagine, Kohaku and
  Illustrious.** FLUX.1 schnell, Z-Image Turbo, Qwen-Image and the off-catalogue Z-Image
  also had a pass and only the generic 1600x1600 line. For schnell and Z-Image Turbo that
  line was looser than the 2.36 MP their siblings had measured. All seven were measured
  and given lines in `d814fc8` (the card, below).

storage access in try/catch:

- **Gallery beside** (`657b712`, `045c421`; `local-diffusion.gallery-beside`). A tick in

  - S or M: eleven or eight cards a row at QHD;
  - Fit or Crop. Fit letterboxes the whole picture, as before. Crop fills the card: the

**The two outside issues.**

- **The watchNext race, and the queued-submit race found beside it, are fixed** (`7212b32`,
  `48831f0`). The check had reproduced the first in 8 of 60 back-to-back submissions.
  - watchNext and resumeActiveJob share one listing (`adoptListedJob`). It checks `busy`
    again when it answers, and is `jobListing` while it is out, so a submission made
    meanwhile queues behind it.
  - A queued POST waits for a listing still out, then takes the page if nothing is
    watched. It queues only an id that is neither watched nor queued already.
  - Each stream is kept by job id (`jobStreams`), so an `await startJob(...)` waits for
    its own job.
  - smoke-jobs holds each ordering open with gated fetches; all four failed before the
    fix. smoke-grid's 300 ms spacing is gone, and it counts one stream per render.
- **smoke-delete's flake is fixed**; see the corrections.

**The card.** The GPU list above was run on the RTX 5080 on 2026-10-05, and
`docs/verified.md` has the rows. The page checks ran the real server (`--defaults`, fp8,
model offload) on a throwaway gallery, with headless Edge at 2560x1305 pressed by real
mouse events. Their rows are under Behaviour, dated 2026-10-05 (`fd4f009`, `7ff6eee`):

- **The results strip and live preview** (Anima Turbo v1.1): one image, a batch of four
  and a 28-seed grid.
  - Through the grid, the gallery head held still under `reserveStrip`'s 559 px.
  - A batch's lone preview sat centred, and each image moved left as the next landed.
    Fixed in `9377de1`.
  - The batch refitted, moving the head 3 px, as the page's scrollbar went at the job's
    end. Fixed in `7733f81`. A stable gutter on the page, tried first (`2338697`),
    narrowed every vw size and was reverted (`3e143b7`).
- **The SDXL pass's stand-in** (WAI v17) held from the first progress event to the
  result, in the result's own box. Its ETA ran long early, since the first step carries
  the VAE encode and the move to the card. The estimate now runs from the first step's
  end (`2a4d2cc`).
- **Pause's words over real cells** (FLUX.1 Krea [dev]). "Pausing…" came 20 ms after the
  press, and the pause at the cell boundary 36.8 s later. Resume and the withdrawal
  behaved as designed.
- **Cancel during a real first load, and its ticker**: a tick a second, and the job
  cancelled 40 ms after the press while the load went on to ready.
- **A time budget against real step times** (Kohaku XL Zeta). The preview said 4 of 6
  seeds, and the grid made 5. `budget_seeds` now counts by the rule that stops the job
  (`6a7570d`).
- **A failure stop from a real exception** (WAI v17, a sweep whose source was deleted
  mid-run) read "Stopped at cell 5 of 10 after 3 cells failed". Its Resume was refused at
  every press with the server's bare sentence; it is now held, with the reason in words
  (`ad01fdc`).
- **The failed-load card against the real Hub**, with no token and nothing cached, for
  the gated FLUX.1 [dev] and Krea 2 Raw and a made-up id. Each gave its own steps and
  link.
- **The critic's first use over a real download.** The line followed each download, and
  the clock reached 199 s for a 199.7 s first use. Two fixes followed: the clock had
  wrapped at QHD (`b396e2e`), and sizes computed in 1024s had been labelled GB
  (`5ec8adb`).

The refine lines were measured one size per process (`d814fc8`). Every catalogued
checkpoint with a pass now has one, in the Refine pass's "Past 4 MP" and Nunchaku tables
and the Checkpoints rows dated 2026-10-05:

- Animagine, Kohaku and Illustrious run the guard's own 2896x2896 at about 10.3 GB.
- schnell stops at 1536x1536, within a gigabyte of the card's top.
- Z-Image Turbo stops at 2048x2048 (12.5 GB), and the undistilled Z-Image at 1536x1536.
- Under Nunchaku, Qwen-Image stops at 1280x1280, which is its line. FLUX.1 dev ran at

The pictures were not reviewed.

The card found three defects, each fixed the same day:

- **A load started while the page's first adapter listing ran failed**, 3 times out of 3.
  The adapter fit's meta-device build patches `Module.register_parameter` for the whole
  process. A load and a build now never overlap (`02cd1d9`). Rechecked on the card: 3 of
  3 loaded.
- **A pass that landed after its source was deleted during its own cell was shown as
  live.** Fixed in `2b89ed5`, checked on the fake.
- **With `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` the load card said to connect to the network.** Fixed in
  `81781bf`, checked against the library's own exception chain.

The rules, notes and README describe the fixes (`cb92838`, `bde3bc2`).

**The seven-area review.** This review's completeness critic had listed areas no finder
reached as gaps it would not brief, and the check found a broken rule in one of them
(Expand's `.value` write). So a second review covered seven: ERNIE's Expand and
translation, NewBie's XML prompts, Z-Anime and NetaYume, Anima, the page under 901 px,
two tabs on one server, and what a screen reader is given. It ran against `e63f004` the
same way, seven finders on fake servers at 2560x1305, 2048x1040@1.25 and 1400x900 with
each finding reproduced by a skeptical verifier. It kept 90 of 92 findings (one refuted,
one a duplicate of U15) and merged them into 85: 19 medium and 66 low. All 85 were fixed
in wave 2 and the final pass, one of them only in part, by decision, and an integration
review of the merged branch found 18 more, all fixed in `936ba28`.
`docs/review-2026-10-05-areas.md` records it, and
`docs/review-2026-10-05-areas-findings.json` is its machine-readable version.

**Validation**, on the final tree (`bde3bc2` plus this record):

- `uv run --group dev pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider` gave **2,114 passed, 4 skipped** and
  31 dependency deprecation warnings in 98.93 s. `main` (`e63f004`) had 1,929 passed.
- **All 34 browser smokes passed** on the final code: 2,482 expectations, none failing.
  - Each ran on a fresh fake server with its own throwaway gallery. The session's runner
    (`run_smokes.py`, outside the repository) started them.
  - Two are new with this work. `smoke-stage`, on 7888, holds Gallery beside at
    2560x1305, 2048x1040@1.25 and 1900x1100. `smoke-narrow`, on 7889 at `--step 0.3`,
    holds one column and narrow windows from 600 to 900 px, with checks at 1400x900 and
    2560x1305.
  - The other 32 are the follow-up's.

---

## 1. Verdict

The page's structure is sound and much of its detail is careful. What makes it feel
clunky at QHD is that it was laid out and tested at 1400x900, where every smoke runs,
and then stretched. In order of impact:

**Panels push the image and the controls off the screen.** On the target screen the
page is one long document, and the panels stacked in it keep displacing what the user
is looking at.

- Opening the prompt check stacks a 669-1136 px panel above the viewer. The image just
  made drops to between 59% and 7% visible, and at 125% it leaves the screen entirely
  (U5).
- With Grid search open, the sticky settings panel is taller than the viewport. Run
  grid, Resume and the grid preview can then be reached only by scrolling to the end
  of the gallery, which keeps paging in more images (U1). The media queries meant to
  prevent this have never applied: they sit above the rule they were meant to override.
- A finished comparison, run or Refine sweep opens its contact sheet below the fold,
  and nothing scrolls to it (U2).
- The viewer collapses and regrows twice per Generate (U26).
- A finished job resets the gallery to the top (U25), and Reuse scrolls to the top for
  no reason (U44).

**Some actions overwrite work or render something else, and say nothing.**

- Examples, Reuse and every tag tool assign `.value`. That wipes the prompt's undo
  history, and in Chromium a later Ctrl+Z can splice two prompts together, which is
  then saved (U4).
- Ctrl+Enter in the grid fold renders the literal `{placeholders}` (U37). A comma in a
  prompt variable silently collapses the grid to one prompt (U8).
- On the SDXL tag checkpoints the chip tray shows tags past CLIP's 77-token cut as
  green (U7). The same happens to a tag added in the Refine dialog (U65).
- The Refine dialog opens FLUX sources at sizes where the default strength repaints
  the subject (U6).
- Delete unstarred deletes the source of a starred refine pass (U10).
- After a server crash the page says "the run carries on" (U9).

**The screen is wasted while the controls are small.**

- The panel is 330 px wide (12.9% of 2560). Of the page's text, 79.5% is 12 px or
  smaller, and the prompt's own buttons are 10 px text on 19 px targets (U14, U15).
- Control borders measure 1.24:1 (U24), and the LoRA select is a white native control
  (U21).
- A single result shows at 887 px with about 1,270 px empty beside it, and its actions
  exist only on a gallery card (U28, U12).
- Gallery cards are 171 px, each with six unlabelled 12 px glyph tools (U13, U96).
- The Refine dialog is 460 px wide and shows the image being refined as a 72 px
  thumbnail (U11).

**Refine is a dead end at the moment the user wants to judge it.** The pass replaces
its source in the viewer. The lightbox shows a one-image list with the lineage as a
bare id, and stepping between pass and source resets the zoom (U3). Section 3 covers
Refine in full.

**Feedback lands in the wrong place, or does not come.**

- Gallery and card notices land in the panel's error slot, about 2,400 px from the
  click (U42).
- Run status is a 113x5 px bar with 11-12 px text (U19).
- Queued jobs are unnamed (U39).
- A stale "Last run" banner sits under each new result (U17).
- Pause gives no acknowledgement (U41).
- Every failure state the gap finders reached says too little, in the wrong place, or
  something untrue: a gated first load, a dead server, failed cells, a critic error
  (U56, U57, U73, U9, U74, U75, U140).

**Controls are unlabelled, hover-only and inconsistent.**

- Card tools are glyphs named only by a tooltip (U13).
- ↻ means two different things, and Favourite is spelled four ways (U104).
- Checkboxes show no keyboard focus (U54).
- Help tooltips run to 130 words (U103), and all twenty `?` badges are named "Help"
  (U98).

**What works, and must be kept.** These are why the recommended direction keeps the
page's bones.

- **The server's previews.** The grid preview, the Refine preview ("about 3 of 28 steps
  run") and the comparison's cost line all show what will happen before anything runs.
  Refusals come back in the server's words.
- **Help and greyed controls.** The help is each checkpoint's own guidance, sourced from
  its authors. Controls that do not apply are greyed with a reason rather than hidden,
  so the panel keeps its shape across checkpoints.
- **The lightbox.** It zooms about the pointer, uses explicit layout, has a
  DPR-exact 1:1, carries the view across same-size cells and has a full keyboard model.
- **Jobs.** Queueing never blocks, a reload reattaches to a running job, and the cancel
  and finish summaries are specific.
- **The prompt check.** Every finding names and links its source, the stale state is
  clear, and Use and Apply write through `replaceBoxText`, so Ctrl+Z works there.
- **Safe defaults.** The Refine dialog leaves the panel untouched, and Delete
  unstarred's typed-count gate has a server-side `expected` pin.
- **Accessibility groundwork.** Submit buttons use `aria-disabled`, live regions are
  deliberate, and muted text was chosen for contrast (5.5:1 and 7.4:1).

---

## 2. Recommended direction

The judge recommends **"Same bones, real scale"**, with grafts from the other two
directions.

Its thesis: the structure is right; what is wrong at QHD is that the page was sized
for about 1400 px and stretched. It adds a token system (type, spacing, control
height, colour and layout widths) with a wide tier from 1800 px and a short-window
tier, so that 2560x1305 and 2048x1040 each get real scale. It spends the spare width
where the user works:

- a 460 px panel;
- the prompt check docked beside the viewer instead of above it;
- the just-made image with its prompt, facts and actions beside it;
- Refine as a two-pane dialog that shows the source large, with the kept region framed.

It is mostly CSS. Every rule is scoped to `min-width: 1800px`, so the 1400x900 smokes
render as today apart from two headings and one fold.

### Scores

Higher is better in every column. For implementation risk, higher means safer.

| Direction | Resolves verified findings | QHD use of space | Learnability | Refine experience | Implementation risk | Respects documented rules | **Total** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Same bones, real scale | 6 | 7 | 9 | 7 | 9 | 9 | **47** |
| Studio workbench | 8 | 9 | 7 | 8 | 5 | 6 | **43** |
| Image-first loop | 9 | 8 | 5 | 9 | 3 | 5 | **39** |

The judge's reasoning, condensed:

- **Same bones** fixes what the user names (everything is small, space is wasted, the
  check pushes the image down) at the lowest cost. In the prototype the panel is
  461 px with 15.5 px body text, Generate sits at y≈993, the gallery shows 8 cards of
  241 px with the first row above the fold, and the docked check leaves the gallery
  head at y≈921 instead of 1604. It keeps every documented rule: the modal Refine,
  `placeTagValidation`'s hosts, the card tools, and click-opens-lightbox. Alone it
  leaves U3 (comparing a pass with its source), U2 and U6 open, and it caps the
  just-made image at 757 px instead of 887.
- **Studio workbench** uses the screen best. The page never scrolls, a render shows at
  868x1116 with 20 library cards beside it, the sheet opens in the canvas, and a pass
  lands beside its source. The cost is a no-scroll shell: every `?` tip needs a JS lift
  to escape `overflow: auto`, and the library's IntersectionObserver has to be
  re-rooted. A card click changes meaning (select, then zoom), and a non-modal Refine
  that re-points to whichever card is clicked edges back toward the "mode" the
  2026-10-04 design note retired. At 2048x1040 the output row is already hidden
  behind the pinned Generate.
- **Image-first loop** has the best Refine flow: the source at exactly 1024x1024,
  profile-derived strength chips, a "Passes of image N" list, and swipe or
  side-by-side compare on one shared zoom. But it is keyboard-first (F, R, E, C, A, S,
  B, `[`), which is hard on an occasional user. It removes the card tools and reverses
  several documented rules. At 125% its presets wrap to three rows and its caption
  truncates. Its estimate is 9-11 days, about twice the base.

### Grafts

| From | Idea | Resolves |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Image-first loop | Swipe and side-by-side compare of a pass against its source, both layers on one zoom and pan, in the existing lightbox (`layoutLightbox` already does explicit geometry and divides by `devicePixelRatio`). Toggled by "Compare with source (S)" when `rec.refine` is set. The source is cover-cropped by `refine.fitted`'s own arithmetic. | U3, U49 (part) |
| Image-first loop | Strength chips under Strength, built from `refineDefaultStrength()`. On FLUX: "0.05 lighter · 0.1 default · 0.2 stronger · sweep 0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2". They replace the 0.4 placeholder that repaints FLUX. | U20 |
| Image-first loop | "Passes of image N (k)" thumbnails in the Refine dialog, newest first. A click opens the compare. | U49 (part) |
| Image-first loop | Ctrl+Enter routed by focus: inside `#refine-dialog` it runs the pass, inside `#grid-section` it clicks Run grid, anywhere else it runs Generate. | U37 |
| Image-first loop | Progress in `document.title`, and a hover caption "id · W×H · seed" on gallery cards. | U19 (part), U79 |
| Studio workbench | A facts grid (model and quantization, size, seed, steps, CFG, true CFG, Mu, LoRA, time) with labelled "Use seed" and "Reuse settings". Used in the viewer's result column and in the lightbox, rendered by one shared helper. | U45, U30, U28 (part) |
| Studio workbench | A lineage chip on a pass ("⤢ Refine pass of image 5 at strength 0.1") linking to the source, a "⤢ 0.1" badge on refined cards, and a just-landed pass shown beside its source. | U22, U126 (part), U3 (part) |
| Studio workbench | Batches of 2-4 laid out as an equal 2x2: 552 px cells at QHD, against 444 px today. | U47 |
| Studio workbench | The crop caption in measured terms ("keeps the middle 100% × 67%"), and Strength focused with its value selected when the dialog opens. | U69, U115 |

### Plan

Steps 1-4 fix high findings that cause lost work or wrong output, are small, and need
no redesign. Step 5 lays a pixel-identical token baseline and a wide-screen smoke, so
that each later step can be checked. Steps 6-14 can each ship alone, highest value
first. The total is about 13-16 developer-days, with no backend change except step 4.
Findings the plan does not cite are independent fixes; Section 4 gives each one its
recommendation.

| Step | What | Resolves | Effort |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1. Unpin the panel correctly | Move `style.css:65-73`'s two `@media` blocks below the `.panel` rule. In `app.js`, a ResizeObserver sets the sticky top to the gutter while the panel fits, and to `innerHeight − height − gutter` while it does not. | U1 | S (2 h) |
| 2. No silent overwrites; Ctrl+Enter goes where focus is | Route every programmatic whole-box write through `replaceBoxText`: the Examples pick, `fillPanel`'s prompt and negative, `writePromptTags`, the recommended negative. Ctrl+Z then restores. Route Ctrl+Enter by focus (graft). | U4, U37 | S (0.5-1 d) |
| 3. Say what will actually render | `previewGrid` lists the resolved prompt even when the variables collapse to one. The Refine dialog opens at 1.5x or the remembered scale only while the size stays within `sizeIsTight`. The chip tray marks chips past the encoder cut while the check is fresh. | U8, U6, U7 | S (1 d) |
| 4. Protect sources; tell the truth when the server dies | `keep_favorites` also keeps refine sources and references of starred rows (`matching_preview`, `delete_matching`, plus a test). On stream give-up, probe `GET /api/jobs/<id>` instead of saying "reload to pick it up". One "server unreachable" state for the topbar and per-action messages. Drop the stale preview. | U10, U9, U73, U135, U136 | S-M (1 d, one backend test) |
| 5. Token baseline and a wide smoke | Tokens on `:root` at today's values, literals replaced by `var()`, pixel-identical. VIEWPORT/DPR env vars in `tools/browser.mjs`. `tools/smoke-wide.json` at 2560x1305 and 2048x1040@1.25 asserts the panel width, body font size, gallery column count and that Generate is on screen. | U92 | S (0.5-1 d) |
| 6. Wide tier: scale, contrast, focus | At `min-width: 1800px`: type and height tokens (body 15.5, labels 13.5 at 2560); the panel at `clamp(400px, 18vw, 480px)`; control borders `#62627a`; primary fill `#6a4cf0`; placeholder in `--muted`; red hover only on destructive buttons; disabled inputs dimmed; a `max-height: 1150px` density tier. `color-scheme: dark` ships together with explicit `:focus-visible` rings. | U14, U15, U21, U24, U54, U89, U91, U94 | M (1-1.5 d) |
| 7. Stop pushing the image down; bring the sheet into view | The idle viewer collapses to one line. The prompt check docks beside the viewer through a `:has()` grid at `clamp(520px, 27vw, 700px)`, with `placeTagValidation` unchanged. `showSheetScope` scrolls the sheet into view when it resolves. The finish line gains Show sheet. | U23, U5, U80, U18, U84, U2, U31 | M (1-1.5 d) |
| 8. Cards that say what they are | `minmax(var(--thumb-min), 1fr)` gives 8 x 241 px at 2560 and 7 x 214 px at 2048. `object-fit: contain`. A word label per tool on hover and focus. ⤢ and ❐ `aria-disabled` where unsupported. A "⤢ 0.1" badge on passes, a hover caption, and a wider search box. | U13, U82, U29, U96, U101, U22, U79, U90 | M (1 d) |

Batches of 2-4 as an equal 2x2. The live preview drawn in a box of the result's aspect, so the viewer stops collapsing. | U28, U45, U47, U26, U12, U126 | M (1-1.5 d) |
| 10. Refine as a two-pane modal | Keep `showModal()` and the dialog's contract. Left pane: the full PNG with the kept-region frame and "keeps the middle 100% × 67%". Right pane: segmented scale buttons showing output sizes, Strength focused and selected, the strength chips, a "Passes of image N" strip, and the outcome as a 13.5 px summary. The dialog is top-anchored and closes only once the POST is accepted. | U11, U69, U110, U97, U115, U20, U83, U49 | M-L (1.5-2 d) |
| 11. Compare a pass with its source | In the lightbox, when `rec.refine` is set: "Compare with source (S)" with Swipe and Side by side on one zoom, through `layoutLightbox`'s explicit geometry. `zoomLightbox`'s floor becomes `min(1, oneToOneScale)`. | U3, U40, U49 | M (1.5 d) |
| 12. Feedback where the eye is | Progress in `document.title`. A `#gallery-error` slot under the gallery head. The last-run banner hidden on `setBusy(true)`. Pause acknowledges at once. The budget-stop count and wording fixed. | U19, U42, U17, U41, U120 | S-M (1 d) |
| 13. Panel structure | Sampling and Output headings. LoRA and references in a Conditioning fold driven by `syncConditioning`. The dead negative box collapsed to one line. Neutral presets, with an underline marking the recommended ones. | U95, U100, U81 | S (0.5-1 d) |
| 14. Lightbox, sheet and tips at the wide tier | A 380 px lightbox column with the facts grid. Sheet headers at 13.5 px and sticky. `.tip { pointer-events: none }`. Tips 340/400 px at 13.5 px. Navigation circles 52 px. | U30, U32, U103, U108, U139, U48 | S-M (1 d) |

### Risks the judge and the designer named for the recommended direction

- **Focus rings.** `color-scheme: dark` turns Chromium's `outline: auto` ring
  near-black on this background. The first prototype iteration showed Check's focus
  as invisible, so explicit `:focus-visible` rings must ship in the same commit.
- **The docked check.** It is a second scroll container, as tall as the viewer (791 px
  at QHD), so a long critic answer scrolls inside it. It relies on `:has()`; without
  it the check stays above the viewer, as today.
- **Panel height.** The bigger scale makes the panel taller: 1189 of 1201 px at QHD
  with the last-run banner. It depends on step 1's measured sticky top.
- **Prompt box sizing.** `field-sizing: content` is Chromium-only, and the prompt box
  grows while typing.
- **Letterboxed cards.** `object-fit: contain` is a visible change of taste.
- **Refine source size.** The dialog loads the full PNG, which takes noticeable decode
  time on a 4 MP source.
- **The Conditioning fold.** It hides the LoRA select from someone who uses adapters
  every time. It auto-opens while a LoRA is selected, and its summary names the
  adapter.
