## 3. The Refine experience

The user singled out Refine. Two finders reviewed it on FLUX.1 Krea [dev]: one as a
workflow, one as a dialog. A gap finder then repeated it on Animagine, an SDXL tag
checkpoint. **32 of the 141 kept findings touch Refine**, four of them high. They are
listed here in the order a user meets them; full entries are in Section 4.

The short version: the dialog is a clear improvement on the old panel mode, and it
does most things right. It leaves the panel untouched, it shows the server's preview
of the cost, it refuses bad input in place, and a sweep opens as a sheet. The clunky
feeling comes from three places:

- the dialog is a narrow form about a picture it barely shows;
- its defaults can repaint the image it promises to keep;
- once the pass lands, the page gives no way to judge it against its source.

### Getting into Refine

- **U13** — On a gallery card, the entry is a 23x24 ⤢ glyph. It appears on hover only,
  is named only by a native title, and reads as "full screen".
- **U101** — On a checkpoint with no refine pass (klein), the card's ⤢ looks live. Its
  refusal lands in the panel, while the lightbox's Refine is correctly dimmed.
- **U28** — The just-made image has no Refine action where it is shown. Reaching one
  takes a click into the lightbox, or a hover over the newest card.
- **U43** — Refine opened from the lightbox closes the lightbox. Cancel then drops
  focus at the first card, and the lightbox position and zoom are lost.
- **U125**, **U126** — "Reuse settings" on a pass opens the Refine dialog, not the
  panel. The dialog is titled for the source, and the reused pass is named only in the
  closed fold.

### The dialog

- **U11** — The dialog is 460 px wide at 2560 (18% of the width). It shows the source
  as a 72x72 cover-cropped thumbnail, which for a 16:9 source is the middle 56%. The
  dimmed stage behind it shows whatever the strip last held, often an unrelated image.
- **U6** *(high)* — It opens at 1.5x or at the remembered scale. A 1.5 MP FLUX source
  therefore opens at 3.5 MP, and after one 2x pass the next opens at 6.2 MP. At those
  sizes the default 0.1 starts at sigma 0.65-0.92, where the measured runs repainted
  the face. The only warning is an 11 px amber "may run tight on 16GB VRAM".
- **U20** — Strength guidance contradicts the checkpoint. The placeholder suggests 0.4,
  and the tip's sweep examples (0.3-0.5) repaint on FLUX. The sweep syntax lives only
  in a 499-character tooltip, and errors arrive in pydantic's wording.
- **U69** — A reshaped size crops away part of the source, and the only sign is the
  muted text "cropped to this shape". W and H are not locked together, so typing one
  side reframes the picture.
- **U97** — The scale buttons (31-46 px, 11 px) and the outcome summary (11 px muted)
  are the least prominent things in the dialog.
- **U110** — The dialog re-centres on every height change. Opening the fold moves its
  own summary 136 px.
- **U115** — It opens focused on Refine, its last control, so the first Tab leaves the
  dialog.
- **U103**, **U139** — The Strength tip covers the controls it explains. The Size tip,
  once clicked, sits over 1.25x/1.5x/2x and swallows clicks: one swallowed click turned
  an intended 2.3 MP sweep into a 4 MP one.
- **U127** — A refused press repeats the preview's sentence in red.
- **U111** — There is no 🎲 on Seed, and Strength ignores the arrow keys.
- **U99** — Escape, Cancel or ✕ throws away a typed sweep, size and prompt edits.
- **U22** — "Refine image 9" and "refine of 14" name ids the page never shows anywhere.
- **U119** — On SDXL the only size warning is FLUX's VRAM line. It fires at 2x, though
  the same pipeline was measured at 8.6 MP (WAI v17).

### On an SDXL tag checkpoint

- **U65** — A tag added at the end of the dialog's prompt lands past CLIP's 77-token
  cut and does nothing. The dialog never says so: in the measured case, 22 of 42 tags
  are read.
- **U66** — The prompt is a plain 3-row box showing 14 of 41 tags. It has none of the
  panel's chips, autocomplete or validation, so a typo goes straight into the pass.
- **U67** — Real SDXL sends no preview frames. During the whole pass the stage shows an
  unrelated earlier image, and the only sign of work is a 12 px counter about 1,000 px
  away.

### After the pass

- **U3** *(high)* — The pass replaces its source in the viewer, and the lightbox opens
  it as a one-image list with the lineage as plain text. Stepping between pass and
  source resets the zoom, because the lightbox view is keyed on exact width × height.
  No route shows both images at the same crop.
- **U83** — Feedback moves to the panel. A queued pass is "1 more job queued", unnamed,
  and a refusal at submit loses the dialog and its edits.
- **U2** *(high)* — A strength sweep's sheet opens below the fold, under a strip that
  repeats its images.
- **U49** — The sweep's sheet has no source cell to compare against, and square cells
  run off the screen.
- **U16** — Sheet cells are sized from the stage width only (697 px for a 3x2 sweep), so
  the matrix never fits one screen.
- **U109** — Turning the sheet off leaves the gallery filtered to the job.
- **U40** — On QHD the 1:1 zoom is unreachable for a 1 MP source, which is where a
  sharpening pass is judged.
- **U79** — A refined card looks exactly like its source.
- **U10** *(high)* — Delete unstarred deletes the source of a starred pass, after which
  the pass cannot be made again.

### What each direction makes of it

**Same bones, real scale** keeps Refine a modal and makes it a two-pane working
surface.

- The dialog is 1400x820, anchored at the top so it grows downward.
- The left pane shows the full PNG at up to 818x818, with a dashed frame on the kept
  region labelled "kept: 56% of the source" whenever the shape crops.
- The right pane holds the existing controls at panel width: a 4-cell scale control
  showing each factor's output size, the server's outcome in a 13.5 px summary box,
  and a 46 px Refine button with a Ctrl+Enter chip.
- The viewer's result column adds "Refine…" beside the just-made image (verified: it
  opens "Refine image 20" on the new image).

The dialog's contract is untouched. On its own it leaves U6, U20, U99, U83, U115 and
the compare (U3) to other fixes. The plan grafts those in (steps 3, 10 and 11).

**Studio workbench** turns Refine into the inspector's panel, non-modal.

- The canvas shows the source at 1:1, with the crop frame and the caption "keeps the
  middle 100% × 67%".
- Strength takes focus. When the pass lands, source and pass appear side by side at
  the same displayed size, with a lineage chip.
- From 1100 to 1799 px it is a 420 px side sheet.

It solves the "can't see the picture" and "can't compare" problems well. The
non-modal design is its risk: Ctrl+Enter and Escape must be routed by focus, and a
panel that re-points to whichever card is clicked edges back toward the mode the
design notes retired.

**Image-first loop** turns Refine into a non-modal drawer over the gallery shelf.

- The source stays at 1:1 in the stage with the kept-region frame.
- Strength chips come from the profile's default (0.05 / 0.1 / 0.2 and a sweep on
  FLUX), and the drawer lists "Passes of image N".
- After Refine the drawer reopens on the same source with what was typed. The result
  lands in a swipe compare against its source on one shared zoom, and holding B flips
  between them.

It covers more Refine findings than either other direction (U3, U20, U49, U99, U115).
But it is keyboard-heavy, and it reverses the dialog's documented modality.

**The judge's combination** keeps the base's modal two-pane dialog and adds:

- Loop's compare, placed in the lightbox, whose explicit-geometry layout already exists
  (plan step 11);
- Loop's strength chips and passes list, and Studio's measured crop caption and
  Strength focus (step 10);
- the opening-size cap (step 3) and the source protection (step 4).

Three things are left out of the plan: the dialog-side prompt check for SDXL (U65,
U66), the SDXL placeholder (U67), and the draft kept on close (U99). Each has its own
recommendation in Section 4 and fits the plan without conflict.

---

## 4. Findings

Each entry has the same parts:

- a header line giving severity, frequency, effort, the reviewing areas and the code
  pointers;
- **Observed**: what was measured, with screenshot paths relative to the scratchpad base;
- **Why it matters**;
- **Recommendation**: the verifier's final one;
- **Rule**: the documented rule involved and how the fix keeps it, where there is one;
- **Smokes**: the smoke steps the fix touches;
- a closing line with the workflow's M or G id, its sources, and the verdict.

### HIGH

#### U1. The sticky settings panel runs past the viewport, so Run grid, Resume and Budget cannot be reached; the media queries meant to unpin it never apply

**high** · every-session (at 125% always; at 100% whenever Grid search is open) · effort S · visual, panel, grid, walkthrough · `web/style.css:65-92`, `web/app.js:169-173`, `web/index.html:303-411`

**Observed.** `style.css` puts `@media (max-width: 900px)` and `@media (max-height: 900px) { .panel { position: static } }` *above* the equally specific `.panel { position: sticky; top: 68px }`. The later rule always wins: at 1400x900 `matchMedia('(max-height: 900px)')` is true and the panel is still sticky. The rule arrived that way in commit 496bfb1.

- **2560x1305, grid fold opened by a real click.** The panel is 1489 px tall. Run grid sits at y=1505-1548, below the 1305 px viewport, and stays there while the panel is pinned (y=1497 at scrollY 1000). It comes into view only at the end of the document, after the gallery has auto-loaded every page (cards 96 → 144 → 160).
- **2048x1040@1.25, grid closed.** The panel is 1089 px tall. The last-run box is cut off and the Grid search summary stays at y=1112 until the end of the page.
- **1400x900.** Generate (top at y=907-915) is never on screen until the end of the document.

Screenshots: `verify-panel-1/m1-q-03-open.png`, `verify-panel-1/m1-q-04-open-scrolled.png`, `find-visual/shots/s1400-08-scrolled-more.png`, `find-panel/shots/e02-125pct-scrolled.png`.

**Why it matters.** Run grid, Resume, Budget and the grid preview are the grid's only controls. With a real gallery the user has to scroll through every image to reach them. The comment at `style.css:69-72` describes exactly this failure; the fix written for it never took effect.

**Recommendation.**
1. Move the two `@media` blocks below the `.panel` rule (after l.92), so that `max-width: 900px` really unpins the one-column layout.
2. Replace the 900 px height guess with a measured sticky-bottom top in `app.js`. A ResizeObserver plus a resize listener sets `panel.style.top = Math.min(68, innerHeight - panel.offsetHeight - 18) + 'px'`. A panel that fits stays pinned at 68. A taller one scrolls until its bottom meets the viewport, then holds there. Simulated at QHD with the grid open: top −184 px, and Run grid at y=1227-1270 at every scroll position (`verify-panel-1/m1-simA-1000.png`).
3. When the fold opens, call `els.gridRun.scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest'})`, with smooth scrolling unless reduced motion is set.
4. Do not make the panel `overflow-y: auto`. That clips the Examples menu (whose right edge is x=421 against the panel's 348), the Animagine tag menu and the right-hand tips.
5. Add a smoke step at 1400x900 with the grid open: after a wheel over the stage, `els.gridRun.getBoundingClientRect().bottom <= innerHeight`.

The known cost: with the grid open, the top 184 px of the panel (the prompt) sits above the fold while the user scrolls the gallery.

**Rule.** The fix carries out `style.css:70-73` ("sticky only where the whole panel fits") with a measurement instead of a guess. `style.css:85-91` holds: the panel stays positioned at z-index 10, so tips still paint over cards. The finder's claim that the smokes run with a static panel at 1400x900 is false; the panel is sticky there today.

**Smokes.** No smoke asserts the panel's position. Re-run `smoke-animagine.json:59-60` and `:94-96`, which compare Generate's top before and after chips or a check. The 1400x900 smokes will now run with a sticky-bottom panel, but they drive it through eval.

*M1 · from visual-1, panel-1, grid-1, walkthrough-8 · adjusted: the overflow remedy was rejected, and walkthrough-8's 311 px focus scroll did not reproduce.*

#### U2. A comparison's, run's or Refine sweep's contact sheet opens below the fold, and nothing scrolls to it

**high** · every-session · effort S · walkthrough, analysis, grid · `web/panel.js:241-249`, `web/sheet.js:546-554`, `web/generation.js:478-479`, `web/app.js:220-225`, `web/style.css:650-661`

**Observed.** FLUX fake at 2560x1305, a comparison of 3 versions x 4 seeds, at scrollY 0 when it finishes:

- The sheet's top is at y=1650, in a 1305 px viewport.
- Above it sit the prompt check (2161x890 at y=76) and the viewer (554 px tall at y=984). The viewer holds the 12 comparison images unlabelled in 8 columns, so one row mixes versions. No sheet cell is in view.

The other ways into a sheet:

- **Runs picker.** The sheet opens at y=742 under the 554 px strip, with none of its 12 cells fully visible.
- **Fresh page.** The 380 px placeholder sits above the sheet; 4 of 12 cells are visible.
- **Refine strength sweep.** Its preview says "Opens as a sheet when it is done". The sheet opens at y=568 under its own 6-image strip, with 697 px cells and row 1 cut off.
- **125%.** One row is visible.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-1/m4-qhd-02-compare-done.png`, `verify-grid-1/m4-qhd-05-runs-picker.png`, `verify-grid-1/m4-refine-sweep-done.png`.

**Why it matters.** The comparison and the sweep exist to produce their sheet. When they succeed, the only visible change is the cell note and an unlabelled strip that looks like the result.

**Recommendation.** In `showSheetScope`, keep `setSheet(true)`'s promise and read `galleryEpoch`. When the promise resolves, if sheet mode is still on and the epoch is unchanged, call `document.querySelector('.gallery-head').scrollIntoView({block: 'start'})`, with `.gallery-head { scroll-margin-top: 72px }`.

Scroll to the gallery head, not to `.sheet`, so the search box, Favorites and Sheet stay in view. This one change covers the Runs picker, the card ⊞, the comparison (`generation.js:479`) and the Refine sweep (`refine.js:373`). Also route the last-run banner's job branch (`app.js:224`) through `showJob`.

Leave the Sheet toggle alone. Do not hide the viewer (a running job lands there) and do not fold the check panel. Pair this with U16's height cap.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: the comparison "opens its submission (job:<id>) as the sheet when done - Favorites unticked first", and the prompt check sits above the results. Scrolling keeps both. `scrollIntoView` does not move focus, so the focus rules are unaffected. It must run after the promise resolves, because `setSheet` waits for `learnSheetOrder` and `renderSheet`.

**Smokes.** None asserts a scroll position. Re-run `smoke-comparison.json`, `smoke-analysis.json` 134-135 and 315-319, `smoke-refine.json` 79-82 and `smoke.json` 62-102. `smoke-paging.json` 26-33 is unaffected as long as the Sheet toggle does not scroll.

*M4 · from walkthrough-1, analysis-6, grid-5 · adjusted. analysis-6's per-variant cell cap belongs with U16.*

#### U3. A refine pass can't be compared with its source: the lineage is a bare id, the two aren't adjacent, and stepping between them resets the zoom

**high** · every-session · effort M · gallery, walkthrough, refine-flow · `web/lightbox.js:89-113`, `web/gallery.js:91`, `web/generation.js:391-397`, `web/refine.js:98`

**Observed.** FLUX fake: a 1024² source (id 9), then lightbox Refine, 2x, Refine.

- **In the viewer.** Once the pass lands, `viewerRecords` holds only the pass; the source left the strip when the first image arrived.
- **In the lightbox.** Opening the pass from the strip gives a one-item `lbList`: prev and next are disabled and there is no counter. The lineage is the 12 px text "refine of 9 @ 0.1", not a link.
- **In the gallery.** The refined card's class is plain `card`, with no badge.
- **Stepping between them.** With the pass at 1:1 (scale 1.614), ArrowRight to the source gives "fit · 124%" at scale 1, because `lbView` is keyed on exact width × height.

Source and pass sit next to each other in the gallery only if the source was the newest image.

A prototype that swaps `lbImg.src` to the source's URL and leaves `lbView` alone kept the scale and pan, and showed the source at the same crop.

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/a02-after-pass.png`, `a03-lb-pass.png`, `a04-pass-zoomed.png`, `a05-after-arrow.png`, `d02-source-same-crop.png` (all in `verify-gallery-1/shots/`).

**Why it matters.** The only question a pass asks is whether it improved the source, judged on fine detail. No route shows both at the same crop. This is the flow the user called clunky.

**Recommendation.**
1. **A compare toggle in the lightbox.** In `renderLightbox`, when `rec.refine != null`, show "Compare with source (S)" in `.lb-side`, which has about 700 px free at QHD. Bind `s` in `app.js`'s lightbox key block. On press:
   - fetch `/api/images/${rec.refine}` once and cache it by id. If the answer is "unknown image", disable the toggle with "source #N deleted", as `reusePass` does.
   - set `els.lbImg.src` to the source's URL with `object-fit: cover`. That reproduces `refine.fitted`'s cover-and-centre-crop.
   - leave `lbList`, `lbIndex` and `lbView` alone.
   - label the zoom line "source #9 · 1024×1024". Any arrow or delete returns to the pass.
2. **Make the settings clause a button** that does the same, keeping its text "refine of 9 @ 0.1".
3. **Mark refined cards.** In `card()`, add class `refined` and a "⤢ W×H" badge drawn with `::before`.
4. **Optional: carry the zoom across shapes.** Carry `lbView` across aspect ratios within 1% and clamp to the new image's maximum scale. Use a tolerance, not a `toFixed` key: both proposed keys break on 16-px snapping (2048x1152 against 1360x768). This needs a design-notes amendment.

Do not put the source into `viewerRecords`.

**Rule.** design-notes "The lightbox zooms": the view is keyed on width × height and carried across same-size records. The toggle needs no keying change; carrying across aspects widens the rule. CLAUDE.md: a refine source is a gallery id, fetched by `GET /api/images/{id}`.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json:55` expects `lbMeta` to include "refine of <id> @ 0.1"; keep that wording. Add toggle steps after it.

*M5 · from gallery-1, walkthrough-7, refine-flow-2 · adjusted: adjacency is conditional, and the aspect keys proposed by the finders fail. The plan's step 11 grafts Image-first loop's swipe and side-by-side modes onto this toggle.*

#### U4. Examples, Reuse and the tag tools assign `.value`, so a half-written prompt is overwritten with no Ctrl+Z

**high** · common · effort M · panel, tags, walkthrough · `web/app.js:41-51`, `web/gallery.js:341-345`, `web/tags.js:52-65`, `545-554`, `677-683`, `web/analysis.js:310-320`

**Observed.** A driver sending real key codes; as a control, typed text followed by Ctrl+Z undoes normally.

- **Examples (FLUX).** A typed prompt was replaced by Examples item 1. Ctrl+Z and `execCommand('undo')` left the example in place. The saved panel state already held it, and no note appeared.
- **Reuse.** The card's ↻ is a 23x24 glyph beside ⤢. Over a typed prompt it replaced the prompt and also changed seed ''→7, steps 28→20, CFG 3.5→7 and size 1024→512, with no note.
- **Undo after Reuse.** Two Ctrl+Z presses produced "a lighthouse on a cliff at duskMoody night photography, …". That is a splice of the reused text with an earlier example, which `persistPanel` then saved, and it survived a reload.
- **Animagine tag tools.** Chip ×, the "safe" rating button, Examples, "Use the recommended negative prompt" and autocomplete accept: none can be undone. The rating button followed by Ctrl+Z also produced a splice.

Screenshots: `verify-panel-1/m2-01-examples-open.png`, `verify-panel-1/m2-02-after-reuse.png`, `find-tags/shots/t3-06-examples.png`.

**Why it matters.** A long prompt is the user's main work in the panel, and FLUX's guide asks for 30-80 words. One misclick in a menu that opens over the prompt box destroys it. In Chromium the stale undo stack can also corrupt the prompt, and the corruption is then persisted.

**Recommendation.**
- **Route every whole-box write through `replaceBoxText`** (`analysis.js:315`): the Examples pick, `fillPanel`'s prompt and negative (`gallery.js:342-344`), `writePromptTags` (`tags.js:545`) and the recommended negative (`tags.js:677-683`).
- **Autocomplete accept.** In `applyTagSuggestion`, select [start, end) and call `execCommand('insertText', false, text)`, then `clearTimeout(tagSuggestionTimer); hideTagSuggestions()` as `usePromptText` does.
- **Chip focus.** Where a chip started the write, refocus that chip with `preventScroll`.
- **Reuse.** Snapshot the panel (the object `persistPanel` builds) before `fillPanel`. Then show "Settings from image N loaded - your prompt was replaced." with an inline Undo that restores seed, size, steps/CFG/mu and the owned flags.
- **Examples.** Show the same note with Undo when an Example replaces different non-empty text.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: Use and Apply write through `replaceBoxText` "so Ctrl+Z restores"; all chip mutations go through `writePromptTags`; programmatic writes call `promptReplaced()`.
- Fix it inside `writePromptTags` and keep calling `promptReplaced()`.
- `replaceBoxText` already falls back to `.value` for a greyed negative box.
- Reuse's checkpoint-mismatch note stays, with the Undo appended.

**Smokes.** Values are unchanged and the checks use `includes()`, but re-run:
- `smoke.json:25-27`
- `smoke-flux.json:20`, `37-52`
- `smoke-animagine.json:19-46` (focus expectations at :36 and :38) and `86-90`
- `smoke-illustrious.json:18`, `33-35`
- `smoke-wai.json:20`, `32-34`
- `smoke-kohaku.json:24-26`
- the Examples steps in `smoke-anima`/`netayume`/`newbie`/`z-anime.json:10-12`
- `smoke-conditioning.json:79`, `110`
- `smoke-refine.json:121`

*M2 · from panel-2, tags-2, walkthrough-3 · confirmed, and worse than reported: stale undo entries splice two prompts.*

#### U5. The prompt check and chip tray stack above the viewer and push the image just made below the fold, while the space beside the image stays empty

**high** · common · effort M · analysis, walkthrough, tags · `web/style.css:648`, `web/index.html:64-71`, `414-416`, `web/tags.js:522-538`

**Observed.**

| Case | Panel | Image top | Image visible |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FLUX, 2560x1305, before Check | none | 91 | 887 of 887 px (all) |
| after Check | 2161x669 at y=76 | 778 | 527 of 887 px (59%) |
| after Ask the critic | 1136 px tall | 1245 | 61 px |
| 2048x1040@1.25, after Check | | | 280 of 707 px |
| 2048x1040@1.25, after the critic answers | | | 0 px |
| Animagine, 18 tags, after the critic | | | 0 px |
| Animagine, 70 tags, after Check | | | 130 px |

A Ctrl+Enter pressed with the panel open lands its image at y=1245. Beside the 887 px image, about 1,274 px of width goes unused. The panel closes only through its Close button; Escape does not close it.

Screenshots: `verify-analysis-1/shots/q-02-check.png`, `q-05-critic.png`, `snone-02-critic.png`, `t-01-18tags-check.png` (all in `verify-analysis-1/shots/`).

**Why it matters.** The loop is check, fix, Generate, look. While the panel is open, every result lands off-screen.

**Recommendation.** From `min-width: 1800px`, put the open check in a sticky side column. analysis-1's CSS as written puts the panel *below* the viewer's row on checkpoints without chips (Ask the critic ends up at y=1413), so use the verified version instead. Let S be `.stage:has(> #tag-validation-wide > #prompt-analysis:not([hidden]))`:

- S is a grid with columns `minmax(0,1fr) clamp(520px,30vw,760px)` and rows `auto auto 1fr`.
- Every child of S spans `1/-1`, except:
  - `#tag-validation`: column 1, row 1;
  - `#prompt-analysis`: column 2, rows 1 to span 3, `position: sticky; top: 68px; max-height: calc(100vh - 86px); overflow-y: auto`;
  - the viewer: row 2; the viewer note: row 3;
  - the gallery head, gallery, sheet and Load more: rows 4-7.

The explicit rows and the trailing `1fr` row are load-bearing. Without them the gallery auto-places into the empty chip row, or the viewer is pushed down to y=343.

Result: the image stays at y=109 and fully visible after Check and after the critic, at both viewports. The panel scrolls inside itself, and focus lands on its h2 at y=87. The CSS is saved as `verify-analysis-1/variant-C.css`. Below 1800 px keep the stacking, optionally capped at `max-height: 55vh` with its own scroll.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: "On a two-column page validation lives above the results … The prompt check's panel follows it" (via `placeTagValidation`). The point of that rule is to keep both out of the sticky settings column, so they cannot push Generate down. The CSS-only side column keeps the DOM order and `placeTagValidation`, and Generate stays at y=915 (FLUX) and y=897 (Animagine). Reword "above the results" to "beside the results from 1800 px" in CLAUDE.md, the design notes and the `index.html` comment.

The cost: a `.many` batch's 8 slots shrink from 258 to 159 px while the panel is open. `:not(:has(> #viewer.many))` can exclude that case.

**Smokes.** None fail; 1400x900 is below the query. Re-run `smoke-animagine.json` step 93 and `smoke-analysis`'s heading-focus steps. Covering the new layout needs a QHD smoke (plan step 5).

*M3 · from analysis-1, walkthrough-9, tags-8 · adjusted: the literal CSS misplaces the panel, and the tags-8 and walkthrough-9 variants overlap row 1. The same side column largely resolves U80 and U18 at the target width.*

#### U6. The Refine dialog opens at a size beyond what the checkpoint holds, and on FLUX the default strength then repaints the subject

**high** · common · effort M · refine-dialog, refine-flow · `web/refine.js:63-69`, `117-119`, `189-197`, `337-344`, `web/sizes.js:123`, `web/state.js:225-226`, `backend/main.py:1161`, `backend/refine.py:132`

**Observed.** FLUX fake, empty preferences.

- **A 1.5 MP source.** A 1664x928 source (the 16:9 1.5 MP preset) opens at 2496x1392, which is 3.5 MP. The only warning is the 11 px amber "may run tight on 16GB VRAM". The preview still reads "about 3 of 28 steps run". Focus is on Refine, so ⤢ followed by Enter runs it.
- **A 4 MP source.** A 2048² source opens at 2560², which is 6.6 MP.
- **After one 2x pass.** The preferences store scale 2, so the next sources open at 4.1 MP (fox) and 6.2 MP (harbour).

The verifier wrote a script for FLUX.1 dev's dynamic shift and checked it first: it reproduces `verified.md`'s measured 0.474, 0.62, 0.752 and 0.463 exactly. With it:
- at the harbour's default size, strength 0.1 starts at sigma 0.653;
- at the remembered 2x, 0.1 starts at sigma 0.918;
- on a 4 MP source, 0.1 starts at 0.935.

`verified.md` records:
- the face "began to change" at 0.62;
- 0.1 at 2x (sigma 0.752) "repaints the man";
- FLUX fills the card at 2.4 MP and spills at 4.2 MP.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-1/a02-harbour-dialog-el.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/a09-4mp.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/a12-fox-remembered-2x.png`. The script is `verify-refine-dialog-1/sigma.py`.

**Why it matters.** The fastest path through the dialog (open, Enter) on the default checkpoint spills VRAM for minutes and redraws the subject the user wanted to keep. That is wrong output, after time wasted.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Opening size.** Apply the remembered scale, or 1.5x, only while `refineScaled(...)` stays at or below `sizeIsTight` (2.56 MP). Otherwise open at the largest `REFINE_SCALES` factor that does, else 1x. When the remembered factor is skipped, title its button "(last run at 2×)", so it stays one click away.
2. **Size-dependent strength.** For a single strength with a pipeline loaded, `/api/grid/preview` returns `start_sigma`: the live scheduler's sigmas at `start_index`, after `set_timesteps` with this size's mu. Before a load, or for an unprofiled pipeline, it returns null. `previewRefine` appends "· starts at 65% noise", in the warn style when that is more than 0.1 above the same strength at 1.5x of 1 MP. Before a load, show a FLUX-profile note under Strength when the size exceeds 2.56 MP, worded from `verified.md`: at 2x, 0.1 started at sigma 0.75 and repainted the subject; Mu 1.15 at strength 0.2 kept it.

Keep the 8.4 MP guard as the hard limit.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md and the design notes say the dialog opens at "1.5x the source's size or the remembered scale, else the largest factor that fits". Reword that to "…that fits and is not past the tight line". The fix keeps "a typo guard, not a VRAM model": `refine_max_side`/`refine_max_pixels` and `tests/test_profiles.py` are untouched, and the cap reuses the page's existing 2.56 MP line. A server-side sigma must be read from `scheduler.sigmas`, never computed by a client formula.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json` step 52 expects a 1344x1728 refined source to open at 2016x2592 ("5.2 MP"). Under the cap it opens at 1x, so that step needs updating. Steps 18, 28 and 97 are unaffected. Step 24 uses `startsWith`.

*M9 · from refine-dialog-2, refine-flow-1 · confirmed, and stronger than reported. Cost of the fix: SDXL and WAI, which ran 8.6 MP comfortably, also open capped. One click on 1.5x or 2x still goes there (see U119).*

#### U7. The chip tray shows every tag green and never marks the 77-token CLIP cut

**high** · common · effort M · tags · `web/tags.js:349-466`, `486-505`, `web/style.css:258`, `web/analysis.js:357-411`, `backend/main.py:612-622`, `834-848`

**Observed.** Animagine fake at 2560x1305, with a realistic 39-tag prompt: identity tags first, the card's quality suffix last.

- **The tray** is 2176x112 at y=76 and shows 39 green ✓ chips, with no marker of any kind.
- **Check** reports CLIP-L and CLIP-G at 110 of 75 tokens, with the cut at code point 308, inside chip 26. So 13 chips (33%) are never read, including all four of the guide's suffix tags: masterpiece, high score, great score, absurdres.
- **The only token figures** on the page are in Check's Encoders column, 286 px wide at 12 px.

The finder's own 70-tag prompt lost 47 of 70. The fake tokenizer counts one token per word or mark; real CLIP BPE is usually denser on Danbooru names, so real cuts come at least as early.

Screenshots: `verify-tags-1/a02-long-tray.png`, `verify-tags-1/a03-long-check.png`, `find-tags/shots/t6-04-70tags-check.png`.

**Why it matters.** On the SDXL tag checkpoints (Animagine, Kohaku, Illustrious, WAI), the cut is the most important fact about a long tag list. The always-visible view puts green ticks on tags the model never sees, and Check is the only place that says so.

**Recommendation.** Two steps.

1. **S, no backend.** While `analysisResult` is fresh, take the earliest prompt encoder's `cut`, convert it with `units()`, and find the comma item that contains it. Give that chip and every later one `.past-cut` (opacity .55, dashed border, title "not read: CLIP-L/CLIP-G stop at 75 tokens"). Mark the chip the cut splits "partly read".
2. **M.** Add `POST /api/prompt/measure`, which returns only `prompt_encoding.measure(...)` (the encoders and `encoders_detail`) on a `_detached` thread, with no rules and no tag lookup.
   - Call it from `validatePromptTags` after a 600 ms debounce, with its own epoch.
   - A tokenizer still loading renders "cut unknown", never "no cut".
   - Show the count through the `::before` label: set `data-tokens="110/75"` and use `content: "Prompt tags · Danbooru " attr(data-tokens)`, amber when over.
   - Draw any divider in CSS (for example a box-shadow on the first `.past-cut`), not as an element.

Calling `/api/prompt/analyze` from the tray would be wrong: it runs every rule plus up to 64 Danbooru lookups on every typing pause.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md, Prompt analyser: Check works cold on CPU tokenizers, never through `ensure_loaded` or the gate; `measure` never raises; offsets are code points. No rule makes the cut Check-only. A first measure may fetch tokenizer files before anyone presses Check. That is allowed (the "never from `/api/status`" rule is about status), but it should be noted.

**Smokes.** CSS-only markers break nothing. A real divider element would break the steps that iterate `els.tagValidation.children`: `smoke-animagine` 10 and 14, `smoke-illustrious` 13, 21 and 24, `smoke-kohaku` 12 and 17, and `smoke-wai` 17. Add steps for `.past-cut` and for "cut unknown".

*M13 · from tags-1 · adjusted.*

#### U8. The grid preview hides the resolved prompt when the variables collapse to one, so "size = big, small" silently renders "a big, small fox"

**high** · occasional · effort S · grid · `web/generation.js:278-286`, `331-344`, `web/index.html:319`, `326`, `backend/grid.py:210-225`

**Observed.** The setup:
- prompt: "a {size} fox sitting in the snow";
- variables: "size = big, small", with commas, as in every other grid box;
- Steps 6-12x2, CFG 2,3.5,5.

The preview reads "12 images · 4 steps x 3 CFG x 1 mu x 1 seed · about 1s · ~62.7 KB / steps 6, 8, 10, 12 · CFG 2, 3.5, 5 · mu none". It shows no prompt line and no warning colour. `/api/grid/preview` returns `values.prompt = ['a big, small fox sitting in the snow']`.

With "big | small" the preview reads "2 prompts" and lists both. `promptLines` returns nothing for fewer than two prompts.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-1/m12-comma.png`, `verify-grid-1/m12-pipe.png`.

**Why it matters.** The preview is the one check on prompt substitution, which its own comment calls "the half of a prompt grid that goes wrong quietly". It switches off exactly when the variable failed to vary, and a whole night's comparison then runs on one prompt.

**Recommendation.**
- **Show the prompt.** In `previewGrid`, list the resolved prompt whenever the variables box has text, even when there is only one, prefixed "prompt: ". Change `promptLines`' guard to `if (!prompts || !prompts.length) return []`.
- **Warn on single-valued variables.** A client-side pass over the variable lines adds the warn class to any line matching `/^\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*=(.*)$/` with no "|" in its value, plus a message:
  - if the value contains a comma: "size has one value 'big, small' - values split on |, not commas";
  - otherwise: "size has one value: it never varies".

Keep the `|` split.

**Rule.** design-notes "The prompt is an axis: variables": values split on `|` because a comma belongs to the prompt. The warning is advisory and client-side; the server's single parser is unchanged.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json` 151-154 uses `includes()`; re-run it. `smoke-conditioning.json` 66 is unaffected.

*M12 · from grid-3 · confirmed.*

#### U9. "Lost the generation stream. The run carries on — reload to pick it up." is false when the server died

**high** · occasional · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `web/generation.js:486-500`, `507-525`, `backend/main.py:1226-1264`, `web/panel.js:168-194`

**Observed.** A scratch supervisor ran around the fake (`--step 0.3`), with a Runs-4 job and a hard `os._exit` at T+12.3 s.

**Long outage, restart after 30 s.**
- From 12.8 to 24.6 s the panel showed "reconnecting… (1)" through "(5)". The bar stayed frozen at 34.8%, still promising "~23s left".
- At 27.7 s the red message appeared while the server was still down.

**Fast restart, after 1 s.**
- The reconnect hit the new process's 404 for the old job id.
- The same message appeared 3.5 s after the kill, beside a topbar that flipped to "not loaded" a second later.

**In both cases.**
- `/api/jobs?include_finished=true` returned `[]` after the restart.
- Doing what the message says (reloading) showed no banner and an empty viewer.
- The Runs picker lists the Runs-4 job as "1 img".
- Before the reload, the job's image was in the viewer but not in the gallery.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/blong-02-mid.png`, `bfast-01-after-gen.png`, `blong-05-reload.png`.

**Why it matters.** The message tells the user to wait for, or reload into, a job that no longer exists. On an overnight grid it hides that most of the night is missing.

**Recommendation.** In `source.onerror`'s give-up branch, probe `GET /api/jobs/<id>` (the route exists, `main.py:1226-1231`). Let N be the images finished so far (`shown.size`) and M the last `total_runs` times the batch size.

- **Network error.** Say "Can't reach the server — it may have stopped. The N image(s) finished so far are saved. Start it again; the page will notice." U73's offline state then takes over.
- **404.** Say "The server restarted, and this job ended with it after N of M images. They are in the gallery; Generate again for the rest (a grid: tick Resume)."
- In both of those cases, call `resetGallery()` and `loadRuns()`.
- **200.** Call `streamJob(jobId)` again: `events()` answers `done` for a finished job.

The finder's idea of skipping the retries when the stream is closed is moot, because the branch already tests `readyState === CONNECTING`.

**Rule.** design-notes "A job outlives the page; per-stream queues": keep the five auto-reconnects and the `/api/jobs` reattachment. The probe runs only after the stream gives up.

**Smokes.** None break a stream.

*G1.4 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-4 · adjusted. U136 (drop the stale preview) is a one-line companion fix.*

#### U10. Delete unstarred deletes the source of a starred refine pass, and nothing before the click says the pass depends on it

**high** · occasional · effort M · destructive-actions · `backend/storage.py:737`, `781`, `backend/main.py:1413`, `backend/clear.py:103`, `159`, `web/gallery.js:224-228`, `278-327`, `web/lightbox.js:112`, `web/refine.js:110-113`

**Observed.** FLUX fake with 76 images, including source 63 (1024², seed 400) and pass 78 (1536², a refine of 63 at strength 0.1). The pass was starred, then Delete unstarred ran with an empty search.

- **Before.** The dialog read "Delete 72 unstarred images in the gallery (FLUX.1-Krea-dev only), keeping 4 starred? … Type 72 to continue:", with no word about the source.
- **After.** "Deleted 72 images, keeping 4 starred." `GET /api/images/63` returns 404.
- **The kept pass, afterwards:**
  - the lightbox still reads "refine of 63 @ 0.1";
  - the Refine dialog says "itself a refine of image 63";
  - Reuse fills the panel at 1024x1024 with "Image 63, the source this was refined from, is no longer in the gallery, so the pass cannot be made again."
- **The per-card confirm** for image 63 does not warn either, although the search `refine:63` returns [78].

Screenshots: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v1-pass-lightbox.png`, `v1-pass-refine.png`, `v1-pass-reuse.png`.

**Why it matters.** A star means "keep this". The source is part of the pass's reproduction set, and its PNG is deleted from disk, with no undo, by a gesture whose dialog never mentions it. The user finds out at a later Reuse.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Keep the sources in storage.** Add one predicate under `keep_favorites`, shared by `matching_preview` and `delete_matching`: `id NOT IN (SELECT refine FROM images WHERE favorite = 1 AND refine IS NOT NULL)`, plus the `json_each(reference)` equivalent for references.
   - `matching_preview` returns `sources_kept`.
   - `main.py:1413` becomes `unstarred = records − favorites − sources_kept`. Without that, every sweep answers 409.
   - `clear.py:103/159` use the same field.
   - The dialog reads "…keeping 4 starred and 1 image a starred pass was refined from (63)?".
2. **Warn per card.** `deleteImage` asks `/api/gallery?limit=1&all_models=true&search=refine:<id>`. If anything matches, the confirm adds "Image 78 was refined from this one; once it is gone that pass cannot be made again."
3. **Mark missing sources.** `list_images` LEFT JOINs the source and returns `refine_missing`. Render "refine of 63 (deleted) @ 0.1" and "itself a refine of image 63, since deleted".

A warn-only variant (count and name the sources in the dialog, without keeping them) is the alternative if keeping them automatically feels too implicit.

**Rule.** design-notes "Delete unstarred": the scope is the search box plus `favorite = 0`, and the gate is typing the count back, with `expected` compared under the write lock. The predicate must be shared and the dialog's count derived from it, or the gate breaks. Sources stay ids.

**Smokes.** None break as long as the wording keeps its substrings: `smoke.json:174-181`, and `smoke-refine.json:55`, `:60` and `:138`.

*G2.1 · from gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end-1 · adjusted: the original omitted the count formula and the CLI.*

### MEDIUM

#### U11. At QHD the Refine dialog is 460 px wide and shows the image being refined as a 72 px cover-cropped thumbnail

**medium** · every-session · effort S · refine-dialog, refine-flow, visual · `web/style.css:967-986`, `web/index.html:546-547`, `web/refine.js:98`, `109`

**Observed.**
- **Size.** The dialog is [1050,372,460,561] at 2560x1305, 18% of the width. At 125% it is the same 460 CSS px.
- **The thumbnail.** It is 72x72, with natural size 384x214 and `object-fit: cover`, so a 16:9 source shows only its middle 56%.
- **The page behind it.** The stage shows whatever the strip last held: refining image 3 showed image 6 behind the dialog.
- **The lightbox.** Opening Refine from it closes the 1270 px view the user was looking at.
- **Taller states.** With the fold open the dialog is 460x833. For another checkpoint's image it is 851 px, and the fold summary wraps to two lines.

A runtime prototype gave a 1400x1138 dialog, with the source at 918x1100 at natural size beside a 420 px controls column. It needs no scrolling, even with the fold open.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-1/a01-harbour-dialog.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/a05-harbour-crop.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/b-02-proto1.png`.

**Why it matters.** Strength and scale are judgements about this picture, made while looking at a 72 px swatch. It wastes about 2,000 px of width in the feature the user named.

**Recommendation.** At `min-width: 1600px`:

```css
.refine-dialog { width: min(1400px, calc(100vw - 64px)); max-height: calc(100vh - 64px) }
.refine-dialog[open] { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr) 420px; column-gap: 24px; align-content: start }
.refine-dialog > * { grid-column: 2 }
.rf-source { display: contents }
.rf-source img { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 1 / span 12; width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: min(calc(100vh - 140px), 1100px); object-fit: contain; align-self: start }
.rf-source-text { grid-column: 2 }
```

- Use `height: auto`: a fixed height letterboxes a 16:9 source.
- In `openRefine`, set `src = rec.url` when the query matches (else `thumb_url`), and alt "Image N, the source".
- Below 1600 px keep one column, but give the thumbnail `object-fit: contain`.
- The crop overlay is U69.

**Rule.** The modal contract is unchanged: `showModal`, focus kept inside, Escape closes, Refine autofocused, DOM and tab order as now. The dimmed backdrop exists because "the page behind is context". The source in the dialog serves that purpose better than the stage behind it.

**Smokes.** None; the refine smokes run at 1400x900. Step 20's source line is unchanged.

*M6 · from refine-dialog-1, refine-flow-4, visual-7 · adjusted (high → medium: nothing blocks or misleads).*

#### U12. The viewer stacked above the gallery pushes the gallery down to one row, or below the fold

**medium** · every-session · effort M · visual, gallery, results · `web/style.css:654`, `664`, `686`, `web/panel.js:257-278`

**Observed.** 144 images at 2560x1305.

| Viewer holds | Viewer height | Gallery heading at | Cards fully visible |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| nothing (empty state) | 380 px | y=509 | 48 |
| one 1024² image (887x887) | 917 px | y=1046 | 12 |
| 28 images (4 rows of 258 px tiles) | 1090 px | y=1219 | 0 |
| 48 images (6 rows) | 1625 px | y=1756 | — |

A 4x2 run shows seeds 300-307 oldest-first in the viewer, then again newest-first as the gallery's first row. At 125%, one image leaves no card fully visible. A prototype of auto-fill columns brought 28 images down to 554 px, with 36 cards visible.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m14-02-one.png`, `verify-results-1/m14-04-many28.png`, `verify-results-1/m14-06-many60.png`, `verify-results-1/m14p-qhd-many28.png`.

**Why it matters.** The gallery is the user's other working surface. At QHD, the tier meant to keep it close pushes it a screen down.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Cap the many tier.** At `min-width: 2000px`: `.viewer.many { grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(160px, 1fr)); max-height: min(560px, 45vh); overflow-y: auto; align-content: start }`, with the images at width 100%. If the strip was scrolled to its end before an append, keep it there. Set `#viewer-note` to "N this run, scroll the strip or open one to walk them all".
2. **Shrink the empty state** (see U23).
3. **Link the strip to the gallery.** Outline the gallery cards whose ids are in `viewerRecords` (`.card.this-run`), cleared in `clearResults`. Do not reverse the strip's order.
4. **Optional, L.** A two-column stage at 2000 px and wider.

Drop the finder's proposal to shrink the single image to 55vh.

**Rule.** design-notes "The running batch on wide screens" asks for 8 columns at 2000 px and wider, so QHD fits more than 7 per row. Auto-fill gives 12 per row at 100% and 9 at 125%, which keeps the purpose; update the note's wording. VIEWER_MAX is unchanged.

**Smokes.** None; every smoke runs at 1400x900.

*M14 · from visual-4, gallery-6, results-5 · adjusted.*

#### U13. Card tools are six glyph-only 23-29 px buttons; Refine is a "⤢" that reads as "expand"

**medium** · every-session · effort S · copy-a11y, visual, refine-flow · `web/gallery.js:100-152`, `web/style.css:783-807`, `web/lightbox.js:120-125`

**Observed.**
- **Sizes.** The hover tools are ↻ 23x24, ⤢ 23x24, ❐ 25x24, ⊞ 26x24, ☆ 24x24 and ⌦ 29x24, with 12 px glyphs from font fallback.
- **Labels.** They sit at opacity 0 until hover or focus, and are named only by a title and an aria-label.
- **Overflow.** The row is 182 px wide inside a 171 px card, so Delete (x 514-543) is clipped by about 6 px.
- **❐ on FLUX.** FLUX takes no references, yet ❐ is live and writes a refusal into the panel.
- **⤢.** It is the usual full-screen sign, while a click on the image already opens the lightbox. The lightbox labels the same action "⤢ Refine".

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/b01-card-hover.png`, `verify-gallery-1/shots/b02-ref-refusal.png`, `find-visual/shots/p-05b-card-tools.png`.

**Why it matters.** The main way into Refine takes a hover, a wait for a native tooltip, and a guess.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Visible names.** `tool()` sets `b.dataset.tip` (Reuse, Refine…, Reference, Sheet, Star, Delete), shown through `.card .tools button:is(:hover, :focus-visible)::after { content: attr(data-tip); position: absolute; left: 6px; bottom: 100%; … }`. Anchored to `.tools`, the label is never clipped by the card.
2. **Fix the overflow.** Hide ❐ where references are unsupported, with `#gallery.no-references .ref-tool { display: none }` toggled by `syncConditioning`. Or use 190 px cards at 2000 px and wider, which gives 11 columns.
3. **Separate Delete.** `.card .tools .del { margin-left: auto }`.
4. **Refine where there is none.** Make ⤢ `aria-disabled` at opacity .45 where `!refineSupported`; its click still explains why.

Keep the glyphs as the buttons' text: the smokes find them by it.

**Rule.** `style.css:791-797`: the tools are revealed on `:focus-within` and on coarse pointers. The aria-label carries the name. "A ⤢ on a checkpoint without a pass says so and opens nothing": use `aria-disabled`, never `disabled`.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json:20` and `smoke-conditioning.json:28` and `:33` find the buttons by "⤢" and "❐". They are unaffected while the glyphs stay.

*M15 · from copy-a11y-3, visual-12, refine-flow-9 · adjusted. New in verification: the clipped Delete.*

#### U14. The panel is a fixed 330 px at every width, and its 5-row prompt box hides half of a guide-length prompt

**medium** · every-session · effort S · panel, tags, visual · `web/style.css:57-63`, `365`, `web/index.html:59`

**Observed.**
- **Width.** The panel is 330 of 2560 px (12.9%); the stage is 2161 px (1649 at 125%).
- **The prompt box.** `#prompt` is 294x123 at 14/21 px. A 50-word prompt is 75% visible, 71 words 59%, and 82 words 49%. FLUX's guide asks for 30-80 words.
- **Tag prompts.** On Animagine, 70 tags are 27% visible, while the chip tray shows all 70.

A wide-tier simulation (panel `clamp(400px, 18vw, 480px)`, `field-sizing: content` capped at 12 lines) showed an 82-word prompt in full. The panel grew only 6 px at QHD (1095 against 1089), and 48 px at 125%.

Screenshots: `verify-panel-1/m16-tags70.png`, `verify-panel-1/m16-q-panel-71w.png`, `verify-panel-1/m16-q-sim-wide.png`.

**Why it matters.** The prompt is edited every session, in a 5-line box beside 2,000 px of idle stage.

**Recommendation.** `@media (min-width: 2000px) { main { grid-template-columns: clamp(400px, 18vw, 480px) minmax(0, 1fr) } #prompt { field-sizing: content; min-height: calc(5lh + 20px); max-height: calc(12lh + 20px) } .size-row label.sub { flex: 1 } }`. Keep `resize: vertical`.

Ship it after U1's measured sticky top, because at 125% the panel grows. A cheaper interim fix is to remember the textarea's dragged height. The type size is U15.

**Rule.** `style.css:70-73` keeps the panel sticky only where it fits, so cap the growth in lines; the finders' 30-40vh caps would conflict with U1. The 8-column viewer tier still holds; its cells just shrink. The 901 px threshold is untouched.

#### U15. Most visible text is 10-12 px at QHD 100%, and the prompt's Examples/Check/Reset are 10 px text on 19 px targets

**medium** · every-session · effort S · copy-a11y, panel, walkthrough · `web/style.css:26`, `97`, `130`, `135-142`, `150`, `173`, `472`, `549-557`, `57-63`

**Observed.**
- **The page at 2560, fresh, 552 measured characters.** 36 at 10 px, 261 at 11, 142 at 12, 43 at 13 and 49 at 14: 79.5% at 12 px or smaller, and 54% at 10-11 px.
- **The panel's text nodes.** 38 at 11 px, 20 at 10, 9 at 12, 1 at 13 and 3 at 14.
- **Controls.**
  - `#btn-examples` is 57x19, Check 43x19 and Reset 40x19, all at 10 px.
  - The `?` badges are 14x14; the presets are 32x27 at 11 px.
  - The Seed box (107 px) shows about 9.5 of 16 digits.
- **Injecting the type increase alone at the 330 px panel**
  - "4 MP" wrapped to a second row;
  - the True CFG label split from its `?`;
  - the panel grew from 1089 to 1200 px.
- **The same type with the panel at `clamp(360px, 17vw, 440px)`.** Height-neutral (1087 px), no wraps, and the Seed box became 211 px.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v02-panel.png`, `verify-visual-1/shots/v40-panel-fixed.png`, `verify-visual-1/shots/v41-panel-fixed-wide.png`.

**Why it matters.** At about 109 PPI, 10-11 px text costs reading effort on every visit, and that small text is where the app puts its guidance.

**Recommendation.** One block at `min-width: 2200px` (QHD at 100% only):

| Selector | Change |
| --- | --- |
| `main` | columns `clamp(360px, 17vw, 440px) minmax(0, 1fr)` |
| `.sub i`, `.hint`, `.tip`, `.menu li`, `.analysis-note.sub`, `.compare-note` | 12.5px |
| `.field > span`, `.section > summary` | 13px |
| `.ghost.tiny` | 12px, padding 2px 9px (24 px tall) |
| presets | 12.5px, padding 5px 9px |
| `.help` | 17x17 at 11px |
| `.prompt-analysis` | 13px |
| analysis h3 | 12px |
| source, stale and critic-kind badges | 11px |

Leave the body at 14 px. Never ship the type change without the width change.

**Rule.** No rule sets the type scale. Keep `.field em` at `#a2a2b0`. Gate the change at 2200 px rather than 2000: at 125% the OS already renders 11 px as 13.75 device px, and that panel is already taller than its viewport.

**Smokes.** None behind 2200 px. `smoke-animagine.json` step 13's 12 px chip check runs at 1400.

#### U16. Sheet cells are sized from the stage width only, so a multi-row matrix never fits the QHD screen and thumbnails are upscaled up to 2.7x

**medium** · every-session · effort M · grid, gallery, walkthrough · `web/sheet.js:372-383`, `web/style.css:736-762`, `backend/storage.py:41`

**Observed.** The columns are `auto repeat(N, minmax(96px, 1fr))`, with aspect-ratio cells.

| Sheet | Cell size | Grid height | Thumbnail upscale |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 4x3 steps×CFG | 524 px | 1607 px (viewport 1305) | 1.36x (384 px webp) |
| 3x2 variables | 691 px | 1413 px | 1.8x |
| 4x4 comparison | 512 px | 2090 px | |
| Favorites, 2 images | 1040 px | | 2.71x, visibly soft |
| Refine sweep, 3x2 | 697 px | | |
| 4x3 at 125% | 396 px | 1223 px (viewport 1040) | |

Only a 10x6 run comes close to fitting.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-4x3-scrolled.png`, `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-comparison-scrolled.png`, `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-fav-sheet.png`.

**Why it matters.** The sheet exists to compare cells at a glance. At QHD the common grids, with 2-4 values per axis, need scrolling between rows, and the cells are too soft to judge.

**Recommendation.**
- **Fit by height as well as width.** `renderSheet` sets `--sheet-rows` and `--sheet-ar` on `.sheet-grid` and builds `auto repeat(n, minmax(96px, var(--cell-max)))`, with `--cell-max: calc((100vh - 230px) / var(--sheet-rows) * var(--sheet-ar) - 6px); justify-content: center`. With U2's scroll, a 4x3 becomes about 355 px cells and fits on one screen; a 3x2 about 530; a 4x3 at 125% about 265.
- **Sharpness.** Add `srcset="${thumb_url} 384w, ${url} ${width}w"` with `sizes` set to the computed cap, and keep `loading=lazy`. The export stays on thumbnails.
- **Optional.** A Fit/Fill toggle stored under a `local-diffusion.*` key.

walkthrough-12's extra point is U31.

**Rule.** design-notes "The contact sheet": the whole run is asked for in one page, cells keep the commonest aspect with `contain`, and many columns scroll sideways while the page never does. The 96 px floor stays, and the export reads `sheetLayout`.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke.json` 62-102, `smoke-paging.json` 26-33, `smoke-comparison.json`, `smoke-refine.json` 79-82 and `smoke-analysis.json` 134-135.

*M18 · from grid-4, gallery-11, walkthrough-12 · confirmed. Fix together with U46: both live in `renderSheet`'s column template.*

#### U17. The "Last run finished" banner stays up through new runs, describing an older job, and its Show run opens that older run

**medium** · every-session · effort S · walkthrough, results, grid · `web/panel.js:168-200`, `web/generation.js:41-63`, `445`, `517`

**Observed.** Fresh profile, with finished jobs already on the server.

- **During a new Generate** the banner (296x88 at y=1045) stays up, and moves Grid search from y=1105 to 1147.
- **When the job finishes**, "Finished 1 image in 3s." sits directly above "Last run finished at 4 Oct, 04:05 pm: 2 images (2/2 cells)."
- **After a 12-image grid** the banner still holds the previous job, and Show run sets the search to that older run.
- **On reload** the banner announces the grid this page had just watched to its end. Only Show run or ✕ records a job as announced.
- **At 125%** the banner spans y=1003-1073 and is cut by the fold.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m19-02-done-two-statuses.png`, `verify-results-1/m19-01-running-with-banner.png`.

**Why it matters.** Two statements about different runs sit side by side, both phrased as "the last run". A control labelled "Last run" opens an older run, most often right after a grid, which is when the user reaches for it.

**Recommendation.**
- In `setBusy(true)`, set `els.lastRun.hidden = true` without calling `remember()`.
- In `streamJob`'s `done` branch, call `remember(ANNOUNCED_KEY, jobId)`. Do not do this in `onerror`, so that a lost stream is still announced after a reload.
- Leave `announceLastRun` on load for jobs this browser never saw finish.
- Optionally relabel the button "Show that run".

**Rule.** design-notes "The morning after": the banner appears when nothing is live, for a job finished within 24 h, once per job per browser. Hiding it without `remember()` treats it as superseded, not dismissed. A job watched to its end was already announced by its finish line.

**Smokes.** None reference `#last-run`.

*M19 · from walkthrough-4, results-11, grid-15 · confirmed.*

#### U18. Encoders and Order each get one 286 px track of seven, and the reordered prompt is clamped before you Use it

**medium** · every-session · effort S · analysis, tags · `web/style.css:336-339`, `348-352`, `391-392`, `web/analysis.js:467-477`

**Observed.** `.analysis-body` computes to 7 tracks of 286 px at 2560, and 5 of 307 px at 2048@1.25.

- **Animagine, 18 tags.**
  - Order is [687,227,286,218]. The 6-step chain wraps to two lines, and its GUIDE badge to a third.
  - "Partly checked" takes four lines.
  - The "In that order:" quote is 209x52 with a scrollHeight of 104, so half of it is hidden.
- **70 items.** "Partly checked" runs to seven lines, and 86% of the quote is hidden.
- **In U5's side column**, Order is still 356 px wide and still clamped.

The Use reordered button's title holds the whole text, but only as a hover tooltip.

Screenshots: `verify-analysis-1/shots/t-02-order.png`, `t-07-order-70.png`, `iCt-01-check.png`.

**Why it matters.** On a tag checkpoint, the order and its rewrite are what Check is for. Use reordered gets pressed on text the user can only partly see.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Full-width layout,** at `min-width: 1400px`: `.analysis-body { grid-template-columns: minmax(260px, 360px) minmax(0, 1fr) }`. The `.full` sections still span `1/-1`.
2. **In U5's side column:** `grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr)`, so Encoders stacks above Order at about 735 px.
3. **Unclamp the quote:** `.order-reordered .analysis-quote { display: block; -webkit-line-clamp: none; max-height: calc(1.45em * 6); overflow-y: auto }`.

Keep `auto-fit` below 1400 px.

**Rule.** `style.css:334-335` puts Encoders and Order side by side where there is room. `style.css:348` and `analysis.js:467` say the reordering is "read before it is used"; a scrolling box serves that better than a clamp. "Partly checked" and "Not checked" stay visible.

#### U19. Run status is a 113x5 px bar with 11-12 px text in the left column, and the tab title never shows progress

**medium** · every-session · effort M · results, panel · `web/index.html:262-288`, `web/style.css:70-73`, `617-626`, `web/generation.js:41-63`, `403-411`, `web/status.js:210-229`

**Observed.** A 4-run job at 2560x1305:

- the bar is 113x5 at x=35, and the step bar 113x3;
- "run 1/4" is 12 px, and the ETA "2s elapsed · ~10s left" 11 px;
- Pause is 49x26 and Cancel 53x26, while Generate is a 296x43 "Generate (queue)";
- the live preview sits at x=1287-1607;
- `document.title` stays "Local Diffusion": nothing in `web/` writes it.

At QHD the sticky panel keeps the bar on screen while the gallery scrolls. At 2048@1.25 the panel is 1147 px tall and sticky: the ETA sits on the fold at y=1023-1040, and the banner and Grid search are below it.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m24-qhd-01-running.png`, `verify-results-1/m24-125-02-scrolled.png`.

**Why it matters.** "How far, how long, how do I stop it" is in the smallest type on the screen, furthest from the image. A background tab shows nothing at all.

#### U20. Strength guidance contradicts the checkpoint, and the sweep syntax is hidden

**medium** · every-session · effort S · refine-dialog, refine-flow · `web/index.html:578-582`, `web/refine.js:121-123`, `340-344`, `backend/model_defaults.py:884-899`

**Observed.** FLUX fake.

- **Placeholder.** Strength opens prefilled with "0.1", so its placeholder, "0.4 or 0.3,0.4,0.5", shows only when the box is cleared. At that moment the preview still says "strength 0.1 · about 3 of 28 steps run".
- **The tip.**
  - It is 499 characters of 11 px text in a 300x161 box, and covers Versions, Seed, the seed line, the fold summary and the preview.
  - Its sweep examples, "0.3,0.4,0.5 or 0.2-0.5x0.1", are the same fixed text for every checkpoint.
  - On FLUX at 1.5x, `verified.md` says 0.2-0.6 "repaint the man".
- **Errors.**
  - "abc" gives "strength: 'abc' is not a number".
  - 1.5 gives "strength: Input should be less than or equal to 1".
  - 1 previews "28 of 28 steps run" with no note that nothing of the source is kept.
- **Sweeps.** A typed sweep works well: "3 images · strength 0.05, 0.1, 0.15 / Opens as a sheet when it is done".

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-1/a06-strength-blank.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/f-01-strength-tip.png`.

**Why it matters.** Strength decides whether the pass keeps the picture, and the same number means four times more on SDXL than on FLUX. Copy that suggests SDXL values on FLUX leads to repainted results, and the sweep that would settle the question is hidden.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Placeholder.** `openRefine` sets it from `refineDefaultStrength()`: "0.1 or 0.05,0.1,0.15" on FLUX.
2. **A "Compare 3" button** beside Strength. It writes d/2, d and 1.5d rounded to 0.05, dropping any value that leaves no step: 0.05, 0.1, 0.15 on FLUX; 0.2, 0.4, 0.6 on SDXL; 0.25, 0.5, 0.75 on schnell at 4 steps. Add one visible line: "Several values, comma-separated, run side by side and open as a sheet".
3. **The tip.** `_REFINE_HINTS` formats its sweep example from `refine_strength`. Shorten the tip to two sentences, keeping "starts at {refine_strength:g} on this checkpoint".
4. **Server wording.** "Strength is a fraction between 0 and 1", and name the box in "is not a number".
5. **Description.** Append " · keeps nothing of the source" for a single strength of 0.9 or more.

The plan's step 10 prefers Image-first loop's chip row for item 2.

**Rule.** design-notes: "A strength is an index into the checkpoint's schedule", and refusals are shown "in the server's words". So reword the messages on the server rather than duplicating the check in the client. A sweep is never remembered: the button only fills the box.

**Smokes.**
- `smoke-refine.json` step 22: "starts at 0.1 on this checkpoint".
- `smoke-refine.json` step 84: "strength 0.1 of 4 steps"; keep that server message.
- `smoke-conditioning.json` step 18.
- `tests/test_api_refine.py:23` and `tests/test_refine.py:56-57`.

*M29 · from refine-dialog-3, refine-flow-6 · adjusted.*

#### U21. The LoRA select is an unstyled white native control, and the page has no dark color-scheme for checkboxes and scrollbars

**medium** · every-session · effort S · visual, panel · `web/index.html:245`, `web/style.css:1-13`, `465-468`, `709-712`, `732-735`, `994-997`, `519`

**Observed.**
- **The select.** `#lora` computes to a white background, black text, a 1 px `#767676` border and radius 0, at 226x19 and 13.33 px. It sits beside a 41 px Weight input, and the two labels are 22 px apart in the same row.
- **No color-scheme.** `html`'s `colorScheme` is `normal`, so the checkboxes in the gallery head are white, a wide sheet gets a white horizontal scrollbar, and the page scrollbar is light.
- **The fix verified in two parts.**
  - `color-scheme: dark` on its own darkens the checkboxes and scrollbars, but leaves the select 19 px tall.
  - The injected select rule made `#lora` 226x41 on `rgb(26,26,33)`, aligned the labels, and left the panel's height unchanged.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v03-lora-row.png`, `v21-sheet-scrollbar.png`, `v24-sheet-scrollbar-colorscheme-dark.png`, `v52-panel-line-strong.png`.

**Why it matters.** After Generate, it is the brightest object in the panel on every visit, and it makes the UI read as unfinished.

**Recommendation.**
1. **color-scheme.** `:root { color-scheme: dark; accent-color: var(--accent) }`. Ship it together with explicit `:focus-visible` rings: under `color-scheme: dark`, Chromium's `outline: auto` turns near-black (see U54).
2. **The select.** `#lora, .refine-dialog select { width: 100%; padding: 9px 6px; font: inherit; background: var(--panel-2); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px; color: var(--text) }` plus `#lora:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--accent) }`.

Leave the gallery-head, compare and sheet selects as they are. A global `select` rule would also restyle the compare selects below 901 px.

**Rule.** `.row.disabled select` still dims the row. Ids, values and `autocomplete` are untouched.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U22. Refined images look like any other card, and "refine of 14" or "Refine image 9" name ids the page never shows

**medium** · every-session · effort S · refine-flow, copy-a11y · `web/gallery.js:91-139`, `web/lightbox.js:99-113`, `web/refine.js:108-113`, `142`, `148-150`, `web/sizes.js:216`, `backend/search.py:21-42`

**Observed.** Two 1264x848 renders (ids 3 and 4), then a 0.1 pass of image 4 (id 5, 1904x1280).

- **The cards.** Cards 5 and 4 both have class `card`, inner text consisting only of the six glyphs, and identical alt and aria-label.
- **The lightbox.** Its settings line reads "…, refine of 4 @ 0.1, seed 7, 0.1s" in 12 px muted text. The counter reads "1/3" with no id.
- **The dialog.** Its title read "Refine image 4" while the gallery count read "(2)". Reuse on the pass shows "Refine image 4" in the title and "as image 5 was made" in the fold: two ids that nothing on the page explains.
- **In the code.** Ids are rendered only in `refine.js` and `sizes.js`, and the search grammar has no `id:` key.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-flow-1/shots/a02-gallery-after-pass.png`, `a04-lightbox-pass.png`, `a06-reuse-opens-dialog.png`.

**Why it matters.** A pass is meant to look like its source. Without a mark, starring and deleting "the better one" is guesswork, and the lineage text leads nowhere.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Mark refined cards.** `card()` adds class `refined` and `data-strength`, and draws a top-left chip: `.card.refined::before { content: '⤢ ' attr(data-strength); position: absolute; top: 6px; left: 6px; z-index: 1; padding: 2px 7px; border-radius: 10px; background: rgba(0,0,0,.62); color: #fff; font-size: 12px; pointer-events: none }`. Do the same on sheet cells, unless strength is already one of the sheet's axes.
2. **Show "#id" wherever the page names an image.**
   - Prefix the lightbox line with "#5 · " and add the id to the counter ("1/3 · #5").
   - Render the provenance "refine of #4 @ 0.1" as a link that opens the source with `openLightbox(source, [source, rec])`.
   - Put "#id" in the thumbnail's aria-label.
   - Replace "image N" with "#N" in `refine.js` and `sizes.js`.
3. **Link to the passes.** An "Its passes" link in the lightbox sets the search to `refine:<id>`.
4. **Search by id.** Add `'id': ('id', int)` to `search.FILTERS` and to the search tip.

**Rule.** Ids remain the only way the page names an image. An `id:` filter stays inside the key:value grammar. The badge is a pseudo-element at top-left, so it clears the star and the tools.

**Smokes.**
- `smoke-refine.json:22` ("Refine image " + id), `:55` ("refine of " + id + " @ 0.1"), `:60` ("itself a refine of image " + id), and `:138` if its note changes.
- `smoke-conditioning.json:50` and `smoke-flux.json:45` still pass if "#id · " is a prefix.

#### U23. Every fresh page load opens with an empty 380 px "Generated images appear here" box above the gallery

**medium** · every-session · effort S · visual · `web/index.html:416-418`, `web/style.css:650-661`, `web/panel.js:250-276`, `web/generation.js:380-426`

**Observed.**
- **After a reload with 15 images** the viewer is 2176x380 at y=76, and the gallery head is at y=509: the box takes 29% of the viewport, and 36.5% at 2048@1.25.
- **With 0 images** the page is the same box, a disabled "No runs yet" select and bare background, with no pointer to Examples or Ctrl+Enter.
- **The placeholder** is 14 px muted text.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v10-reload.png`, `v30-reload-2048.png`, `v01-empty.png`.

**Why it matters.** The first thing on screen after every reload is a placeholder, and the user's work starts lower down.

**Recommendation.**
- **Collapse the empty box.** `.viewer:has(> .placeholder) { min-height: 0; padding: 10px 14px; justify-content: flex-start }`.
- **One-line placeholder:** "Nothing generated this session - Ctrl+Enter runs the prompt; click a gallery image to open it."
- **Zero-image install.** `.viewer.empty-install`, set from the first gallery page when `total === 0 && !other_models`, keeps the tall box with a button that opens Examples.
- **What to key on.** Key the state on the placeholder being present, never on `viewerRecords.length`: a preview frame clears the strip while `viewerRecords` is still empty.

**Rule.** The viewer is session-only by design (`gallery.js:247-248`); do not fill it from the gallery.

**Smokes.** None. `smoke-paging` step 15 still passes.

#### U24. Input and button borders measure 1.15-1.24:1, so empty fields and secondary buttons almost disappear

**medium** · every-session · effort S · visual · `web/style.css:1-13`, `103-114`, `135-142`, `601-612`

**Observed.** Measured contrast:

| Pair | Contrast |
| --- | --- |
| `--line #26262f` on `--panel` | 1.24:1 |
| `--line` on `--panel-2` | 1.15:1 |
| field fill against the panel | 1.07:1 |
| muted text | 5.52:1 |
| disabled ghost text (at opacity .45) | 2.08:1 |

The empty Mu box, True CFG's "off" box and Seed's "random" box read as dark patches. With a stronger border injected, they read as fields. The proposed `.7` opacity for disabled controls made the disabled Run comparison look the same as the enabled Ask the critic.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v50-panel-before.png`, `v52-panel-line-strong.png`, `v53-check-line-strong.png`.

**Why it matters.** It misses WCAG 1.4.11 (non-text contrast) on every control in every session, and is behind much of the UI's flat, murky look.

**Recommendation.**
- **A stronger control border.** Add `--line-strong: #666680`: 3.33:1 on `--panel`, 3.11 on `--panel-2` and 3.54 on `--bg`. Use it for `textarea`, `input[type=number|search|text]`, `select`, `.ghost` and `.help`. Keep `--line` for panels, cards and dividers.
- **Disabled controls.** Keep them at opacity .45, optionally with a dashed border.
- **Dropped proposals.** The `#1d1d26` fill (1.11:1, invisible) and the .7 opacity. The placeholder is U89.

**Rule.** The `aria-disabled` submit buttons stay as they are, and `.field em` stays at `#a2a2b0`.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U25. Each finished job resets the gallery to the top and drops the pages already loaded

**medium** · every-session · effort M · results · `web/generation.js:478-480`, `web/gallery.js:7-21`, `23-77`

**Observed.**
- **Today.** With 281 images, scrolled until every page loaded (scrollY 3613, 281 cards), a finished Generate leaves scrollY 0 and 48 cards, and the document shrinks from 4918 to 1904 px.
- **A prepend prototype**, injected in the page only, went from 282 to 283 cards. Scroll anchoring held the same rows (scrollY 3613); the first visible card moved by one slot.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m39-01-deep.png`, `m39-02-after-done.png`, `m39-03-after-done-prepend.png`.

**Why it matters.** Browsing older images during a render is the natural thing to do, and every job end throws that position away, once per job when Runs are set or jobs are queued.

**Recommendation.** Outside sheet mode, replace `resetGallery()` on done (`generation.js:480`) with `prependNew(made)`:

1. Fetch `/api/gallery` under the same search, favorites and all_models, with `limit = min(made + PAGE, sheetPage)`.
2. Unshift the records whose id is greater than `galleryRecords[0].id`, and prepend their `card()`s.
3. Update the total, the count, `otherModels`, `syncRerender` and `syncDeleteUnstarred`.
4. Chain after `galleryPending`, and drop the result if `galleryEpoch` changed.
5. Fall back to `resetGallery()` when the gallery is empty or the fetched page shares no id with the page.

Leave `overflow-anchor` at its default.

**Rule.** design-notes "Paging by id": the paging anchor is the last record, and prepending leaves it alone. Sheet mode keeps its reset or `showJob` path.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke.json:166`, `smoke-refine.json:20`, `smoke-conditioning.json:45` and `:92`, and `smoke-paging.json`.

*M39 · from results-2 · confirmed. See also U44.*

#### U26. The viewer collapses at the first preview frame and then regrows, so the gallery jumps about 540 px twice per Generate

**medium** · every-session · effort M · results · `web/generation.js:380-397`, `414-427`, `web/panel.js:250-278`, `web/style.css:662-679`

**Observed.**
- **After a 1024² result** the viewer is 2176x917 and the gallery head is at y=1046.
- **At the next job's first preview frame** `generation.js:418` calls `clearResults()`. The viewer collapses to 380 px (head at y=509, preview 320x320), then returns to 917 when the image lands: two 537 px shifts per Generate.
- **An 8-run sweep** reflows the tiles from 887 to 444 to 258 px.
- **At 125%** the viewer goes from 737 to 380 px, a 357 px shift.

The comment at `generation.js:380-381` says results are replaced only once the first image lands; the preview branch contradicts it.

Screenshots: `verify-results-2/shots/a01-first-done.png`, `a02-second-preview.png`, `s02-125-preview.png`.

**Why it matters.** The page moves under the pointer during every render. The last result also vanishes seconds into the next job, so it cannot be compared with the new preview.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Size the preview like the result.** Draw the preview in the box the result will take: `aspectRatio` from the job's size, the max-height of its tier (68vh for one image), `object-fit: contain`, keeping the dashed .85 look.
2. **Pick the tier up front.** Choose it from the expected count (`num_images` × `total_runs`, from the first status frame), not the running count.
3. **Optionally keep the old result.** Move `clearResults()` into `show()` only, and give the preview its own slot. Appending it to the flow would wrap a ninth cell.
4. **Fallback with no layout change.** At the first preview frame set `els.viewer.style.minHeight` to its current `offsetHeight`, and clear it in `show()`.

**Rule.** `generation.js:380-381`: results replace the viewer once the first image lands. design-notes "The live preview" (never a record, dropped when the image lands) and "The running batch on wide screens" are both kept.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke-preview.json` steps 5-8.

*M40 · from results-3 · confirmed. Fits with U47.*

#### U27. The model-load wait is a static "loading weights…" with no elapsed time, and the topbar drops the model name

**medium** · every-session · effort M · results · `web/status.js:83`, `213-218`, `web/generation.js:48`, `427-432`, `web/app.js:155-163`, `189-197`

**Observed.** A scratch wrapper made the load take 15 s.

- **The wait.** The panel shows "loading weights…" with the bar at 0%, `#cell-note` stays empty, and the topbar reads "loading weights…" without the checkpoint's name. Only the 8 px dot pulses.
- **Lag.** For 1.4 s after Generate, the topbar still said "not loaded" beside the panel's "loading weights…".
- **Cancel during the load.**
  - The panel said "Cancelled before anything finished." while the topbar kept "loading weights…" for 12.7 s more.
  - Unload was then enabled. Pressing it gives a 409, and that message stays on screen after the load finishes.
- **Unloaded.** The topbar shows Load and Unload side by side.

Screenshots: `verify-results-2/shots/l03-loading-6s.png`, `l04-cancel-during-load.png`, `l01-topbar-unloaded.png`.

**Why it matters.** A real first load takes minutes, and a static 0% bar looks the same as a hang. The page never says which checkpoint is loading, or that a cancel did not stop the load.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Elapsed time.** While the last status has `waiting_on === 'load'`, run a 1 s client ticker from submit: "loading weights · 0:42" in the progress text, and "The first load of a session takes a few minutes." in `#cell-note`. Use the viewer placeholder only when the viewer is empty.
2. **Indeterminate bar.** A sliding gradient on `.fill`; the existing reduced-motion rule stills it.
3. **Topbar.** "${short} · loading…" and "${short} · not loaded".
4. **No lag.** Call `refreshStatus()` as soon as the POST returns.
5. **One Unload writer.** `syncUnload()` disables Unload when `busy || expandingPrompt || critiquing || modelState === 'loading'`. Hide Unload while Load is shown.
6. **Cancel during a load** says "Cancelled — the weights keep loading, so the next Generate starts sooner."
7. Clear a leftover Unload 409 when the state turns ready.

**Rule.** design-notes "waiting_on and the ETA": `waiting_on` is a field, not a fourth state, and the ticker must be client-side rather than built from server timestamps. `els.unload.disabled` has two writers today that overwrite each other; merge them into one function.

**Smokes.** `smoke-analysis.json:56`, `:60` and `:265` (Unload disabled while the critic runs) pass with one function.

*M42 · from results-8 · adjusted: the finder's "Unload enabled throughout" was wrong; it is disabled during a watched load.*

#### U28. The just-made image has no actions where it is shown; star, reuse and refine are on the gallery card only

**medium** · every-session · effort M · results · `web/panel.js:262-301`, `web/gallery.js:91-152`, `208`, `246`, `web/app.js:230-240`

**Observed.**
- **The viewer** holds one IMG and no buttons after a Generate.
- **Unused width.** A single 1:1 result at 887x887 leaves 1274 px (59%) of the 2161 px viewer empty; a 2:3 result leaves 72%.
- **Where the actions are.** Star, Reuse, Refine and Delete exist on the newest gallery card's hover row (on screen at 100%, partly below the fold at 125%) or in the lightbox after a click.

Screenshots: `verify-results-2/shots/c01-single-portrait.png`, `c03-lightbox.png`.

**Why it matters.** Judge, then star, refine or reuse: that follows every Generate. Each action costs an extra open and close beside unused space, and Refine never starts from the image the user is looking at.

**Recommendation.**
- **A tool row on the result.** `appendResult` wraps each viewer image in `<figure class="result" data-id>` holding the `thumb()` img and a `.tools` row built from the card's helpers: `reuseButton(rec)`, the card's ⤢ handler, ☆ calling `setFavorite`, and ⌦ calling `deleteImage`. Skip ❐ and ⊞.
- **Visibility.** Always visible in the single and multi tiers; on hover or focus in `.many`.
- **Stars.** Extend `setFavorite`'s selector to `.card[data-id], .result[data-id]`.
- **Optional.** A 300 px caption column with seed, size, steps/CFG and duration.

Do not use the class `.card`. The plan's step 9 grafts Studio's facts grid into this column.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md lists Refine's entry points (the card's ⤢, the lightbox's Refine, Reuse on a refined record). A viewer ⤢ is a new one, using the same handler: on klein it says why and opens nothing. Add it to the list. `forgetImages` removes every `[data-id]`, so the new element is cleaned up too.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json` 37, 121 and 181 count `.card`. `smoke-conditioning` 28 and 52-53 need a third step for the viewer ⤢. Clicks on `#viewer img` still reach the result.

*M43 · from results-9 · adjusted: the cost is one extra click through the lightbox, not a dead end.*
