#### U30. The lightbox side column is 280 px of 12-13 px text over about 945 px of empty space, and omits the negative prompt, the date and the run

**medium** · every-session · effort M · gallery · `web/lightbox.js:99-112`, `web/index.html:510-516`, `web/style.css:845-855`, `891-903`

**Observed.**
- **The column.** `.lb-side` sits at x=2247, 280 px wide. The prompt is 13 px and the settings 12 px. The settings end at y=121 and the zoom group starts at y=1066, leaving 945 px empty; the actions sit at y=1115-1287.
- **The negative prompt.** On a record whose negative is "blurry, watermark, …" with true CFG 1.5, the column shows "true CFG 1.5" but never the negative text. The negative prompt is displayed nowhere in the gallery UI.

- **The overlay.** At `rgba(0,0,0,.92)` the panel and gallery cards show through behind the column.

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-2/shots/q-l01-lightbox-negative.png`, `q-l02-lb-side.png`.

**Why it matters.** The lightbox is where an image is judged and reused. Its reproduction set is incomplete there, and the run the image came from can't be reached.

- **Widen the column.** At `min-width: 2000px`: `.lb-side { flex-basis: 360px; width: 360px } .lb-prompt { font-size: 15px }`.

- **A solid backdrop.** `.lightbox { background: #000 }`, or opacity .97.

Keep `#lb-meta` scrolling, the controls pinned and `#lb-close` autofocused.

#### U31. There is no direct path from a finished grid to its sheet, and the Sheet button over the whole gallery builds an empty matrix

**medium** · every-session · effort S · grid · `web/generation.js:451-480`, `web/app.js:217-226`, `web/panel.js:168-197`, `web/sheet.js:240-249`

**Observed.** 124 images in 6 runs.

- **After Run grid.** `#cell-note` reads "Finished 24 images in 3s. Only 12 distinct." with no button. Directly under it, the banner from page load still points at the previous run (U17).
- **Sheet with an empty search.**
  - Columns are Seed and Rows are Steps: 228 cells, none filled.
  - The note reads "0 of 124 loaded · pinned to thing none, subject none, style none, size none, ground none, cfg 2", and the seed headers overlap.
  - The cause is that `reconcileAxes` pins each variable axis to `values[key][0]`, which in a mixed gallery is null ("none").
- **What does work.** The Runs picker and the card's ⊞, which is one of six hover glyphs.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-2/m53-01-done.png`, `verify-grid-2/m53-02-sheet-unfiltered.png`, `verify-grid-2/m53-03-sheet-run.png`.

**Why it matters.** The sheet is the payoff of a grid. The visible Sheet button shows an empty matrix, and the controls that work are hidden.

**Recommendation.**
1. **A Show sheet button.** In `streamJob`'s done branch, after the "Finished …" text, add a ghost button that calls `showRun(data.run_id)` for a grid, or `showJob(jobId)` for a job-only record. Do not auto-open grids.
2. **The stale banner.** Hide or refresh it (U17).
3. **Sheet with an empty search.** When the gallery holds more than one run, set the search to `run:<newest>` with the notice "Showing the newest run - clear the search for the whole gallery". Never redirect a non-empty search.
4. **Better pins.** `reconcileAxes` pins each axis to the value with the most records under the other pins.

**Rule.** design-notes "The contact sheet": the sheet's filters are the gallery's, so free-text sheets must stay possible. A comparison keeps `showJob`.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:157`: append the button, do not replace the text. `smoke-paging.json:29` and `:32` set a search first, so they are unaffected.

*M53 · from grid-6 · adjusted: it reproduced worse than reported (0 of 124 filled).*

#### U32. Sheet headers are 11 px grey, scroll away while lower rows are read, and clip long values

**medium** · every-session · effort M · grid · `web/style.css:738-752`, `web/sheet.js:212-216`, `420-433`

**Observed.**
- **Size.** Column heads and row heads are 11 px `rgb(139,139,154)`. That is about 5.9:1, so the problem is size, not contrast.
- **Clipping.** Column heads 658-990 px wide are still clipped at 44 characters, and row heads are 170x33 and clipped too ("subject weathered old fisherman with a grey beard a…").
- **Scrolling.** With row 2 centred, the column headers are at y=−89 and the controls at −129, behind the 58 px topbar. A 3x2 sheet with 658 px cells starts at y=633, so no multi-row sheet of 2-3 columns ever shows its headers beside its second row.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-2/m54-01-long-default.png`, `verify-grid-2/m54-02-long-subject-cols.png`, `verify-grid-2/m54-05-scrolled-row2.png`.

**Why it matters.** In a matrix the labels carry the meaning. Losing them, or clipping off the words that differ, sends the user back up the page or into the lightbox.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Size.** `.sheet-grid .head, .sheet-grid .rowhead { font-size: 13px; color: var(--text) }`.
2. **Sticky headers.**
   - `.sheet-scroll { overflow: auto; max-height: calc(100vh - 74px) }`.
   - Column heads sticky at top 0 and row heads sticky at left 0, each on a `--bg` background; the corner cell sticky on both.
   - `.sheet { scroll-margin-top: 66px }`, scrolled into view on open (U2).
3. **No clipping on screen.** Render labels unclipped, with a 3-line clamp and the full value as `title`. Keep `clip()` for the export and the pin options.
4. **Row head width.** `.rowhead.text { max-width: clamp(170px, 12vw, 320px) }`.

A softer alternative to the nested scroller is to cap the cell size so that three rows fit (U16).

**Rule.** design-notes "Prompt labels on the sheet" and "The sheet exports client-side". `LABEL_CHARS` stays for the export.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json` 83, 88, 97 and 102 and `smoke-analysis.json` 135 and 316 compare header text exactly. Keep "<label> <value>" with no extra text nodes.

*M54 · from grid-8 · adjusted.*

#### U33. Every keystroke turns all chips into dashed "…", and the half-typed tag flashes red

**medium** · every-session · effort M · tags · `web/tags.js:483-505`, `web/style.css:274`

**Observed.**
- **Typing.** With a 13-tag all-valid prompt, typing ", s" turns all 14 chips grey and dashed with "…".
- **A 600 ms pause** gives 13 ✓ chips plus a red "? sm" for the half-typed word.
- **The next keystroke** turns them all grey again.
- **Layout.** The tray height stayed at 48 px and the next sibling's top at 142 px throughout; the mark width varies by 0-3 px.
- **On real Danbooru** an uncached lookup keeps the whole tray grey for the length of the network round trip.

Screenshots: `verify-tags-1/b02-settled.png`, `verify-tags-1/b03-typing.png`, `verify-tags-1/b04-paused.png`.

**Why it matters.** The tray flickers across its full width at every pause in typing. The one red chip is the word still being typed, so red stops meaning "fix this".

**Recommendation.**
- **Cache verdicts.** Keep a Map from canonical tag text to its last result, cleared when the checkpoint changes. `scheduleTagValidation` renders cached results at once and marks only new items as unchecked.
- **Spare the word being typed.** While the prompt has focus, the item under the caret is the one being typed. If its result is unknown, render it neutral (`.typing`: solid `--line` border, no mark) until a comma, the caret moving away, or 1.5 s. A valid result shows at once.

Drop the proposed `min-height`: this state causes no layout shift.

**Rule.** `tags.js:483-505` and design-notes "Animagine XL 4.0":
- outside changes to the prompt close an open chip editor;
- a lookup failure is "unchecked, never invalid";
- the epoch guard stays.

Cache per checkpoint, because the vocabulary cutoffs differ.

**Smokes.** `smoke-animagine` 12-14 types "made up tag" and expects it to be `.unknown` while the caret is still inside it. Add a blur or a trailing comma.

*M63 · from tags-4 · adjusted: the layout-shift part was dropped.*

#### U34. The viewer forgets every earlier result, and clicking the big image opens a one-image lightbox

**medium** · every-session · effort M · walkthrough · `web/panel.js:250-276`, `web/lightbox.js:7-11`, `114-116`, `web/style.css:650-697`, `web/generation.js:480`

**Observed.**
- **Six single Ctrl+Enter iterations.** `viewerRecords` held 1 each time. The 1024² image shows at 887 px in a 2176x917 viewer, with about 1261 px empty.
- **Clicking the image** opens a lightbox with no counter and both arrows disabled.

**Why it matters.** Iterating on a prompt is the core loop. The most natural click leads nowhere, and the earlier results are off-screen whenever the check is open.

**Recommendation.**

#### U35. On klein the card's ❐ gives no feedback where it was clicked, re-adding is a silent no-op, and at 125% the limit refusal is below the fold

**medium** · every-session on klein · effort M · klein references · `web/gallery.js:108-122`, `web/style.css:783-807`

**Observed.** klein fake.

- **A real click on a card's ❐** (25x24, hover only): the card class stays `card`, `aria-pressed` stays null, focus stays on the button. The chip appears in the panel's list at [35,717,296,52], up to about 1660 px away.
- **Re-adding image 8** changes nothing and shows nothing.
- **A fifth add** writes "Four reference images is the limit; each one lengthens the sequence." at [35,1047]. At QHD that is visible but about 1130 px from the card. At 2048@1.25 it lands at y=1053 in a 1040 px viewport, so nothing visible changes.
- **With the check open at QHD**, the first card row's ❐ is centred at y=1301 of 1305.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v05-card-after-add.png`, `v07-fifth.png`, `v32-125-fifth.png`.

**Why it matters.** References are added while scanning the gallery. With no confirmation where the click happened, users click again, can't tell which cards are already references, and at 125% hit the limit with no visible response.

**Recommendation.**
- **Mark references.** `syncReferenceMarks()`, called from `renderReferences`, sets class `ref` and `data-ref-n` on `.card` and `.sheet-cell`. `.card.ref` gets a 2 px accent outline (the border is already used by `.fav`) and a `::before` number badge.
- **Make ❐ a toggle.** It gets `aria-pressed` and the label "Reference image N - click to remove". Keep its text "❐".
- **At the cap.** The ❐ on non-reference cards becomes `aria-disabled`.
- **Feedback in place.** A role=status bubble over the tool row for about 2 s: "Added as image N", "Removed", or "Four is the most here; remove one first".

**Rule.** `setReferences` stays the one writer. Use `aria-disabled`, not `disabled`.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:33` finds ❐ by its text; keep the glyph.

*G3.3 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-3 · confirmed. Separate from U62 (numbering the chips) and U42 (where messages land).*

#### U36. On klein the Reference images help says the pipeline "takes none", and the count note warns of a cost that was measured as absent

**medium** · every-session on klein · effort S · klein references · `web/index.html:229-236`, `web/sizes.js:186-205`, `web/state.js:41`, `web/gallery.js:119`

**Observed.**
- **The tip.** On klein (`referenceSupported` true), `#reference-tip` reads: "Reference images condition the picture on one you have already made. This checkpoint's pipeline takes none - FLUX.2 [klein] is the one here that does." It is static markup, and `els.referenceTip` is never written.
- **The note** reads "(1, each lengthening the sequence)" up to "(4, …)".
- **The measurement.** design-notes records "The 2-3x cost the review predicted did not appear": 10.2 s with no reference, and 8.2-11.3 s with one or two.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v02-ref-tip.png`.

**Why it matters.** The one checkpoint that takes references tells the user it doesn't, in the very help they open to learn how references work.

**Recommendation.**
- **The tip.** `syncConditioning` sets it from the capability.
  - Supported: "Up to four gallery images the picture is conditioned on, in this order. With two or more, say in the prompt what to take from each by its number - "the jacket from image 1". Each is resized to about 1 MP on the way in."
  - Unsupported: today's sentence.
- **The note.**
  - With 0 references: "(add one with ❐ on a gallery image)".
  - With 1: "(1 of 4)".
  - With 2 or more: "(N of 4 - name them image 1…N in the prompt)".
- **The limit message** (`gallery.js:119`): "Four reference images is the most this panel sends; remove one first."

**Rule.** `index.html:226-228`: controls that don't apply are greyed with the reason in the badge. A single reference needs no "image 1" (design-notes:2992), so the note must not ask for numbers when there is only one.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:37` expects "lengthening the sequence"; update it. `smoke-flux.json:27` ("takes none") stays.

*G3.4 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-4 · adjusted.*

#### U37. Ctrl+Enter inside the grid fold runs Generate, which renders the literal {placeholders} as a single image

**medium** · common · effort S · grid, panel · `web/app.js:255-263`, `web/generation.js:217`, `web/index.html:262`, `410`, `README.md:228`

**Observed.** The prompt was "a {size} fox sitting on {ground}, golden hour", with variables `size` and `ground` and a CFG axis of 2,4; the preview said 8 images. A real Ctrl+Enter in `#grid-cfg` sent only one POST, to `/api/generate`, and showed "Finished 1 image in 0s.". The image's alt text is the raw template, and the Runs picker gained "1 img · a {size} fox …". Pressing it in `#grid-steps` does the same. The Generate and Run grid buttons have empty titles, and the text "Ctrl+Enter" appears nowhere in the DOM: Refine's title carries it only while the dialog is open. Screenshot: `verify-grid-1/m7-ctrl-enter.png`.

**Why it matters.** The page's submit shortcut, pressed while editing the grid, renders one wrong image and leaves a junk entry in the Runs picker. If the user walks away straight after pressing it, the night's grid never runs.

**Recommendation.**
- Route the shortcut by focus: `if (els.refineDialog.open) void runRefine(); else if (els.gridSection.open && e.target.closest?.('#grid-section')) els.gridRun.click(); else generate();`.
- Set the title "Generate (Ctrl+Enter)" on `#btn-generate` and "Run grid (Ctrl+Enter from a grid box)" on `#btn-grid`, and update `README.md:228`.
- Optionally, add a client-only guard in `generate()`: if the fold is open, the variables box is not empty and the prompt contains a `{word}`, show "Run grid fills the {placeholders}; Generate would render the braces." and return.
- Do not add a server-side refusal: plain Generate takes braces literally on purpose, for example NovelAI-style `{tag}` emphasis.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md says "Ctrl+Enter in the dialog runs the pass, not Generate", which is the precedent for a context-dependent shortcut. `README.md:228` ("generates from anywhere") needs a sentence about the grid fold.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json` 100-103 tests the dialog branch, which is unchanged.

*M7 · from grid-2, panel-4 · adjusted (high → medium: the wrong output is visible at once).*

#### U38. Use rewrite, Use reordered and Apply replace the prompt, and the original disappears from Compare and from the critic's answer

**medium** · common · effort S · walkthrough, analysis · `web/analysis.js:84-86`, `326-339`, `469-477`, `893-909`

**Observed.**
- Before Use, Compare offers Original, Reordered and Rewrite.
- A real click on the critic's Use rewrite then changes three things:
  - Compare offers only Original, which is now the rewrite;
  - Run comparison is disabled, titled "Needs a second version of the prompt to compare against";
  - `#critic-result` is empty, `critiqueResult` is null, and the replaced prompt appears nowhere on the page.
- Ctrl+Z brings the prompt back but marks the check stale. Comparing then means Check again, plus about 15 s for a new critique.
- On Animagine, the order finding's Apply suggestion is byte-identical to Use reordered.

analysis-5's report of a 223 px page scroll after Use did not reproduce. Screenshots: `verify-analysis-1/shots/q-05-critic.png`, `q-07-after-use-rewrite.png`, `t-01-18tags-check.png`, `t-04-after-apply.png`.

**Why it matters.** "Try the rewrite, then compare it with what I had" is the obvious order. It ends at a disabled button and a second critic run, and the only way back is an undo nothing mentions.

**Recommendation.**
- In the critic's Use rewrite and Use reordered handlers, before `usePromptText(text)`: if Your version is empty, copy `analysisResult.source` into it, tick `compareChoice.yours`, and add a note under Compare: "Your version holds the prompt before Use rewrite - Run comparison tests both on the same seeds."
- Add "Compare first? Use replaces the prompt; Ctrl+Z brings it back." to both buttons' titles.
- Extending this to Check's Use reordered and Apply is optional, but it changes four Animagine smoke expectations.
- On the order finding whose suggestion equals the reordered prompt, replace its Apply with the text "Same as Use reordered above".

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md says Use and Apply write through `replaceBoxText`; the comparison never writes `els.prompt`; and Your version is the user's own text, cleared with the panel and never persisted. Filling Your version only when it is empty keeps all three rules, and the stale critic answer is still dropped.

**Smokes.** Scoped to the critic, nothing fails; `smoke-analysis` 85-92 gains one expectation. Extended to Check's Use, `smoke-animagine` 98, 107, 111, 129 and 137 change.

*M8 · from walkthrough-2, analysis-5 · adjusted (high → medium, reproduced partly: nothing is lost, since Ctrl+Z works).*

#### U39. Queued jobs have no name: the page never says which running or queued job Cancel will act on

**medium** · common · effort M · results · `web/generation.js:65-71`, `124-165`, `192-199`, `backend/jobs.py:123-160`

**Observed.**
- Three jobs were queued: fox, whale and parrot, each with Runs 2.
- While the whale job ran:
  - the prompt box held the parrot's text;
  - the panel showed "run 1/2", "6/10" and "1 more job queued behind this one.";
  - `/api/jobs` carried no prompt, and the page text never contained "whale".
- A real click on Cancel stopped the job being watched (fox, 1 of 2 kept), not the one in the prompt box.
- On load, the page reattached to a job an earlier page had queued, and nothing named it.
- Cancel queued cancels every waiting job at once.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m10-01-panel-B-running.png`, `verify-results-1/m10-03-after-cancel.png`.

**Why it matters.** After a few submissions the user cannot tell what is rendering or what is waiting. Cancel may stop a job other than the one they mean, and there is no way to drop one of several queued jobs.

**Recommendation.**
- Backend: add `"prompt": self.request.prompt[:120]` to `Job.snapshot`, and optionally the image count and size.
- Frontend:
  - Under Generate, show "Now: <first 60 chars> · 1024×1024 · 2 images" at 13 px, filled from the status frame.
  - Title Cancel "Stop "<snippet>" after the current step".
  - Render `#queue-note` as one row per queued job, each with an × button (aria-label "Cancel queued job: <snippet>") that POSTs to `/api/jobs/{id}/cancel`. Keep Cancel queued when two or more jobs are waiting.

**Rule.** design-notes "The browser's queue": Cancel acts on the watched job and Cancel queued on the rest. The per-row cancel uses the existing route.

**Smokes.** None. Re-run `tests/test_api*.py` for the jobs routes.

*M10 · from results-1 · adjusted (high → medium).*

#### U40. At QHD the lightbox never shows a 1 MP image at 1:1: the 1:1 button, the 1 key and double-click all do nothing

**medium** · common · effort S · gallery · `web/lightbox.js:55`, `64-68`, `213`

**Observed.** At 2560x1305 the stage is 2211x1269.

| Image | Fit | What 1:1 does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1024² | "fit · 124%" | zoomed to 496%, the 1 key gives "fit · 124%" at scale 1 |
| 1360x768 | "fit · 163%" | a real double-click, the 1 key and the 1:1 button all leave it at fit |
| 896x1152 | "fit · 110%" | — |
| 1024² at 2048@1.25 | "fit · 123%" | — (one-to-one scale would be 0.816) |

The stage's title still promises "double-click for 1:1". Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/b08-169-lightbox.png`, `verify-gallery-1/shots/c01-125-lightbox.png`.

**Why it matters.** The DPR-exact 1:1 exists so that sharpening, steps or refine sweeps can be judged without resampling. On QHD, every default-size image is judged through a 1.1-1.65x upscale, while three controls that promise otherwise silently do nothing.

**Recommendation.**
- `zoomLightbox`: let the floor be `Math.min(1, oneToOneScale(rec))`, and keep `if (next <= 1) lbView.x = lbView.y = 0`.
- Label: `Math.abs(scale - one) < 1e-3 ? '1:1' : scale === 1 ? 'fit · p%' : 'p%'`.
- `ondblclick`: if the scale is not 1, call `zoomFit()`.
- Keep `.zoomed` above 1, and keep the stage click closing the lightbox only at or below 1.

**Rule.** design-notes "The lightbox zooms" says "a scale relative to fit (never below 1)". On QHD that floor defeats the purpose of 1:1, so change that sentence and the comments in `lightbox.js`.

**Smokes.** None break: smoke.json's cells are above fit at 1400x900. Add a step for a below-fit image.

*M11 · from gallery-2 · adjusted (high → medium).*

#### U41. Pause gives no acknowledgement until the cell ends

**medium** · common · effort S · results, copy-a11y · `web/generation.js:205-215`, `403-411`, `434-440`, `web/style.css:613`, `626`, `backend/jobs.py:487-492`

**Observed.**
- A 3-run job:
  - at 0 ms the text is "pausing after this cell…";
  - at 146 ms it is overwritten by "run 1/3", and the button still says Pause;
  - at 819 ms it reads "paused — GPU handed back" and the button says Resume. The bar is squeezed to 5 px, the ETA line is empty, and Pause turns danger-red on hover.
- A single-image job: Pause at step 7/28 was overwritten at 312 ms by "8/28". The job ran to the end with `pause_requested` true, because one image has no next cell to pause before.
- On the card, one FLUX cell takes tens of seconds; that is how long the click looks ignored.

Screenshots: `verify-results-1/m23-01-paused-progress.png`, `verify-results-1/m23-02-paused-panel.png`.

**Why it matters.** With no feedback, the user's next action is a second click or Cancel, which ends the run instead of holding it.

**Recommendation.**
1. On click, relabel the button "Pausing…" with `aria-pressed=true`, and treat a click on "Pausing…" as withdrawing the request. Derive the label from `pause_requested` in each status snapshot.
2. While a pause is requested, the progress text reads "run 2/6 · pausing after this run" instead of being overwritten.
3. On the paused acknowledgement, keep the progress text at "run 1/3" and write "Paused after run 1/3 · GPU handed back" into `#cell-note`.
4. Exclude Pause from the danger hover (`.ghost.small:not(#btn-pause)`, or U91's opt-in class).
5. On a running one-cell job, make Pause `aria-disabled` with the title "A single image has no pause point — Cancel stops it".

In plain jobs, say "image" or "run" rather than "cell".

**Rule.** design-notes "Pause": the label flips to Resume only on the worker's acknowledgement, and the pause lands at the cell boundary. "Pausing…" claims nothing, so it keeps that rule.

**Smokes.** No smoke mentions pause. `smoke.json:157` needs the done summary to keep overwriting `#cell-note`.

*M23 · from results-6, copy-a11y-9 · confirmed.*

#### U42. Gallery and card notices land in the panel's #error slot, about 2,400 px from the control that caused them

**medium** · common · effort S · gallery, copy-a11y · `web/state.js:316-329`, `web/gallery.js:65-69`, `108-127`, `287-324`, `web/index.html:292`, `423-466`

**Observed.**
- Delete unstarred with the free-text search "harbour": the button is at [2418,517], and its refusal appears in red at [35,1003], 2383 px to the left and 486 px down.
- ❐ on a card at [1090,568]: the refusal lands at [35,1003], with literal backticks ("no `image` parameter").
- The search "seed:abc": the grid shows 0 cards while the header still says "(48)", and the explanation is in the panel.

The panel is sticky, so the message is on screen, but in the other column. Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/b03-delete-unstarred.png`, `b02-ref-refusal.png`, `b04-bad-term.png`.

**Why it matters.** At this size the user is looking at the right half of a 2560 px screen. A click with no visible effect reads as a broken button, and an empty grid beside a count reads as lost data.

**Recommendation.**
- Add `<p class="error" id="gallery-error" role="alert" hidden>` after `.gallery-head`.
- Generalise to `showError(message, note = false, slot = els.error)`, keeping unhide-before-write and the lightbox mirror.
- Route these to the new slot:
  - Delete unstarred's four outcomes;
  - the card's ❐ and ⊞ refusals;
  - the card ⤢ note, through a slot parameter on `openRefine`;
  - `loadGallery`'s catch;
  - Re-render's errors.
- Clear the slot in `resetGallery`, and clear the count on a failed page.
- Put each message in one slot only, or screen readers hear it twice.
- Reword the ❐ refusal without backticks: "This checkpoint can't use reference images; FLUX.2 [klein] can."
- Keep Reuse's own notes in the panel, because Reuse scrolls there.

**Rule.** `showError` unhides before writing (because the slot is role=alert) and mirrors into `#lb-error` while the lightbox is open. design-notes "Delete unstarred": whether a search is strict stays the server's decision.

**Smokes.**
- Change: `smoke.json:178`, `182` and `190`; `smoke-paging.json:51`; `smoke-conditioning.json:29`; and `smoke-refine-unavailable.json:13`, if `openRefine`'s default slot moves.
- Unchanged: `smoke-refine.json:138` and `:142`; `smoke-conditioning.json:54`.

#### U43. Closing the lightbox, or cancelling a Refine opened from it, drops focus at the first card instead of where the user was

**medium** · common · effort S · gallery, refine-dialog · `web/lightbox.js:156`, `web/refine.js:98`, `397-404`, `web/app.js:235-238`

**Observed.**
- A walk: 60 images; opened card 1, pressed ArrowRight 40 times to 41/60 (id 22), then Escape. Focus landed on the first card (id 62) at scrollY 0, and card 22 was not marked.
- Refine from the lightbox: from 9/60, zoomed, ⤢ Refine closed the lightbox and opened the dialog. Escape then left both closed, with focus on the card first opened; the 9/60 position and the zoom were gone.

At QHD about 48 cards fit on screen, so the walked-to card leaves the screen only on longer walks. Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/b05-after-walk-escape.png`, `b06-refine-from-lb.png`, `b07-after-refine-cancel.png`.

**Why it matters.** Triage is walk, close, act. And looking at the Refine dialog and then cancelling costs the whole lightbox session, which makes the dialog feel risky to try.

**Recommendation.**
1. Return focus to where the walk ended.
   - `closeLightbox` captures `lbList[lbIndex]`.
   - The programmatic callers (`openRefine`, the Reuse handler, `forgetImages`) set a flag, `lbHandoff = true`.
   - On a user close (Escape, ✕, a backdrop click), focus that record's card or viewer image inside a `requestAnimationFrame`, which also scrolls it into view.
2. Reopen the lightbox when Refine is cancelled.
   - `openRefine` saves `refineReturn = { id, gallery: lbList === galleryRecords, list }` when the lightbox is open, and `runRefine` clears it.
   - `refineClosed` resolves the record by id and calls `openLightbox(rec, list)`.

The alternative is to stack the dialog over the lightbox. That contradicts the documented rule and needs a guard in `app.js`'s lightbox key block.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: the lightbox's Refine closes the lightbox, and `refineSource` is null once the dialog closes. Reopening on cancel keeps both. Do not focus a card when Reuse closes the lightbox, because Reuse scrolls to the panel.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json:58-71`: add `els.lightbox.open` at :71. `smoke.json:57-59`: a backdrop close now focuses the last card.

*M26 · from gallery-9, refine-dialog-5 · adjusted.*

#### U44. Reuse scrolls the page to the top even when the sticky panel is already in view, and a result made while scrolled lands off-screen

**medium** · common · effort M · gallery, walkthrough · `web/gallery.js:400-401`, `web/generation.js:391`, `480`, `web/style.css:65-73`

**Observed.**
- At QHD, a card at scrollY 611 with the panel sticky and the prompt at y=111-236: ↻ scrolls to 0. At 125%, from 721 to 0.
- At 125%, Ctrl+Enter at scrollY 458:
  - the viewer grows above the viewport, and scroll anchoring moves the page to 815;
  - the viewer ends up at top −739;
  - the only signs are "Finished 1 image in 2s." and a new card at top left.
- At QHD, a batch of 4 at scrollY 700:
  - during the run the viewer's bottom is at −244;
  - at the end scrollY is 0 and the cards drop from 127 to 96, because of `resetGallery`.

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-2/shots/q-r01-before-reuse.png`, `q-r02-after-reuse.png`, `s-r04-after-generate-scrolled.png`.

**Why it matters.** Each Reuse from deep in the gallery loses the user's place. A result that lands off-screen looks as if nothing happened.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `reuseIntoPanel`, scroll only if the prompt is off screen: `if (r.top < 0 || r.bottom > innerHeight) els.prompt.scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest', …})`.
2. Today's scroll to the top is also what brings the viewer into view for the next Generate. So pair step 1 with a "New image ↑" button beside `#progress-text`. Show it when a job's first image or preview frame lands while the viewer is above the viewport; its click scrolls the viewer into view. Never auto-scroll.
3. Keep the position across a job by prepending new cards instead of resetting the gallery (U25).

**Rule.** The reduced-motion handling stays. The static-panel layouts still scroll.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke-conditioning.json:79` and `:110`, `smoke-flux.json:41` and `:52`, and `smoke-refine.json:121-135`. With step 3, also `smoke.json` and `smoke-paging.json`.

#### U45. There is no one-click way to keep the seed of the image just made, and the seed is shown only in the lightbox

**medium** · common · effort S · panel, walkthrough · `web/generation.js:151-161`, `453-461`, `web/index.html:254-260`

**Observed.**
- Generate with a blank Seed box: the box stays empty (placeholder "random"), and `#cell-note` says "Finished 1 image in 0s.". The seed (5720723289104536) exists only in `viewerRecords`; the viewer shows no text and the image no title.
- The 107 px Seed box shows "452275645" of 4522756457521740.
- A grid run with a blank Seed writes its base seed back into the box, and the next plain Generate silently reuses it.
- The only one-click way to take a seed is the card's ↻, which also replaces the prompt, size, steps and CFG (U4).

Screenshots: `verify-panel-1/m32-01-after-generate-panel.png`, `verify-panel-1/m32-02-seed-16.png`.

**Why it matters.** "Same seed, tweak the prompt" is the usual loop once something good turns up.

**Recommendation.**
- Do not put the button next to the dice: the Seed row is already full at 296 px.
- Put it in the finish line instead: "Finished 1 image in 4s · seed 5720723289104536" with an inline "Use seed" button. For a batch, say "seeds X–Y" and write the first. The button sets only `els.seed`, then calls `syncRuns(); schedulePreview(); persistPanel()`.
- Show the same caption under the viewer when it holds one image.
- `#seed { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums }` with the spinners hidden, or take the width from U14.
- When a grid has written its base back, the run hint says "Seed fixed at …; 🎲 for a random one".

**Rule.** `generation.js:151-161`: a blank Seed draws a fresh seed on every Generate, and only a grid writes its base back, which Resume needs. Both stay. Unload stays enabled while unloaded, because it still releases the critic and the tokenizers.

#### U46. A single-axis sheet is one horizontal ribbon of 96 px cells, several screens wide

**medium** · common · effort M · grid, visual · `web/sheet.js:206-231`, `341-373`, `460-466`

**Observed.**
- A 120-seed run: one row of 96x96 cells, `scrollWidth` 12246 against 2176; 21 of 120 cells visible and the page below empty.
- A 28-image batch, where only the seed varies: 28 columns, 21 visible (16 at 125%).
- With Sheet off, the same run shows as 171 px gallery cards that wrap, so the sheet is the worse view of the same images.
- `buildSheetCanvas` produces a 12254x194 image, past `EXPORT_MAX_EDGE`, because the 96 px floor wins.

Every multi-image Generate opens this way from the Runs picker. Screenshot: `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-120-scrolled.png`.

**Why it matters.** Seeds are the commonest sweep, and at QHD they get the worst layout.

**Recommendation.**
- In `renderSheet`, when there is no row axis and `colValues.length × 102 > clientWidth − 40`, lay the cells out as `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr))` with no header row.
- Each cell carries its own label ("Seed 903") along its bottom edge, in `colValues` order.
- `buildSheetCanvas` wraps the same way: `ceil(sqrt(n))` columns, each cell labelled underneath.
- Keep the single strip when it fits.

**Rule.** `chooseAxes` keeps Seed as a seeds-only run's one dimension, and the page never scrolls sideways. Two-dimensional and variant sheets keep their matrix.

#### U47. Batches of 2-4 are shown at 34vh, using about 42% of the viewer's width

**medium** · common · effort S · results · `web/style.css:662-670`, `web/panel.js:262-269`

**Observed.**

| Batch | Today | Screen used |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2 squares | two 444x444 tiles | 900 of 2146 px |
| 3 at 2:3 | 298x444 each | |
| 4 squares | 444 px each | |
| 4 at 16:9 | wrapped 2x2 at 786x444 | |
| 2 squares at 125% | 354 px tiles | 44% |

The finder's first proposal, one row at 68vh, would halve four 16:9 tiles. Screenshots: `verify-results-2/shots/b01-batch2-square.png`, `b02-batch3-portrait.png`, `b04-batch4-wide.png`.

**Why it matters.** A batch of 2 or 3 is the common way to compare seeds, and it gets a quarter of the area the screen has room for.

**Recommendation.** Choose the column count in JS, from `appendResult` plus a ResizeObserver:
- For c in 1..n, with rows = ceil(n/c), tile = min((W − (c−1)·12)/c, ((H − (rows−1)·12)/rows)·a), where H is 68vh, a is the aspect ratio, and n is the expected count (U26), capped at 4.
- Set `--cols` and `--tile-h`, with `.viewer.multi { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--cols), auto); justify-content: center } .viewer.multi img { max-height: var(--tile-h) }`.

That gives 2 squares at 887 px, 3 at 702, 4 at 523, three 2:3 tiles at 595x887, and four 16:9 tiles in a 2x2 at 776x437. Mixed aspects fall back to flex wrap, and batches over 4 are unchanged.

**Rule.** "The running batch on wide screens" governs only the over-4 tier; its "keep their existing layout" is a scope statement, not a reason. Update the sentence.

**Smokes.** None assert tile sizes, and `#viewer img` is still the first result.

*M41 · from results-4 · adjusted.*

#### U48. Starring on the sheet needs a lightbox round trip, the star mark is a 12 px glyph, and the Favorites sheet drops the shared settings

**medium** · common · effort S · grid · `web/style.css:765-771`, `816-834`, `web/sheet.js:404-428`

**Observed.**
- A starred 691 px cell shows a 12 px `::after` star in its corner, with its border unchanged. A gallery `.card.fav` gets an 18 px star in a 28 px badge and an amber ring.
- Sheet cells have no buttons. The lightbox walks the sheet in reading order (1/6 → 2/6), so triage is a click, then arrow and F for each keeper, then Escape.
- With Favorites ticked, the two keepers become a 2x2 with two empty cells at 1040 px. Their shared seed, steps 4 and CFG 2 appear nowhere.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-fav-cell.png`, `verify-grid-1/sh-qhd-fav-sheet.png`.

**Why it matters.** Marking keepers on the sheet is how a night's results are narrowed down. The mark is nearly invisible, and the filtered view hides the settings that made those images keepers.

**Recommendation.**
1. Share the border, ring and 28 px badge between `.card.fav` and `.sheet-cell.fav`.
2. Add one keydown listener on `#sheet-grid`: F on a focused cell image, with the lightbox closed, calls `setFavorite`. Optionally add a hover-only ☆ with `tabindex=-1`.
3. In `renderSheet`, print the axes that have a single value in a new `<p class="hint" id="sheet-fixed">`, for example "Fixed: steps 4 · CFG 2 · seed …". Keep it out of `#sheet-note`. Optionally add it to the export caption.

**Rule.** `setFavorite` toggles `.fav` by class and never re-renders the sheet. A ☆ in every cell would add up to 1024 tab stops.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json` 97 and 102 and `smoke-analysis.json` 319 compare `#sheet-note` exactly, hence the separate element. Re-run `smoke.json` 85-89.

*M48 · from grid-9 · adjusted: the triage cost is the lost side-by-side view, not keystrokes.*

#### U49. A strength sweep's sheet has no source to compare against, and square cells run off the screen

**medium** · common · effort M · refine-flow · `web/sheet.js:28-30`, `372`, `485-491`, `web/panel.js:236-248`, `web/generation.js:478-480`

**Observed.** Sweeps of 0.05, 0.1 and 0.2 were run on a 3:2 source (1264x848 → 1904x1280) and a 1:1 source (1024² → 1536²).

- Square source at QHD:
  - cells are 712 px with their bottom at y=1439, 134 px past the fold;
  - the viewer strip above repeats the same three images (y=76-550), and the sheet starts at y=662.
- The 3:2 sweep fits (717x482 cells, bottom at 1207).
- At 125% the square cells are 542 px, bottom at 1178 of 1040.
- In sheet mode the source is visible nowhere: the gallery is hidden, the strip shows only passes, and the lightbox list holds only passes.
- The column heads are 11 px muted text.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-flow-1/shots/b01-sweep-square.png`, `b02-sweep-square-full.png`, `a07-sweep-sheet.png`.

**Why it matters.** The design notes say "The strength axis exists to put the candidates on one sheet". Without the original on that sheet, the strength is picked by comparing passes with each other.

**Recommendation.**
1. When every sheet record shares one non-null `refine`, fetch the source once and add a leading column:
   - a `.sheet-cell.source` headed "Source #4", spanning all rows;
   - clicking it opens `openLightbox(source, [source, ...sheetRecords])`;
   - the export draws it as the first column;
   - it is never an axis value, and never counted in `cellOf`, `keep_rates` or `stats`.
2. After a fresh sweep sheet, scroll to it (U2).
3. Cap the column width in JS, not with `max-height` on the cells: `maxW = (innerHeight − 160) × aspect` and `repeat(n, minmax(96px, ${maxW}px))`, with `justify-content: center`.
4. Optionally, set the heads at 13 px in `var(--text)`.

**Rule.** design-notes "The contact sheet": layout and export are client-side. A strength of null means a direct render, so the source must never become an axis value.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json:79-82` holds. Exclude `.source` from cell counts in `smoke-analysis` 134 and the comparison smokes.

*M49 · from refine-flow-7 · adjusted. Seen in passing: Re-render reads "Re-render 3 at 1024×1024" on a sheet of 1536² passes.*

#### U50. Clicking a finding selects its words in the prompt box but leaves them scrolled out of view

**medium** · common · effort S · analysis · `web/analysis.js:280-292`, `545-555`, `web/index.html:59`

**Observed.**
- After a 76-word prompt is typed, the 296x125 box is scrolled to the bottom (scrollTop 116 of 249).
- A click on the "no people" warning selects characters [34,43] on line 2, but scrollTop does not change, so the selection is out of view.
- The cut link, clicked with the box scrolled to the top, selects the tail and leaves scrollTop at 0.
- The finder's fix (select before focusing) and two other focus tricks left scrollTop unchanged. A mirror-div scroll worked.

Screenshots: `verify-analysis-1/shots/sel-01-current.png`, `q-04-prompt-after-finding-click.png`, `sel-03-mirror.png`, `sel-04-mirror-tail.png`.

**Why it matters.** Clicking a finding to find its words is how a warning with no Apply gets acted on. The click looks as if it did nothing.

**Recommendation.** After `setSelectionRange` in `selectIn`:
1. Create a hidden mirror div with the textarea's computed width, box-sizing, padding, border, font, line-height, spacing, `white-space: pre-wrap` and `overflow-wrap`.
2. Fill it with the text up to the selection start, plus a zero-width marker.
3. Set `el.scrollTop = max(0, marker.offsetTop − el.clientHeight / 3)`, then remove the div.

That is about 15 lines. It serves findings, the cut link and the critic's quotes. An optional 600 ms outline flash confirms the click.

**Rule.** The still-in-place guard and the `units()` conversion run first. A greyed negative box still selects nothing.

**Smokes.** None fail. Add "selection inside the scroll window" checks to `smoke-analysis` 24-27 and 34-35.

*M58 · from analysis-2 · adjusted: the proposed reordering does not work.*

#### U51. The critic's answer is a ~460 px stack of 2,135 px-wide boxes; the diff starts collapsed, and the rewrite's findings repeat the prompt's without saying so

**medium** · common · effort M · analysis · `web/analysis.js:662-755`, `803-846`, `web/style.css:365`, `430-455`

**Observed.**
- At QHD on FLUX, `#critic-result` is [379,500,2135,461]: parts 37 px, issue 49, Rewrite block 241, Reordered 79.
- Both textareas are 2135x55 at 12 px, about 403 characters a line (305 at 2048).
- The diff starts closed ("Changed from original: 11 characters added, 0 characters removed"). Opened, it prints all 427 characters directly above the identical textarea.
- The rewrite's findings carry the same three rules as the prompt's. On Animagine, the order finding is a different instance.
- Run comparison sits at y=1260 on Animagine, and below the fold at 2048.

Screenshots: `verify-analysis-2/q-05-critic-result.png`, `q-06-critic-diff-open.png`, `a-02-critic.png`, `s-03-answer-viewport.png`.

**Why it matters.** The user wants to know what the critic changed and whether it fixes the findings. The change is behind a click, and answering the second question means comparing two near-identical lists by eye.

**Recommendation.**
1. `.analysis-text, .critic-diff { max-width: 120ch }`, about 140 characters a line. The same rule fixes Your version (U122).
2. Wrap Rewrite and Reordered in `.critic-variants`, two columns from 1800 px, keeping `.critic-variant` on each.
3. After `</summary>` (outside it), add a muted summary of the first one to three changed runs from `promptChanges`, up to 80 characters. Keep the diff closed by default.
4. Keep every rewrite finding, tagged "also in your prompt" (same rule) or "new", then add "No longer flagged: …". Do not collapse findings by rule.
5. Put a caption, "How the critic read it", above the parts list, as a `<p>` rather than a `dt`.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: rewrites show character counts and an expandable exact diff (`promptChanges`); the rewrite's findings select inside its own textarea; the rewrite is never called trusted.

**Smokes.** `smoke-rewrite.json:6-8` and `11-12` (summary text, closed default); `smoke-analysis.json:61` and `63`; `smoke-animagine.json:150` (`closest('.critic-variant')`).

*M60 · from analysis-10 · adjusted.*

#### U52. Autocomplete pre-selects its first item and ranks guide tags first, so "lo" then Tab inserts "low quality"

**medium** · common · effort S · tags · `web/tags.js:91`, `218`, `web/app.js:142-144`, `backend/main.py:353-364`

**Observed.** Real keystrokes on Animagine:
- "1girl, solo, lo" lists "low quality" and "low score", both model tags, with the first already active. Tab gives "1girl, solo, low quality" and focus stays in the box.
- "gre" lists "great score" ahead of green hair (590k posts) and green eyes (900k), and Enter writes "great score".
- "1girl, s" pre-selects "safe".
- The chip insert editor has the same trap: "lo" then Tab gives "low quality".

Local tags come first by construction (`main.py:361-362`).

**Why it matters.** A fast typist who presses Tab to move on puts a quality-lowering tag into the positive prompt, and its chip turns green.

**Recommendation.**
- Ranking (`main.py:353-364`): an exact match first; then remote and local tags together, ordered by match quality, whole-word prefix before substring. Never put a recommended-negative tag first in a positive suggestion unless it is an exact match.
- Pre-selection (`tags.js:91`, `218`): pre-select item 0 only when the query is three or more characters and item 0 starts with it. Otherwise leave nothing selected, so Tab moves on and Enter inserts a newline. ArrowDown still selects.
- Optionally, relabel the kind "model" as "guide".

**Rule.** design-notes "Animagine XL 4.0" puts guide tags ahead of remote results so they work offline. That is a reason about availability, not ranking: offline, the local tags are the whole list anyway. The chip editor's rule that "Enter on an untouched edit keeps the tag" stays.

**Smokes.** `smoke-animagine` 61 changes only if the label is renamed. The "green h" + Enter steps (`animagine` 5-10 and 41, `illustrious` 10-13, `kohaku` 9-12, `wai` 11-13) keep working under the three-character threshold.

*M62 · from tags-3 · adjusted.*

#### U53. The Tags helper popover covers 71% of the prompt box it inserts into

**medium** · common · effort S · tags · `web/style.css:197-204`, `web/tags.js:540-543`, `559-579`, `611-613`, `688-691`, `web/app.js:153`

**Observed.**
- `#tags-menu` [122,119,442,579] sits over `#prompt` [35,119,331,244]: it covers 209 of the box's 296 px of width, over its full height, and spills 94 px over the stage. It is the same at 2048@1.25.
- The menu text is 11 px, with 10 px headings.
- With the caret at the end of a 17-tag prompt, "safe" is written as "…, absurdres, safe", after the quality tags. Meanwhile the menu's first line reads "Recommended order: Person count → Character → Series → Rating → Details → Quality", and Check flags the result.
- Clicking the visible strip of the prompt to move the caret closes the menu.

Screenshot: `verify-tags-1/c01-tagsmenu.png`.

**Why it matters.** The helper says it inserts "at the prompt caret", and it hides that caret. Placing one tag where it belongs takes: click the prompt, reopen Tags, click the tag, Check, Apply.

**Recommendation.**
1. Anchor the popover under the textarea, over Negative and Size: move `#tags-menu` into `.prompt-editor` after the textarea, at top `calc(100% + 6px)`, left 0, width 100%. Extend `app.js:153`'s outside-click test to `#tag-helper, #tags-menu`. Do not float it over the stage, where it would cover the first chips.
2. Name the insertion point in the menu: "Inserts after "absurdres" (the end of the prompt) - the guide puts Rating before Details."
3. Slot new tags automatically only if the server supplies the slots, and only after the contract is changed explicitly.
4. Set `.tag-menu` text at 12 px.

**Rule.** design-notes "Animagine XL 4.0": each tag is an explicit action at the current caret, and nothing reorders free-form tags. CLAUDE.md: the page has no reorder rule of its own. Client-side slotting would also be Animagine-specific: Kohaku puts Rating last, Illustrious third, and WAI folds it into Subject and scene.

**Smokes.** None assert where a tag is placed. The outside-click selector must change along with the move.

#### U54. Checkboxes show no keyboard focus at all

**medium** · common · effort S · copy-a11y · `web/style.css:114`, `web/index.html:397`, `446`, `450-452`, `web/analysis.js:937`

**Observed.**
- Tab onto `#fav-only` or `#all-models`: `:focus-visible` is true, but the computed outline is `none` and the border `0px none`.
- Screenshots of the gallery head with either box focused are pixel-identical to one with nothing focused (a PIL diff finds no difference). `#grid-resume` behaves the same.
- Focus on the Sheet button does render.
- The cause is the global rule `textarea:focus, input:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--accent) }`. It catches checkboxes, which do not draw that border.

Screenshots: `verify-copy-a11y-1/m69-head-fav-focused.png`, `m69-head-none.png`, `m69-resume-focused.png`.

**Why it matters.** A Space pressed while focus is invisible toggles Favorites or All models, and the gallery silently re-scopes. This fails WCAG 2.4.7.

**Recommendation.** `input[type=checkbox]:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent-2); outline-offset: 2px }`. Its specificity (0,2,1) beats the global rule, and `--accent-2` is 10.4:1 on `--panel`. Narrowing the global rule to non-checkbox inputs is optional. A focus ring for text inputs is optional polish: their border already passes at 3.45:1.

**Rule.** None. CLAUDE.md's focus rules all depend on focus being visible.

**Smokes.** None.

*M69 · from copy-a11y-1 · confirmed.*

#### U55. The grid fold's key readouts are its smallest elements: a 77 px Run grid button and an 11 px muted preview

**medium** · common · effort S · grid · `web/index.html:302-304`, `409-410`, `web/generation.js:331-344`, `web/style.css:70-73`, `472-476`, `482-508`, `backend/grid.py:425-447`

**Observed.**
- **Button width.** On the Krea 2 Turbo fake, `#btn-grid` is 77x43, while `#btn-generate` is 296x43. The cause: in Edge 154 a `<details>` element lays its children out inside `::details-content`, a block box. The `.section` flex layout and gap never reach them, so the button shrinks to fit and the fold's rows sit 0 px apart.
- **Preview.** `#grid-preview` is 11 px muted text. Its contrast is about 5.5:1, so the problem is size. A 648-image spec wraps to four lines and is padded with "x 1 mu".
- **Reachability.** With the fold open the panel is 1504 px tall. Run grid sits at y=1520 at scrollY 0 and at 1512 at scrollY 600, while the gallery pages in. It becomes visible only at the maximum scroll, and keyboard focus does not reveal it.
- **Prototype.** With width 100%, a 13 px totals line, trimmed factors and a JS-measured sticky top, Run grid stays at y=1227-1270.

Screenshots: `verify-grid-3/q-v05-section-648.png`, `q-v06-preview-648.png`, `q-r03-focus.png`, `q-p02-proto-section.png`.

**Why it matters.** The image count, time and disk space decide whether to start a night's run. They are the faintest text in the fold, beside a button a quarter of Generate's width.

**Recommendation.**
1. `#btn-grid { width: 100% }`. Do not add a flex gap on `::details-content`: it adds about 70 px.
2. Put the preview's first line in `<span class="totals">`: block, 13 px, `var(--text)`, weight 600, amber under `.warn`. The text stays in `textContent`.
3. Client-side only, trim factors equal to 1 except seeds and replace " x " with " × ", falling back to `p.summary` when the result is empty.
4. Use the measured sticky top from U1.

Drop the finder's proposed rename of the fold: "comparisons" would be confused with Compare.

**Rule.** `index.html:302` keeps the fold closed by default. `grid.describe`'s ASCII "x" and its 1-factors are pinned by `tests/test_grid.py:359` and `tests/test_api_grid.py:278`, so trim only in the client.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:154` (warn class; "2 would be identical"), `smoke-conditioning.json:66` ("3 images", "LoRA 0, 0.5, 1"), and `smoke-analysis.json:56` and `265`.

*M97 · from grid-16 · adjusted (low → medium only because of reachability, which is U1's problem; on its own this is low).*

#### U56. A first Generate on the gated default checkpoint fails with a raw six-line GatedRepoError, with no link and no steps, and the retry button is 2,368 px away

**medium** · common · effort M · failure-and-recovery · `backend/main.py:98-106`, `backend/jobs.py:35-54`, `web/status.js:210-219`, `web/app.js:189-197`, `web/index.html:17-20`, `416-418`, `web/panel.js:168-194`

**Observed.** A scratch wrapper made `load()` raise a GatedRepoError built the way huggingface_hub 1.29 builds one. In a fresh profile, pressing Generate:

- `#error` [35,1003,296,108], 12 px text, 278 characters: "GatedRepoError: Cannot access gated repo for url …/resolve/main/model_index.json. Access to model … is restricted. You must have access to it and be authenticated to access it. Please log in."
- The viewer still says "Generated images appear here.".
- The topbar shows "load failed", with a 54x37 Load button at x=2403 titled "Load the weights now".
- The panel showed the error at 4115 ms, while the topbar still said "loading weights…" until 6162 ms.
- At 2048@1.25 the error spans y=1003-1111 in a 1040 px viewport.
- After a reload, the last-run banner repeats the same text, 178 px tall.

Recovery: with the gate cleared, Load reached ready 6.7 s later. Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/aq-02-failed.png`, `aw-02-failed.png`.

**Why it matters.** FLUX.1 Krea [dev] is the default checkpoint and is gated behind a click-through licence. This is the likeliest first thing a new user sees, and the fix (accept the terms, run `hf auth login`, retry) is only in the README.

**Recommendation.**
1. Server.
   - In `ensure_loaded`'s except, map the exception by class over its MRO, as `prompt_encoding._reason` already does.
   - For GatedRepoError: "<repo> is gated on Hugging Face. Accept its terms on the model page (FLUX.1 Krea [dev] is click-through), run `uv run hf auth login` once with a read token, then press Retry load.", taking the gate kind from `launch.CHECKPOINTS[*].gate`.
   - Map RepositoryNotFoundError, LocalEntryNotFoundError and OfflineModeIsEnabled the same way.
   - Add `detail_url` and `detail_kind` to ModelStatus, and keep `short_error` in the log.
2. Page.
   - While the state is "error", fill the viewer placeholder with a card: "Couldn't load <name>", the detail, an "Open the model page" link, and a primary "Retry load" button that calls `els.load.onclick`.
   - Label the topbar button "Retry load" in this state.
3. Call `refreshStatus()` in `streamJob`'s done branch.
4. In `announceLastRun`, skip a job that ended at the load with the same detail that is currently shown.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: loading happens only in `main.ensure_loaded`, under its lock. The Load button starts or retries a load without queueing an image. `#error` is role=alert.

**Smokes.** No smoke reaches the error state; that needs a `--fail-load` flag in `fake_server.py`.

*G1.1 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-1 · adjusted (high → medium: once per install, and the text does name the cause).*

#### U57. The load-failure text is re-posted every 3 s, overwriting any other message, and follows the user into the lightbox

**medium** · common · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `web/status.js:219`, `web/state.js:318-329`, `web/analysis.js:91`

**Observed.** With the status in "error":
- "Enter a prompt first." (18 px) was replaced by the 108 px gated text within one poll (t=519 → 1041 ms); the same happened at 125%.
- After a successful Check cleared the slot, the gated text was back at the next sample.
- Opening another checkpoint's image in the lightbox showed the whole gated error in `#lb-error` from the first poll.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/aq-03-lightbox.png`.

**Why it matters.** While a load has failed, every other message lasts at most 3 s. A user browsing old images gets an alarm inside the lightbox.

**Recommendation.**
- In `refreshStatus`, keep `shownLoadDetail`, and call `showError(s.detail, false, { mirror: false })` only when the detail changes. Reset it when the state leaves "error".
- Extend `showError` with that `mirror` option. Messages caused by the user's own action still mirror into the lightbox.
- Better: let U56's viewer card carry the load failure and keep `#error` free.

**Rule.** `showError` unhides before writing and mirrors into `#lb-error`, because the lightbox covers the panel.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:54`: the mirror must stay the default for user-action messages.

*G1.2 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-2 · confirmed.*

#### U58. The per-image delete confirm can't tell apart the images a slip would confuse

**medium** · common · effort S · destructive-actions · `web/gallery.js:222-228`, `web/lightbox.js:99-113`, `web/app.js:240`, `276`

**Observed.** With confirm stubbed and its text logged:
- Four cells of a steps×CFG grid (ids 64, 67, 70 and 72) produce the same 104-character text: "Delete this image (seed 300)? "a teapot on a windowsill, morning light" The file is removed from disk."
- Source 63 (1024²) and pass 78 (1536², refined from 63) produce the same text as each other.
- Fox neighbours differ only by seed 200 against 201.
- The card's ⌦ is titled only "Delete".

**Why it matters.** `gallery.js:222-223` says the confirm exists so that "a slip is caught here". In a sweep it names nothing that tells the images apart, and the PNG is deleted with no undo.

**Recommendation.** Factor `renderLightbox`'s settings line into `settingsLine(rec)` and use it in `confirmDelete`: `Delete image ${id}${fav ? ' (starred)' : ''}?\n\n${settingsLine(rec)}\n"${snippet}"\n\nThe PNG is deleted from disk, not moved to the Recycle Bin.`

**Rule.** Keep the native confirm and the favourite flag. The lightbox's Delete key stays bound only while the lightbox is open.

**Smokes.** None read the confirm text. `smoke-refine.json:55` reads the settings line, which the shared helper must keep word for word.

#### U59. A card delete reflows the page under the pointer: another card's ⌦ lands under the cursor, focus drops to `<body>`, and the viewer is left as a bare 380 px box

**medium** · common · effort M · destructive-actions · `web/gallery.js:244-268`, `134-135`, `web/panel.js:250-255`, `web/style.css:650-660`, `783-796`

**Observed.**
- After one Generate: the viewer is 917 px, the gallery top is at y=1105, and the new card's ⌦ is at [514,1245].
- A real click on that ⌦, with confirm accepting:
  - the viewer drops to 380 px and the gallery top to y=568, a 537 px move;
  - the element under the pointer is now card 163's ⌦, highlighted.
- Deleting a batch tile's card slides the next card into the slot under the pointer.
- From the keyboard (focus on ⌦, activate it), focus ends on `<body>`.
- The viewer is left with no image and no placeholder, while `#cell-note` still says "Finished 1 image".
- On a card, ☆ and ⌦ are 4 px apart.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v3-after-delete-single.png`, `v3-after-delete-batch-tile.png`.

**Why it matters.** In quick triage (click ⌦, Enter, click ⌦) the second click lands on a different image from the one aimed at, and the confirm (U58) is the only check.

**Recommendation.** In `forgetImages`, for a single-image card delete:
- Replace the card's contents with `<div class="tomb">Deleted · seed N</div>`, remove its `data-id`, and add `.card.gone { pointer-events: none; opacity: .35 }`.
- Freeze a viewer tile's size before emptying it, and put the placeholder back when `viewerRecords` becomes empty.
- Move focus to the next card's image (else the previous one) once the DELETE resolves.

**Rule.** `forgetImages` is the one place the page drops deleted ids. Tombstones exist only in the DOM, and Delete unstarred's `resetGallery()` clears them.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:177-181` counts `.card` after the sweep, so either keep tombstones out of `.card` or apply them to single deletes only.

*G2.3 · from gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end-3 · confirmed. It complements U82 (moving ⌦ away from ☆).*

#### U60. Deleting the last loaded image in the lightbox steps back to an image already seen

**medium** · common · effort S · destructive-actions · `web/gallery.js:264-268`, `73`, `web/lightbox.js:132-154`

**Observed.** 192 images, 96 of them loaded (`galleryMore` true).
- With the lightbox at 96/96 (id 103), Delete shows id 104 at 95/95, the image before. Delete again shows 105 at 94/94.
- ArrowRight then shows id 102 at 95/142, the next unseen image.
- In mid-strip, a delete moves forward (20/75 → 20/74).
- At QHD the first boundary is at 96 images, then one every 48.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v2b-lb-after-del-last-loaded.png`.

**Why it matters.** A forward triage pass reaches a page boundary every 48 images. There, the next Delete targets an image the user has already decided to keep.

**Recommendation.** In `forgetImages`:
- If nothing is left and nothing can be paged in, close the lightbox.
- If `lbIndex` is past the end and more can be paged, call `loadGallery().catch(() => {})`, then clamp the index and render, or close.
- Otherwise clamp the index and render.

While the page loads, show "Loading next…" with an empty image source, so that the deleted image is not left on screen with a live Delete button.

**Rule.** `loadGallery` is serialised through `galleryPending`. Keep `stepLightbox`'s guard.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U61. A klein user can't bring in their own photo or sketch, yet klein's Examples offer "Turn this cat into a dog" and the check calls that prompt fine

**medium** · common · effort L (quick fix S) · klein references · `backend/main.py`, `backend/guides.py:198`, `backend/prompt_rules.py:1603-1616`, `web/app.js:20-55`, `web/sizes.js:199-205`, `pyproject.toml:18`

**Observed.**
- **The example.** klein's Examples list "Turn this cat into a dog". Picked with no references: references [], note "(add one with the ❐ button on a gallery image)". Check says "6 words, prose · no warnings · 1 note", and the note is only the word count.
- **No way in for an outside image.** There are no drop or paste handlers in `web/` (`document.ondrop` is null), and no POST route takes a file. python-multipart is already a dependency.
- **The rule.** `prompt_rules._references` returns early when there are fewer than two references.
- **Not reproduced:** the claim that dropping a file navigates the page away (the driver cannot drop a file).

Screenshots: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v03-examples.png`, `v04-edit-example-check.png`.

**Why it matters.** klein is the catalogue's only reference-conditioning checkpoint, and editing an outside photo is its main use. The Examples and the check both suggest editing works, and the render is an unrelated image.

**Recommendation.**
- **(A) Now, S.**
  1. In `_references`, when there are no references, the checkpoint supports them, and the prompt reads as an edit, add a heuristic info finding with no cite. The test is `/^\s*(turn|change|make|replace|remove|put|give)\b.*\b(this|these|the (photo|image|picture)|image \d)\b/i`. The finding reads: "This reads as an edit of an existing picture, but no reference image is attached - it will render from the prompt alone. Add one with the ❐ button on a gallery image."
  2. In `showExamples`, while no reference is set, append a muted " - needs a reference image" to any example the same regex matches. The example's own text stays verbatim.
  3. Add document-level dragover and drop handlers that call `preventDefault` for files, so a stray drop cannot leave the page.
- **(B) Planned, L.** Add `POST /api/images/import` for PNG, JPEG and WebP, with a size cap.
  - **Storage.** It stores a row with `source='import'`, which needs a `_migrate` entry, goes through `_insert_row`, and gets a chunk marker so that reindex keeps it.
  - **Exclusions.** Such rows are left out of rerender, `completed_cells`, the duplicate count and `keep_rates`, and `_model_scope` admits them on every checkpoint.
  - **Page.** A "+ Add image…" button and drop/paste on `#reference-field` call `setReferences([...ids, id])`.
  - The same route would give Refine a way in for outside images.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: "Reference images are gallery ids, never paths", so an import must create a row and hand back an id. A new column needs `_migrate`. A rule finding with no author sentence behind it is heuristic. The authors'-examples test forbids only "warn", so "info" is allowed.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke-conditioning.json:93-110`; the regex must not fire there.

*G3.1 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-1 · adjusted (high → medium).*

#### U62. Reference chips carry no number, though the prompt, the guide and the check all name references as "image 1", "image 2"; and their order can't be changed

**medium** · common (reported every-session; the verifier narrows it to klein sessions with two or more references) · effort M · klein references · `web/sizes.js:199-222`, `web/style.css:522-546`

**Observed.**
- Four 52x52 chips sit at x=35, 93, 151 and 209 in a 296 px list. Two are near-identical gradients.
- Each chip's title, image alt and image title are empty; only the × is named ("Remove image 8").
- Dragging chip 1 onto chip 3, and Alt+ArrowRight, both leave the order at [8,4,10].
- After ×, focus lands on `<body>`.
- Check with three references reports: "the prompt never says what to take from image 3".

Screenshots: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v06-field-4.png`, `v10-check-3refs.png`.

**Why it matters.** On klein, the order of the chips is what "image 1" and "image 2" mean in the prompt. A wrong order takes the jacket from the wrong image, and the result still looks like a success.

**Recommendation.**
1. Add a `<b class="chip-n">` number badge at bottom left. Set alt and title to "Reference image N (gallery #id)", and label the × "Remove reference image N".
2. Make chips draggable, with Alt+ArrowLeft/Right and Delete on the focused ×. Each move splices a copy of `referenceIds` and passes it through `setReferences`, mirroring `tags.js:402-425`.
3. After ×, focus the next chip's ×, else the previous one's, else the `?` badge.
4. Chips at 66 px: 4 × 66 + 18 = 282 px, which fits the 296 px list; 80 px would not.

**Rule.** `setReferences` stays the one writer: it persists, refreshes the preview and sets the stale badge. The tag chips are the precedent for reordering.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:36`, `40` and `100` treat the × as the chip's only button, so keep the badge a `<b>`. Re-run 95-110.

*G3.2 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-2 · adjusted (high → medium).*

#### U63. The contact sheet has no references axis, so a with-and-without-reference comparison collapses to one cell that reads "1 of 3 loaded"

**medium** · common · effort S · klein references · `web/sheet.js:11-31`, `353-366`, `373-375`, `421-427`

**Observed.**
- "a husky in a red scarf" at seed 77, rendered with no reference, with [8], and with [8,10]: the sheet has 3 records, no row or column axis, one cell spanning the full width, and the note "1 of 3 loaded".
- The same prompt and seed at two sizes collapse to "1 of 2", because size is deliberately not an axis.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v22-sheet-refs.png`.

**Why it matters.** "Did the reference help?" is the core klein evaluation. The comparison tool hides two of three images behind a count that suggests they are still loading.

**Recommendation.**
1. Add `{ key: 'ref', label: 'References', of: r => r.reference?.length ? r.reference.map(id => '#' + id).join(' + ') : null }` after `lora` in `SHEET_AXES`. Use null, not "none", to follow the existing convention. An "adapter" axis is optional.
2. In `renderSheet`, count the records whose cell is already taken, and word the note "3 of 5 shown - 2 share a cell with another image and differ in something the sheet does not lay out (size, checkpoint, references…)". This part covers every collapse, not only references.

**Rule.** A null axis value never invents an axis on a mixed search. The listlike and `VARIANT_AXIS` rules hold, and the export follows `sheetLayout`.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:97` and `102` and `smoke-analysis.json:319` assert "N of N loaded" exactly. That text is unchanged when nothing collides, but re-run them.

*G3.7 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-7 · adjusted.*

#### U64. A result says which references made it only as "ref 8+4", inside a 12 px run-on line

**medium** · common · effort M · klein references · `web/lightbox.js:96-113`, `web/generation.js:418`, `web/gallery.js:91-130`

**Observed.**
- After a Generate with references [8,4], the viewer shows only the result.
- The lightbox's line reads "FLUX.2-klein-4B, 1024×1024, 4 steps, CFG 1, no mu, ref 8+4, seed 500, 0.0s", at 12 px in a 280 px column.
- No image id is visible anywhere else on the page.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v12-lightbox-refs.png`.

**Why it matters.** Judging an edit means seeing its inputs beside the output. And reusing the same references with a new prompt forces a Reuse that overwrites the panel.

**Recommendation.**
- When the record has references, add a `.lb-refs` block under the settings line: a "References" caption, then 64 px thumbnails labelled "image 1", "image 2" (matching U62).
- Each thumbnail is a button that opens that record through `GET /api/images/{id}`, with a "deleted" placeholder when the load fails.
- Add a ghost "Use these references" button that calls only `setReferences(rec.reference)`, `aria-disabled` where references are unsupported.
- Keep "ref 8+4" in the settings line until the smoke changes.

**Rule.** References are ids, resolved through `/api/images/{id}/thumb`, never paths.
