#### U65. A tag added at the end of the Refine prompt lands past SDXL's 77-token cut and does nothing, and the dialog never says so

**medium** · common · effort M · refine on an SDXL checkpoint · `web/refine.js:250-254`, `298-352`, `web/index.html:606`, `web/analysis.js:79`, `backend/schemas.py:291`, `backend/main.py:834`

**Observed.** On the Animagine fake, with a 41-tag source (832x1216), ", smirk" was typed at the end of the prompt in the Refine fold.
- The 3-row box now shows the tail "outdoors, night, … absurdres, smirk", which is exactly the part that is never read.
- The preview stays "1 image · strength 0.4 · about 11 of 28 steps run", and nothing in the dialog mentions tokens, CLIP or a cut.
- `/api/prompt/analyze`, given the dialog's request body, returned 200 with the cut and an order finding.
- The real cached CLIPTokenizer counts 132 tokens. The first tag past the cut is number 23, "outstretched arm", so only 22 of 42 tags are read.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint/shots/qhd-a04-typed-smirk.png`.

**Why it matters.** Adding detail tags is what a hires-style pass is for. On every SDXL checkpoint, an addition at the end of a long tag prompt has no effect, and the user finds out only after waiting for the pass.

**Recommendation.**
- **The check.** In `refine.js`, add `refineCheckTimer` and its own `refineCheckEpoch`; not `refineEpoch`, which `previewRefine` increments on every call. Run `checkRefinePrompt()` when the dialog opens, and 400 ms after any input in the prompt or negative. It POSTs the dialog's request body to `/api/prompt/analyze`.
- **Where it shows.** A new `<p class="hint warn" id="refine-encoders">` just above `#refine-preview`, outside the fold, so it is visible with the fold closed. It reads, for example, "CLIP-L/CLIP-G read 22 of 42 tags (75 of 132 tokens); the rest - from "outstretched arm" - is never read."
  - A ghost "Show" button opens the fold and selects from the cut to the end, using `units()`.
  - Rule findings that carry a whole-field suggestion get an Apply, through `replaceBoxText(els.refinePrompt, …)`.
- **Behaviour.** Show "checking…" while a tokenizer loads, and never block Refine. Do not auto-open the fold.

**Rule.** Check works cold and off the gate. `PromptAnalysisRequest` subclasses `GenerateRequest`, so the pass body is accepted as it is. The call never writes `els.prompt`, and the panel's Check stays bound to `collect()`.

**Smokes.** None break: `smoke-refine` runs on FLUX with no cut, and its step 20 expects the fold closed. Add an Animagine step.

*G4.1 · from gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint-1 · adjusted. Also covers G4.3, which was set aside as its duplicate.*

#### U66. The Refine prompt is a plain 3-row box showing 14 of 41 tags, with no chips, autocomplete or validation

**medium** · common · effort M · refine on an SDXL checkpoint · `web/index.html:550`, `602-612`, `web/tags.js:7-30`, `94-117`, `193-232`, `486-505`, `web/app.js:248-251`

**Observed.** A client stub stood in for Danbooru's suggest and validate endpoints, with "smlie" unknown. Fold open:
- `#refine-prompt` is 81 px tall with 207 px of content, so 14 of 41 tags are visible; the same at 2048@1.25.
- Typing ", sm" shows no suggestions, and no suggest or validate request is made.
- Changing "blush" to "smlie" leaves no mark, the preview unchanged, and Refine live.
- The same typo in the panel shows a red "? smlie" chip within about 0.5 s.
- With the fold open, the header line (the source's prompt, unlabelled, clamped to 3 lines) and the box below it disagree.
- Autosizing applied by hand shows the whole prompt; the dialog is then 959 px tall at y=41 and still fits 2048x1040.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint/shots/qhd-a05-typo.png`, `qhd-a02-fold-open.png`, `dpr125-b02-autosized.png`.

**Why it matters.** The panel spends a whole subsystem on catching unknown tags. The same edit in the dialog the user complained about gets none of it, and a typo costs a whole pass, or a whole sweep.

**Recommendation.**
1. Autosize `#refine-prompt` on open, on fold toggle and on input, to `min(scrollHeight + 2, 40vh)`. At 1800 px and wider, consider a 600 px dialog, or U11's two panes.
2. Add `<p class="hint warn" id="refine-tags">` under the box.
   - After a 400 ms pause, POST the box's comma items to `/api/tags/validate` and show only the invalid ones: "Not found in Danbooru: smlie".
   - Do this only when the checkpoint uses a tag vocabulary, and stay silent on failure.
3. Caption the header line "Source prompt:".
4. Optional: autocomplete, reusing `scheduleEditorSuggestions` and `renderEditorSuggestions` with an `onPick` parameter and a menu inside the dialog.

**Rule.** A failed lookup stays advisory. `writePromptTags` writes only `els.prompt`, so the dialog must not use it. The header line is a deliberate reminder of the picture: label it, don't remove it.

**Smokes.** None break: the FLUX smoke has no tag vocabulary, and step 18 is unaffected. Add an Animagine step.

*G4.2 · from gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint-2 · confirmed.*

#### U67. On SDXL a pass sends no preview frames, so the stage shows an unrelated earlier image for the whole pass

**medium** · common · effort M · refine on an SDXL checkpoint · `web/generation.js:377-395`, `414-427`, `web/refine.js:358-374`, `docs/verified.md:176`, `343`

**Observed.** Real SDXL sends no preview frames (verified.md), so the fake's frames were dropped in the page.
- At QHD: a source, then an unrelated "1boy" render (id 9), then Refine on the source.
  - The 2161x917 stage showed image 9 from 258 ms until 1258 ms (step 7/11), and the pass appeared at 2001 ms.
  - Progress was a 54x18 px label at 12 px, in the left column.
  - The tab title never changed.
- At 125% with Versions 2, the stage stayed empty ("Generated images appear here") through step 15/22, and both results arrived at about 4 s.
- On the card, a WAI 1.5x pass takes 15.2 s, and an 8.6 MP pass 46.2 s per image.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint/shots/qhd-a08-pass-running.png`, `dpr125-b03-pass-running.png`.

**Why it matters.** The only sign of work is a 12 px counter about 1,000 px from the image area, while the stage shows an image that is not the job.

**Recommendation.**
- Pass a placeholder through `startJob(path, body, { sheet, panel: false, placeholder: { src: refineSource.url, w, h, label: 'Refining image N → W×H · strength s' } })`, forwarded to `streamJob`.
- At the first progress event, if there is no preview and a placeholder was given, call `clearResults()` and insert a `<figure class="pending">` as `previewEl`. `dropPreview` and `show()` remove it, and a FLUX preview frame replaces it.
- CSS: `aspect-ratio` from w/h, `max-height: min(70vh, 900px)`, opacity .45, a 1 px blur. The caption updates with the step and the time left.
- The root fix, for all SDXL renders, is an SDXL latent map in `latent_preview`, fitted on the card.
- Also call `preventDefault` in `app.js`'s Ctrl+Enter branch.

**Rule.** The placeholder appears at the first progress event, after any queue wait or load, which keeps the rule that results replace the viewer only once the job runs. The preview is never a record, and the dialog's contract is unchanged.

**Smokes.** None break. The fake sends preview frames even when claiming SDXL, so a new step needs the frames dropped, or a `--no-preview` flag.

*G4.4 · from gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint-4 · adjusted: the chip tray showing the panel's tags is documented placement, not a defect.*

#### U68. Reset wipes the whole panel and the grid spec in one click with no undo, and is styled the same as Check

**medium** · occasional · effort S · panel · `web/panel.js:25-42`, `80-120`, `web/index.html:55-56`, `web/style.css:166`, `173`

**Observed.** The panel was set up with a typed prompt, Steps 32, Seed 4471, 16:9 at 1.5 MP, a grid Steps axis of "20-32x4" with the fold open, and a saved refine entry. One real click on Reset (40x19 at x=291) gave:
- prompt empty, steps 28, seed empty, size 1024²;
- grid axis empty and the fold closed;
- the refine store null;
- no note (`#error` stays hidden).

Ctrl+Z restores nothing, and a reload keeps the reset. Reset and Check are both `.ghost.tiny`, 66 px apart. Reset's hover is the plain ghost hover, not the red one destructive buttons get. Screenshot: `verify-panel-1/m37-01-caption-hover.png`.

**Why it matters.** The loss is the prompt plus a grid spec that has to be retyped exactly for Resume to match.

**Recommendation.**
1. Factor `persistPanel`'s builder into `panelState()`.
2. In `resetPanel`, first take a snapshot from the live DOM: `{panel: panelState(), refine: remembered(REFINE_KEY), owned}`. Do not take it from storage, which is debounced by 300 ms and absent in private windows.
3. After resetting, call `showError('Panel reset.', true)` with an inline Undo that stays live for about 20 s. Undo:
   - writes the fields back, calling `own()` for owned keys;
   - re-remembers both keys;
   - runs `syncSize`, `syncCfg`, `syncConditioning`, `syncRuns`, `schedulePreview` and `scheduleTagValidation(0)`;
   - leaves Resume unticked.
4. Give Reset the danger hover: `.field > span .reset:hover { color: var(--danger); border-color: var(--danger) }`.

**Rule.** design-notes "Panel persistence": Reset is the one thing that empties the store, and it sits away from Generate. An in-memory Undo keeps both: nothing survives a reload, and Resume is never stored.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:139-144` expects the store to be null right after Reset and after a reload. An in-memory snapshot keeps both true.

*M37 · from panel-3 · adjusted (snapshot from the DOM, not from storage).*

#### U69. A reshaped size crops part of the source with only a text note, and W/H have no aspect lock

**medium** · occasional · effort M · refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:189-197`, `web/index.html:567-568`, `web/app.js:248-251`

**Observed.**
- **A square size on a 16:9 source.** 1664x928 refined to 1536x1536 shows "2.4 MP — the source cropped to this shape" in muted 11 px text. Under `refine.fitted`'s cover-and-crop, the pass keeps 56% of the source's width. The 72 px thumbnail looks the same either way.
- **Typing one side.** On an 896x1152 source shown at 1344x1728, typing 2000 into W leaves H at 1728. The line reads "3.5 MP — the source cropped to this shape — may run tight".

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-1/a05-harbour-crop.png`, `verify-refine-dialog-1/a08-fox-w2000.png`.

**Why it matters.** Typing one side is the natural way to ask for "2000 wide". It silently reframes the picture, and the crop is discovered only after the pass.

**Recommendation.**
1. Put a number in the size line: "the source cropped to this shape: keeps the middle 56% of its width", using s = max(W/sw, H/sh).
2. Add an aspect lock between W and H, on by default and switchable off. It acts on trusted input or `change` events: H = round16(W × sh / sw), and the reverse.
3. Once U11 shows the source large, draw the kept region on it with the same cover arithmetic, redrawn in `syncRefine`, and dim the rest with a box-shadow inside an `overflow: hidden` wrapper.

**Rule.** design-notes "The source is covered and cropped, never stretched". Keep the phrase "cropped to this shape", and keep reframing possible. The overlay must match `refine.fitted` exactly.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json` steps 32-33 set W and H from a script. Guard the lock with `isTrusted`, or listen for `change`. Step 28 is unaffected.

*M51 · from refine-dialog-4 · confirmed.*

#### U70. Resume needs the run's base seed, no control supplies it, and the error begins "Value error,"

**medium** · occasional · effort S · grid · `web/state.js:305-314`, `web/panel.js:205-223`, `web/generation.js:151-161`, `backend/schemas.py:604-617`

**Observed.** On the fox variable grid with a blank Seed and Resume ticked:
- The preview reads "Value error, resume needs the seed the grid was started from - the Seed box shows it after Run grid, and the oldest image of the run carries it". Run grid shows the same.
- `/api/runs` already returns the base seed, but neither the Runs picker nor the sheet offers to resume.
- The newest image's seed gives "16 images · 8 already done": a different, overlapping grid.
- Reuse on the oldest image replaces the template, and the preview then says "nothing in the prompt says {size}".
- The correct seed gives "0 images … 24 already done".

Screenshot: `verify-grid-2/m56-01-resume-blank.png`.

**Why it matters.** Resume is how a night that died gets finished. A near-miss seed renders a different grid instead of resuming.

**Recommendation.**
1. When the sheet shows exactly one run, add "Resume this run" to `#sheet-controls`. It:
   - fetches `/api/runs`, sets `els.seed` to the run's seed and ticks Resume;
   - opens the fold, runs `previewGrid` and scrolls Run grid into view;
   - if the preview reports 0 cells skipped, says that the panel's grid does not match this run.
2. Add "Resume" to the last-run banner for cancelled or budget-stopped jobs, using the snapshot's own seed.
3. `readableDetail` strips a leading "Value error, ".

**Rule.** Resume keys on settings and needs an explicit seed; it is never persisted, so the button ticks it for this session only.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:129` and `:65` are unaffected.

*M56 · from grid-11 · confirmed.*

#### U71. Keyboard chip editing drops focus to `<body>` after one Alt+Arrow or Delete, and 70 chips make 140 tab stops

**medium** · occasional · effort M · tags · `web/tags.js:119-132`, `150-160`, `365`, `392-400`, `444-458`, `web/index.html:60`, `65`

**Observed.**
- **Alt+ArrowLeft** on focused chip 2 moves the tag, and 50 ms later focus is on `<body>`. A second press does nothing.
- **Delete** on a chip also leaves focus on `<body>`.
- **Enter, then Escape** refocuses the chip correctly (`closeTagEditor`).
- **Tab stops.** 11 chips are 22 tab stops, because each × is one too.
- **Screen readers.**
  - The aria-label is "1girl. Enter edits it; drag to reorder; Delete removes it."
  - The tray has `role=status`.
  - The prompt's `aria-describedby` includes the tray.

**Why it matters.** The documented keyboard equivalent of drag works for one step only.

**Recommendation.**
1. Add a top-level `tagFocusIndex`, set by `movePromptTag` and `deletePromptTag` before `writePromptTags`, and applied at the end of `renderTagValidation`. That function renders twice, so clear the index on focusout outside the tray.
2. Use a roving tabindex. Left/Right/Home/End move focus between chips; Alt+Left/Right move the tag.
3. Give `.tag-remove` a tabindex of −1.
4. Set the chip's aria-label to "<tag>, <known/unknown/unchecked>. Enter edits, Alt+Left/Right moves, Delete removes."
5. Drop `role=status` from the tray, and add a hidden polite summary ("11 known, 1 unknown") instead.
6. Remove `tag-validation` from the prompt's `aria-describedby`.

**Rule.** design-notes "Animagine XL 4.0": Alt+Left/Right is the keyboard equivalent of drag. Mutations still go through `writePromptTags`. `placeTagValidation` preserves focus when the tray moves at 901 px.

**Smokes.** None break. Add steps to `smoke-animagine`: Alt+Left twice, and Delete keeping focus.

*M66 · from tags-7 · confirmed.*

#### U72. A sheet of prompt iterations has truncated, duplicate column labels in alphabetical order

**medium** · occasional · effort M · walkthrough · `web/sheet.js:86-87`, `98-129`, `132-150`, `391-402`

**Observed.**
- **Six random-seed iterations**, searched "fisherman", then Sheet. The column heads read:
  - "Prompt 35mm photo, portrait of an old fisherman, d…";
  - "Prompt portrait of an old fisherman, deeply weathe…", twice;
  - "… weathered fac…", three times.

  The order is 6, 4, 5, 3, 8, 7: alphabetical, which puts the newest first.
- **22 iterations** give 23 columns with only 3 distinct labels. The heads are 11 px, 96 px wide, with an empty title.
- **Fixed-seed iterations.** Columns 1 and 2 both read "Prompt red tram crossing a bridge in Lisbon at gol…". They differ only by a trailing ", film grain", in about 540 px columns clipped at 44 characters.
- **Random-seed layout.** Iterations with random seeds form a mostly empty diagonal, or the seed gets pinned and the sheet shows "1 of 24 loaded".

Screenshots: `verify-walkthrough-1/shots/m68-01-sheet.png`, `m68-05-fixedseed-sheet.png`, `m68-03-22runs.png`.

**Why it matters.** Successive edits are the commonest comparison, and the sheet cannot say which column is which.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `promptLabels`, when the labels are not distinct or exceed `LABEL_CHARS`, fall back to a word-level LCS diff against the oldest prompt ("+35mm photo,", "−dawn +dusk").
2. `sheetValues` orders the prompt axis like variables, oldest first.
3. Set each head's title to the full prompt.
4. Don't clip the on-screen label where it can wrap. Keep `clipText` for the export.
5. Separately (S): when a pinned axis is a one-per-image seed, add a sheet note pointing to Compare on the same seeds.

**Rule.** design-notes "Prompt labels on the sheet" (keep only what varies; pins address values by position) is refined, not changed. CLAUDE.md lists variables by first appearance.

**Smokes.** Re-run `smoke-comparison.json:20-25` and smoke.json's sheet section.

*M68 · from walkthrough-6 · confirmed.*

#### U73. The topbar stays green, with the model name, the whole time the server is dead

**medium** · occasional · effort M · failure-and-recovery · `web/status.js:118-135`, `210-218`, `230`, `backend/main.py:249-285`, `backend/schemas.py:914-944`

**Observed.** During a Runs-4 job the server was hard-killed at t=12.3 s and restarted 30 s later.
- Every sample from 12.8 s to about 46.6 s read the ready dot and "FLUX.1-Krea-dev".
- The first "not loaded" came at 47.2 s, with nothing saying the server had restarted.
- While a job is being watched, the panel does show "reconnecting… (1)" from 12.8 s.
- `status.js:230` swallows every failed poll.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/blong-03-lightbox-down.png`, `blong-04-late.png`.

**Why it matters.** The topbar is the page's only health indicator, and on an idle page it says the opposite of the truth. That includes the common case of restarting the server to change checkpoints.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `refreshStatus`'s catch, on a network rejection (the fetch `TypeError`, or `err.offline` from U135's wrapper):
   - set the error dot and the text "server not answering — retrying";
   - hide Load;
   - make Generate, Run grid, Refine and Unload `aria-disabled`, titled "The server is not answering".
2. Keep the 3 s poll and the `statusPending` guard. Count only fetch rejections, never a slow poll.
3. Add `started_at` to `ModelStatus`, set once at import. On the first good poll after a failure, or when `started_at` changes, say: "The server restarted at HH:MM — the model is not loaded, and any job that was running ended with it."

**Rule.** `/api/status` stays cheap. Disabled controls use `aria-disabled`, not `disabled`.

**Smokes.** None.

*G1.3 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-3 · adjusted (high → medium). Implement it together with U135.*

#### U74. A grid's skipped cells are invisible while it runs, counted but not named at the end, and Run grid again re-renders everything

**medium** · occasional · effort M · failure-and-recovery · `web/generation.js:95-109`, `157-161`, `445-474`, `backend/jobs.py:130-162`, `404-409`, `446-460`, `532-541`, `backend/main.py:1162-1193`

**Observed.** A 12-cell grid where the 5th call raised.
- The cell line went from "cell 4/12" to "cell 6/12" with no mark.
- At the end: "Finished 11 images in 11s.", then in red "1 cell(s) failed and were skipped; last: RuntimeError: cell 5 blew up".
- Resume [39,1419] and Run grid [35,1487] are both below the 1305 px fold, and below 1040 at 125%.
- The preview never mentions the cells already rendered. Run grid again without Resume rendered all 12 (the gallery went from 13 to 25).
- With Resume ticked the preview says "1 image · … · 11 already done".

Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/c-02-grid1-done.png`, `c-03-resume-preview.png`.

**Why it matters.** One skipped cell leaves a hole in the sheet, and the obvious retry re-renders the whole night.

**Recommendation.**
1. `streamJob` keeps a skipped count from status and done snapshots; progress events don't carry failures. `showCell` appends " · N skipped" in `--danger`.
2. Server: add `failed_cells` ([{label, seed, error}]) to the snapshot, so the end reads "1 of 12 cells was skipped: 3 steps, seed …656635 (RuntimeError: …)".
3. For an `/api/grid` job with failures, show a "Retry the N skipped cell(s)" button. It re-submits the job's own payload (kept by job id in `startJob`) with `resume: true` and the base seed, not whatever the panel now holds.
4. `preview_grid`, with Resume off and an explicit seed, adds a warning line: "11 of these are already in the gallery — tick Resume to skip them".

**Rule.** design-notes "A cell's failure is the cell's" and "Resume keys on settings". No new job status.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:154` and `:157`: append to the text, don't replace it. `tests/test_api_jobs.py:176` changes if the wording does.

*G1.7 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-7 · adjusted.*

#### U75. A grid stopped by three failures shows only the last exception, and the next day's banner reads "6/12 cells" for 3 images

**medium** · occasional · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `backend/jobs.py:396-420`, `532-541`, `web/generation.js:453-462`, `web/panel.js:178-189`

**Observed.** Calls 4-6 raised.
- The cell line went from "cell 3/12" to empty, with the error "RuntimeError: cell 30 blew up".
- No Finished line appeared, and the preview still offers "12 images".
- `/api/jobs` reports status error, 3 failures, 3 images, run 6 of 12, and `stopped_by` null.
- After a reload the banner reads "Last run failed at 4 Oct, 04:45 pm: 3 images (6/12 cells). RuntimeError: …". At 125% its Show run button is at y=1056, below the 1040 px fold.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/c-04-grid2-stopped.png`, `c-05-lastrun.png`.

**Why it matters.** A grid that stopped a third of the way through looks like one bad cell. Half the night can be rendered with one tick, and nothing says so.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `_run`'s except, set `stopped_by = 'failures'` when there were failures. Word the error "Stopped at cell 6 of 12 after 3 cells failed; 3 images kept. Last: <short_error>", which keeps the last exception.
2. In `streamJob`'s error branch, offer U74's retry, labelled "Resume the remaining N cells".
3. `announceLastRun` says "stopped at cell X of Y" for error or cancelled jobs with more than one cell, and "never started" when the run count is 0.

**Rule.** Three failures in a row, or more than a third of the grid, ends the job. Every end goes through `_finish`, and a stop is described by `stopped_by`, not by a fourth status.

**Smokes.** `tests/test_api_grid.py:820` ("blew up" is kept) and `:144` are unaffected.

*G1.8 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-8 · adjusted.*

#### U76. A deleted image comes back as a broken viewer tile after a reload during its run, and deleting it again says "unknown image"

**medium** · occasional · effort M · destructive-actions · `web/generation.js:430`, `449`, `backend/jobs.py:151`, `164`, `597`, `backend/main.py:1368-1372`, `web/gallery.js:230-239`

**Observed.** A 5-run job at 10 steps. Image 209 was deleted from the lightbox, which left the viewer empty.
- After a reload, `resumeActiveJob` reattached. The viewer showed [209 with naturalWidth 0, 210, 211, preview], and `viewerRecords` was [209, 210, 211].
- After the job finished, 209 was still broken, and the line read "Finished 5 images in 16s." though only 4 remained.
- Clicking the ghost tile opens "1/5" on a broken image. Delete asks again, then shows "unknown image" in `#error` and `#lb-error`, and the tile stays.
- `/api/jobs` reports 5 images with one deleted.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v4-reattached.png`, `v4-done.png`, `v4-ghost-delete.png`.

**Why it matters.** A delete appears to undo itself, and the retry looks like a failure.

**Recommendation.**
1. Server: each `Job` keeps its produced count (`image_count`, unchanged) and a set of deleted ids. `jobs.forget_images(ids)` is called from `main.delete` and `delete_unstarred` after the commit. `snapshot(images=True)` filters out deleted records and adds `deleted_count`.
2. Client: in `deleteImage`'s catch, on "unknown image", call `forgetImages([id])` and show `showError('Already deleted.', true)`.
3. Viewer tiles get an `onerror` tombstone (as in U59).
4. The finish line reads "5 images, 1 since deleted".

**Rule.** Replaying the snapshot on reattach is deliberate. `image_count` means how much a night produced, and `tests/test_api_grid.py:102`, `248` and `817` assert it, so it stays.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:157` is unchanged.

*G2.4 · from gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end-4 · adjusted.*

#### U77. A deleted image stays a reference chip: Reuse restores a broken chip with no note, and Generate fails with "no gallery image with id 4"

**medium** · occasional · effort S · klein references · `web/gallery.js:224-228`, `244-269`, `356-359`, `367-397`, `404-410`, `web/sizes.js:207-210`

**Observed.**
- **Case A, Reuse.** Record 11 was made with references [8,4], then image 4 was deleted.
  - On a fresh page, Reuse of 11 restores ids [8,4]; chip 4 has naturalWidth 0, and there is no note.
  - Generate then fails with "no gallery image with id 4".
  - In the session that did the delete, chip 4 still shows its cached thumbnail.
- **Case B, delete a current reference.** Image 5 was added as a reference, then deleted with its ⌦. The confirm doesn't mention it, and afterwards the references are still [5], with the cached 384 px chip.

Refine already handles the same situation: "Image 14, the source …, is no longer in the gallery". Screenshots: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v20-reuse-deleted-fresh.png`, `v14-panel-dead-ref.png`, `v15-chip-of-deleted.png`.

**Why it matters.** A reference that looks present leads to a refused submit, or a refused night, with an error the user cannot map to a chip.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `forgetImages`, if a deleted id is in `referenceIds`, call `setReferences` with the rest and show "Image N was a reference image; it is no longer in the references."
2. In `confirmDelete`, add "It is reference image N in the panel."
3. `reuseIntoPanel` stays synchronous, then asks `GET /api/images/{id}` for each restored reference. It drops only those answering "unknown image", with a note in the style of `reusePass`.
4. A chip's image `onerror` adds class `gone` and a "deleted" label.

**Rule.** `forgetImages` is the one place deleted ids are dropped, and `setReferences` is the one writer. `storage.reference_paths` still refuses a missing reference at submit.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:110-111` expects `referenceIds.length === 1` synchronously after Reuse.

*G3.5 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-5 · confirmed.*

#### U78. Reusing a record on another checkpoint silently drops its references and its adapter

**medium** · occasional · effort S · klein references · `web/gallery.js:356-362`, `379-393`, `web/status.js:252-258`

**Observed.**
- **Another checkpoint.** A Krea-2-Turbo fake shared the klein gallery. With All models ticked, ⚠ Reuse on klein record 14 (references [8,10]) gave:
  - references [], with the note "(this checkpoint takes none)";
  - `#error`: "These settings were made on FLUX.2-klein-4B; the server is running Krea-2-Turbo, whose steps, CFG and mu differ. Prompt, size and seed carry over; check the steps, CFG and mu boxes before generating." Nothing about references.
  - The ⚠ button's title names only the two checkpoints.
- **The same checkpoint.** With the adapter file removed, Reuse of record 16 (demo-style at 0.5) left the LoRA empty and no message at all.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-klein-references-and-lora/v40-krea-reuse-klein-refs.png`.

**Why it matters.** The user believes they are re-making the image, and gets one made without any of its conditioning.

**Recommendation.**
1. `reuseIntoPanel` builds notes from what `fillPanel` could not carry:
   - "Its 2 reference images (gallery 8, 10) are not used: Krea-2-Turbo takes none, so this renders from the prompt alone."
   - "Its adapter demo-style.safetensors @ 0.5 is not applied here - <reason>". The reason is `/api/loras`' detail for an adapter that doesn't fit, else "the file is no longer in the adapter directory".
2. Extend the ⚠ button's title to say that references and adapter will not carry over.

**Rule.** The drop itself is deliberate: the request would be refused at submit. Reuse's message is "a note, not a failure".

**Smokes.** `smoke-flux.json:42`: appending keeps its substrings.

*G3.6 · from gap-klein-references-and-lora-6 · confirmed.*

#### U79. Gallery cards show no prompt, seed, size or run, and nothing separates runs or days

**low** · every-session · effort M · gallery, walkthrough · `web/panel.js:285`, `web/gallery.js:54-58`, `91`, `web/lightbox.js:114`

**Observed.**
- **Card text.** The card image's title is empty; alt and aria-label carry the prompt. The card's only text is the six glyphs.
- **Pass and source.** A 2048 pass card looks the same as its 1024 source.
- **No separation.** A 48-image run and 2-image batches run together across the 12 columns.
- **The lightbox counter** uses the loaded list. It read "41/60", which was correct; the finder's "6/96 of 144" follows from the code but was not reproduced.

The fake's gradient images overstate the problem, since real thumbnails show the picture. Screenshots: `verify-gallery-1/shots/a02-after-pass.png`, `b00-full.png`.

**Why it matters.** Finding a past image is quick only if the user knows a search term. Otherwise, every "which one was that?" costs a lightbox round trip.

**Recommendation.**
1. `thumb()` sets the image title to "W×H · seed N", plus "· refine of M @ s" for a pass, then the prompt on a new line.
2. Add the refined badge (U22).
3. Insert a day divider, a full-width `<div class="gallery-break">`, in `loadGallery`'s append loop whenever the local day changes. `forgetImages` removes dividers left empty.
4. The lightbox counter shows "i/total", or appends "+" while more pages remain.

Do not put a two-line caption under every card: that cuts 12 columns to 9. Use a hover overlay if a caption is wanted.

**Rule.** Paging by id and `forgetImages` are unaffected, because dividers carry no `data-id`.

**Smokes.** None. `smoke-paging` counts `.card`.

#### U83. After Refine, feedback moves to the panel, a queued pass is not named, and a refusal at submit loses the dialog

**low** · every-session · effort M · refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:358-374`, `web/generation.js:44-69`, `124-171`

**Observed.**
- **Queued behind a batch.** Pressing Refine while a batch ran ("Refine (queue)") closed the dialog. The only trace was "1 more job queued behind this one." at 11 px, about 1,340 px to the left of the button.
- **Not queued.** When nothing is queued, the pass's live preview does appear in the stage.
- **The result.** The finished pass has an empty `#viewer-note`, and the banner says only "1 image".
- **A refusal at submit.** With the source deleted while the dialog was open, the dialog closed and "no gallery image with id 11" appeared in the panel at 12 px. The prompt edit was lost.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-1/c-02-after-refine-click.png`, `d-01-refine-running.png`, `c-04-race.png`.

**Why it matters.** It is unclear whether the click took, what was queued, and where the result came from. A refusal costs the edits made in the dialog.

**Recommendation.**
1. `runRefine` closes the dialog only once the POST answers: split `startJob` into submit and watch, or give it an `onAccepted` callback. On a refusal, call `refineFailed` and keep the dialog open.
2. Record a label for each queued job ("refine of image 10"), so `syncQueueNote` can name it.
3. Set `#viewer-note` to "Refined from image 10 at 0.5 · 1344×1728", with a button that opens the source.

**Rule.** `refine.js:354-357`: a pass queues like Generate, shows progress in the panel and its images in the strip. With `panel: false` it never writes the Seed box. The server refuses a deleted source by name; with this change, that happens inside the dialog.

**Smokes.** `smoke-refine.json` step 127 should expect the dialog open and the text in `els.refineError`.

#### U85. Every valid chip looks the same: there is no category mark and no duplicate mark

**low** · every-session · effort S · tags · `web/tags.js:374-389`, `web/style.css:263-277`

- The category appears only in each chip's title ("Danbooru character tag · 16.0k posts").
- Check reports "Repeated: green hair" and the out-of-order tag.

Screenshot: `verify-tags-1/d01-chips.png`.

**Why it matters.** The chips are a tool for reordering, and they show nothing about the property the reordering is for.

**Recommendation.**
- **Category colour.** A 3 px coloured left border by `result.category` (character, copyright, artist, general, meta, and "guide" for model tags), keeping the ✓, the light text and the charcoal fill. Unknown and unchecked chips get no colour.
- **Legend.** As `::after` text or a title, not an element.
- **Order slots.** Only if the server supplies them, as a `slot` field in `TagValidationResult` computed from `analysis.order`.
- **Duplicates.** Later copies with the same canonical tag get `.duplicate`: an amber dotted outline, titled "repeated: adds nothing (heuristic)".

**Rule.** design-notes "Animagine XL 4.0": light text on charcoal, with green only on the mark and outline. CLAUDE.md: "order placed from the guide's own groups, never guessed", and the page has no reorder rule of its own. A client-side slot map would be wrong on Kohaku, Illustrious and WAI.

**Smokes.** `smoke-animagine` 11 is unaffected (the font, colour and background are kept). A legend element would break the steps that iterate the tray's children.

#### U88. "Run" means three different things, and Batch and Runs have no help badge

**low** · every-session · effort S · copy-a11y · `web/index.html:140`, `254-260`, `427-429`, `web/sizes.js:233-244`, `web/generation.js:407-409`, `web/panel.js:183-188`, `205-222`

*M73 · from copy-a11y-7 · adjusted (medium → low).*

#### U89. Placeholders, the Generate label and a stale prompt check fall below WCAG contrast

**low** · every-session · effort S · copy-a11y · `web/style.css:7-8`, `103-114`, `323-333`, `588-599`

**Observed.**
- **Placeholders.** `rgb(117,117,117)` on `rgb(26,26,33)` is 3.76:1, and the placeholders carry state ("random", "off", "min").
- **Generate.** White on `#7c5cff` at 14 px/600 is 4.35:1.
- **A stale check.** `.analysis-body` at opacity .55 puts muted text at 2.52:1, `#b2b2c0` at 3.49:1, body text at 5.26:1 and amber at 4.09:1.

Screenshots: `verify-copy-a11y-1/m74-check-stale.png`, `m74-seed-row.png`.

**Why it matters.** Users read "random" and "off" to know what a blank box will do, and the stale check is meant to stay readable as the last answer.

**Recommendation.**
- **Placeholders.** `::placeholder { color: var(--muted); opacity: 1 }` gives 5.16:1.
- **Generate.** A `.primary` background of `#6d4ff2` gives 5.18:1; leave `--accent` unchanged elsewhere.
- **Stale check.** `.prompt-analysis.stale .analysis-body { opacity: .8; --muted: #a2a2b0 }` gives muted text 5.11, body 9.94 and amber 7.41, and still visibly dims the panel.

**Rule.** A stale check is "dimmed", with Use and Apply dead; the badge and the dead buttons carry that signal. The `aria-disabled` Generate at .5 is exempt.

**Smokes.** None.

*M74 · from copy-a11y-11 · adjusted (medium → low).*

#### U90. The gallery search box is capped at 240 px with a truncated placeholder, and the Runs picker forgets which run is on screen

**low** · every-session · effort S · gallery, copy-a11y · `web/style.css:703-708`, `web/app.js:220`, `web/index.html:425-430`, `web/panel.js:205-223`

**Observed.**
- **The search box.** It is 240x41 at x=1848. The placeholder needs 222 px against 218 available, and renders as "Search prompts, or seed:42 cfg:0.".
- **Wasted space.** The gap before it is 1089 px at QHD and 577 px at 125%.
- **Help.** The `?` badge is 14x14, and its tip 240x337 at 11 px.
- **The Runs picker.** After picking a run, the search reads "run:949bbfa5c2c0" and the select is back to "Runs…".
- **Refine passes.** A pass appears in Runs with the same text as its grid.

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-2/shots/q-h01-head.png`, `q-h04-after-run-pick.png`.

**Why it matters.** Search is the main way to find a past image. A truncated hint invites a query that does not parse, and a hex id is the only label of the current view.

**Recommendation.**
1. `@media (min-width: 2000px) { .gallery-head input[type=search] { max-width: 480px } }`.
2. Sync `#run-list` from the search: in `resetGallery` and at the end of `loadRuns`, select the option matching `^run:<id>$`, else the empty option. Drop `app.js:220`'s reset.

A refine label in Runs would need a backend field; drop it otherwise. Leave the `?` at 14 px unless every badge grows.

**Rule.** `style.css:703-705`: the box starts at 140 px so the bar stays on one row on a laptop. Picking a run searches `run:<id>` and opens the sheet.

**Smokes.** None expected; re-run smoke.json.

*M75 · from gallery-14, copy-a11y-18 · adjusted.*

#### U91. Every small ghost button turns danger-red on hover, like Delete unstarred

**low** · every-session · effort S · visual, grid · `web/style.css:611-613`, `644`, `772`, `913`, `web/index.html:169`, `277-284`, `455-464`, `web/sheet.js:318`

**Observed.**
- Hovering Sheet (active or not), Delete unstarred or Orientation all give `rgb(255,92,114)`.
- On the active Sheet button, the red hover overrides the accent colour.
- The `.ghost.small` buttons are Orientation, Pause, Cancel, Cancel queued, Show run, the banner's dismiss, Re-render, Sheet, Delete unstarred and Export PNG.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v12-sheet-active-hover.png`, `v13-orientation-hover.png`.

**Why it matters.** Red on view toggles teaches the user to ignore it, so it stops working where it matters.

**Recommendation.**
1. Delete `style.css:613`.
2. Add `.ghost.danger:hover:not(:disabled):not([aria-disabled="true"]) { color: var(--danger); border-color: var(--danger) }` and put class `danger` on `#btn-cancel`, `#btn-cancel-queued` and `#btn-delete-unstarred`. `#lb-delete` has its own red hover.
3. Remove `:644`.
4. Add `.ghost:disabled:hover, .ghost[aria-disabled="true"]:hover { color: var(--muted); border-color: var(--line) }`.

A neutral replacement rule at the old specificity would still override the active accent.

**Rule.** None.

**Smokes.** None.

*M76 · from visual-9, grid-14 · confirmed.*

#### U92. Two caption systems and many one-off greys and sizes, and the sampling row has no caption of its own

**low** · every-session · effort S · visual, copy-a11y · `web/style.css:97`, `130`, `342-345`, `421-425`, `438-441`, `489-499`, `588-599`, `web/index.html:190`, `410`

**Observed.**
- **Hard-coded values.** After `:root`, there are 65 hard-coded hex values (32 distinct; `#fbbf24` appears 15 times), 14 distinct gap lengths, radii from 3 to 10 px plus 50% and 999px, and 11 control heights from 15 to 43 px.
- **Two caption styles.** `.field > span` is 12 px/600 uppercase, while `.sub i` is 11 px sentence case.
- **Sampling.** The Steps row sits under SIZE and its "1.0 MP" hint, with no caption of its own.
- **Run grid.** `#btn-grid` is 77x43 (U55).
- **Contrast.** White on `#7c5cff` is 4.35:1.

Screenshots: `verify-visual-1/shots/v02-panel.png`, `v23-panel-grid-open.png`.

**Why it matters.** The panel looks assembled rather than designed, and its grouping reads wrong when scanned.

**Recommendation.**
1. Add a "Sampling" caption above Steps/CFG/Mu/True CFG (`index.html:190`). The controls keep their sentence-case labels.
2. `.analysis-source, .analysis-stale, .critic-kind { text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; font-size: 11px }`.
3. `#btn-grid { width: 100% }`.
4. `.primary { background: #6a4cf5 }`, which gives 5.3:1.

Tokens are optional tidying; the plan's step 5 introduces them.

**Rule.** Keep `.field em` at `#a2a2b0` and the amber stale badge. No smoke reads `innerText`, so `text-transform` is safe.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U93. Fixed explanations outweigh the check's findings, and the Order "Not checked" note refers to the app in the third person

**low** · every-session · effort S · analysis, copy-a11y · `web/analysis.js:454-466`, `web/index.html:133`, `138`, `140`

**Observed.**
- **FLUX word counts.** The panel has 306 words, of which the findings are 110. Order has 39, 36 of them in the note "Not checked: Check can only place a tag checkpoint's comma tags in its order…". Compare has 94, 52 of them fixed text.
- **Animagine.** 251 words, of which the findings are 58.
- **copy-a11y-17's proposed wording is false.** A comma-separated tag prompt on FLUX returns `order_checked` False, and prose on Animagine returns True.

Screenshots: `verify-analysis-2/q-01-check.png`, `a-01-check.png`.

**Why it matters.** What changes from one prompt to the next is buried under text that never changes.

**Recommendation.**
1. `analysis.js:457-460`: "Not checked: this app checks word order only in a tag checkpoint's comma-separated tags. Ask the critic, or test a reordered version under Compare on your own seeds." That is 28 words, with "Test…" when there is no critic.
2. Move `index.html:140`'s mechanics line into a `?` tip after the Batch select.
3. Keep the 4-vs-8 seeds caveat visible.
4. Leave the encoder role line and the order finding's text alone: the critic's restatement filter and the tests rely on them.

**Rule.** The "Not checked" prefix stays. The page never says the authors give no order. The seed caveat is visible on purpose (design notes).

**Smokes.** `smoke-analysis.json:36` and `:142`. `:124` still passes. `smoke-animagine.json:99` and `:104` pass if the server message is left alone.

#### U96. A fixed 160 px card minimum gives 12 small cards per row at QHD, smaller than at 1400 px

**low** · every-session · effort S · gallery · `web/style.css:716-720`, `782`, `backend/storage.py:38-41`

**Observed.**

| Viewport | Card size | Cards per row |
| --- | --- | --- |
| QHD | 170.9 px | 12 |
| 2048@1.25 | 174.3 px | 9 |
| 1400x900 | 192 px | 5 |
| QHD, simulated `minmax(220px)` | 231 px | 9 (the tools fit) |
| 2048@1.25, simulated `minmax(220px)` | 227 px | 7 |

300 px cards at 125% would need 402 device px, more than `THUMB_SIDE` (384). A 16:9 thumbnail's short side is only 216 px. Screenshots: `verify-gallery-3/q-gallery.png`, `w125-gallery.png`.

**Why it matters.** A 171 px square on a 27-inch panel is too small to judge, and it is what makes the tool row overflow.

**Recommendation.**
- `.grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(var(--card-min, 160px), 1fr)) }` with `--card-min: 220px` at 2000 px and wider.
- **Optional size toggle.** An S/M toggle, hidden below 2000 px (the gallery head is full at 1400), stored under `local-diffusion.gallery.v1`.
- **Optional L size.** Only together with a `THUMB_SIDE` of 512; existing thumbnails stay at 384.
- Update the comment at `storage.py:38`.

**Rule.** App keys use the `local-diffusion.*` prefix and are accessed inside try/catch.

**Smokes.** None at 1400 px. `smoke-paging`'s 48-card counts are unaffected.

*M86 · from gallery-13 · adjusted.*

#### U97. The scale buttons and the outcome summary are the least prominent things in the Refine dialog

**low** · every-session · effort S · refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:180-187`, `web/style.css:549-557`, `472`, `web/index.html:565-572`, `622`

**Observed.**
- **The scale buttons** are 31-46x27 at 11 px, and their output sizes are only in titles.
- **W and H** are 207x41 at 14 px.
- **The preview** is [1069,841,422,17] at 11 px muted, styled the same as the seed hint. Some text elsewhere on the page is 10 px.
- **Injected CSS** adding the sizes with `::after` and a 13 px preview gave four 101x47 buttons and 23 px of extra height, with the buttons' text unchanged.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-2/shots/x-qb-closed.png`, `x-qf-closed.png`.

**Why it matters.** The visual weight is inverted. The boxes most users never touch dominate the factor choice and the summary of what will run.

**Recommendation.**
1. In `syncRefine`, set `button.dataset.size`.
2. `.refine-dialog .presets button { flex: 1; font-size: 13px }`, with `::after { content: attr(data-size); display: block; font-size: 10px; color: var(--muted) }`.
3. `#refine-preview` at 13 px in `var(--text)`, amber under `.warn`. Keep it above the actions, not inside them.
4. Optionally, a 540 px dialog at 2000 px and wider.

**low** · every-session · effort S · copy-a11y · `web/index.html` (every `.help`)

**Observed.**
- All 20 badges have aria-label "Help", role note and tabindex 0.

- 18 of the tips are already their input's `aria-describedby`.
- Tabbing from the prompt to Generate passes seven "Help" stops.

Screenshot: `verify-copy-a11y-2/m106-panel-steps-tip.png`.

**Why it matters.** A screen reader announces "Help, note" seven times before reaching Generate.

**Recommendation.**
- Give each badge a static aria-label, "<caption> help", plus an `aria-describedby` pointing at its tip.
- Prefix the grid's duplicate captions, for example "Steps axis help". The dialog's become "Refine size help" and "Refine strength help".
- Keep the role, the tabindex, and the badge's position outside the label.

**Rule.** `index.html:183-189` keeps the badge outside the label, so that a tap does not forward focus to the input.

#### U99. Escape, Cancel or ✕ on the Refine dialog throws away a typed sweep, size and prompt edits

**low** · common · effort S · refine-flow, refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:117-128`, `152`, `371`, `399-404`, `web/app.js:246-247`

**Observed.** In the dialog: Strength set to the sweep "0.05,0.1,0.15", scale 1.25x (1120x1440), and ", sharp detail" added to the prompt. Escape, then reopen the same image: Strength is back to "0.1", the size to 1344x1728, the prompt to the original, and the fold is closed. A click on the backdrop does not close the dialog. Screenshot: `verify-refine-dialog-1/b-06-edited.png`.

**Why it matters.** The dialog shows the source at 72 px (U11), so "let me look at the image first" is a natural move, and it costs the whole setup.

**Recommendation.**
- Keep an in-memory `refineDraft = {id, values}`.
- **Save.** `refineClosed` saves the draft when the close was not a run and some value differs from what `openRefine` filled in.
- **Restore.** Reopening the same source, other than through Reuse, restores the draft and shows "Your unsent settings for image N · Start over" in the fold note.
- **Clear.** Clear the draft on a run, on `resetPanel`, on a checkpoint change, and when another source is opened.

Land this after U11.

**Rule.**
- A sweep is never remembered. The draft is kept in memory only, per source, and is never written to `local-diffusion.refine.v1`.
- CLAUDE.md's "fills the dialog from the record" gains "unless an unsent draft for the same image is restored, which it says".

**Smokes.** None, provided the draft is saved only when values differ and is cleared on a run and on `resetPanel`.

*M28 · from refine-flow-5, refine-dialog-11 · adjusted (medium → low).*

#### U100. On the default checkpoint the negative prompt is greyed, and the True CFG box that enables it is 320 px below

**low** · common · effort S · panel · `web/sizes.js:161-181`, `web/index.html:155-158`, `216-223`

**Observed.**
- On FLUX the negative box is disabled, with the note "(needs True CFG > 1 — ~2× slower)" wrapping to two lines.
- True CFG sits at y=620, while the negative box is at y=300.
- A real click on the greyed box does nothing: only `pointerdown` fires. Typing 1.5 into True CFG enables it.
- On Turbo the note reads "(needs CFG > 0)".
- The finder's proposed button inside the note would take over the textarea's label (`label.control` becomes the button).

Screenshots: `verify-panel-2/shots/v1-panel.png`, `v5-turbo-panel.png`.

**Why it matters.** The negative prompt is one of the first things a user tries, and on the default checkpoint it looks broken. The fix is learned once.

**Recommendation.**
- Turn `#negative-field` into a div with `<label for="negative">`, as the Prompt field already does.
- While the box is off and a negative is supported, add a ghost tiny button after `#neg-note`, "Set True CFG" or "Set CFG". It focuses and selects the deciding box, and writes nothing.
- Optionally, set `pointer-events: none` on the greyed box and let a click on the field do the same.
- Do not prefill a value: 1.5 would be wrong on Qwen-Image and on schnell.

**Rule.**
- design-notes "True CFG is its own field".
- `collect()` sends no negative while the box is disabled.
- The client invents no values.

**Smokes.** None break if the note keeps its prefix: smoke-flux:29, smoke.json:148 and smoke-animagine:79.

*M38 · from panel-8 · adjusted (medium → low).*

#### U101. On a checkpoint with no refine pass, the card's ⤢ looks live and its refusal lands in the panel

**low** · common · effort S · refine-flow · `web/gallery.js:107-108`, `web/lightbox.js:117-125`, `web/refine.js:90-96`, `web/status.js:180-199`

**Observed.**
- **The card.** On klein, the card's ⤢ has no `aria-disabled`, full opacity and its normal title.
- **The lightbox.** Its Refine button is `aria-disabled`, at opacity .45, titled "This checkpoint has no refine pass".
- **The click.** On the card's ⤢ it opens nothing, and writes a 12 px muted note in the panel, about 380 px to the left.
- **❐ on FLUX** behaves the same way.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-flow-1/shots/c01-klein-card-hover.png`, `c02-klein-after-click.png`, `c03-klein-lb-actions.png`.

**Why it matters.** The same capability is shown in two different states, and the card's answer is easy to miss.

**Recommendation.**
- A `syncCardTools()` sets `aria-disabled` and a reason title on ⤢ and ❐, from `refineSupported` and `referenceSupported`.
- Call it from `card()` and whenever status flips those capabilities.
- Style it with `[aria-disabled='true'] { opacity: .45 }`.
- Optionally, add a short-lived `aria-hidden` `.card-note`.
- Keep the panel slot (role=alert).

**Rule.** "A ⤢ on a checkpoint without a pass says so and opens nothing." Use `aria-disabled`, never `disabled`.

**Smokes.** `smoke-conditioning.json:28-29`: keep the glyph, the handler and the panel slot.

*M50 · from refine-flow-8 · adjusted (medium → low).*

#### U102. Export PNG covers only the pinned slice, omits the prompt, prints the export time, and gives every slice the same file name

**low** · common · effort S · grid · `web/sheet.js:441-445`, `518-543`

**Observed.**

- **The caption.** "run:… · pinned to ground grass, cfg 2" / "FLUX.1-Krea-dev · 1024×1024 · run … · 4 Oct, 04:05 pm". It has no prompt, and 04:05 is when the export was made; the run itself ran at 04:02.
- **File names.** Two slices both download as `sheet-50fde9555e4e.png`; Edge adds " (1)" to the second.

Screenshot: `verify-grid-2/m55-01-export.png`.

**Why it matters.** The export is "the one artefact worth keeping from a night", and without the prompt it cannot be understood later.

**Recommendation.**
- Add a prompt line: the top-left cell's prompt, plus the variables as declared.
- List the settings that have a single value.
- Print the oldest record's `created_at`, not the export time.
- Put the pins in the file name.
- Optionally, add "Export all pins", within `EXPORT_MAX_EDGE`.

**Rule.** design-notes "The sheet exports client-side". Exporting only the visible slice is the documented contract.

#### U103. Help tooltips run to 90-130 words at 11 px, and Strength's covers the fields it explains

**low** · common · effort S · copy-a11y · `backend/model_defaults.py:884-899`, `web/index.html:397-407`, `574-591`, `web/style.css:144-158`

**Observed.**
- **Search tip:** 129 words.
- **Resume tip:** 124 words; its bottom edge sits exactly at the 1305 px viewport edge.
- **Prompt variables tip:** 107 words.
- **Strength tip:** 91 words, 300x161, over Versions, Seed, the seed line and the preview.

All are 11 px. Screenshot: `verify-copy-a11y-1/m71-refine-strength-tip.png`.

**Why it matters.** Long tips get skipped, and the one sentence that tells the user what to do is lost with the rest.

**Recommendation.**
- **Strength.** Shorten `_REFINE_HINTS`'s supported text to about 40 words, with the action first. Keep "starts at {refine_strength:g} on this checkpoint", and move the shift theory to the README or the design notes.
- **Direction.** Open the dialog's Strength tip upward.
- **Resume.** Trim the tip to its instruction.
- **Search.** Keep its tip as a syntax card.
- **Size.** At 2000 px and wider, tips 320/380 px wide at 12.5 px.

Tips drawn from author guides keep their length.

**Rule.** Guide prose is the authors' own and is held by `test_every_cite_is_the_guides_own_words`. Only the app's own copy is shortened.

#### U104. One glyph or word means different things: ↻ is both Reuse and Re-render; Favourite, Favorite, Favorites and starred are mixed; and the card star's label ignores its state

**low** · common · effort S · gallery, copy-a11y · `web/gallery.js:100`, `142-151`, `209-213`, `226`, `web/generation.js:240`, `web/index.html:446`, `523`, `web/lightbox.js:117`, `web/panel.js:215`, `247`

**Observed.**
- **The card star's state.** On a starred card, the star's aria-label and title still say "Favorite" after a re-render. "Unfavorite" appears only after a toggle within the page, the title never changes, and there is no `aria-pressed`.
- **Four spellings of one idea.**
  - The delete confirm: "(favourite, seed 33)".
  - The tick: "Favorites".
  - The lightbox button: "★ Favourite".
  - Delete unstarred, and "3★" in the Runs picker.
- **Two meanings of ↻.** On cards it means Reuse; in the gallery head it is "↻ Re-render 3 at 1024×1024".
- **The lightbox** has four actions, with no ⊞ and no ❐.

Screenshots: `verify-copy-a11y-2/m77-head-search.png`, `m77-lb-side.png`.

**Why it matters.** Two terms for one concept sit beside the page's only bulk delete, and a screen reader is told the wrong state.

**Recommendation.**
1. **Fix the star's state.** Set its label, title and `aria-pressed` from `rec.favorite`, in both `card()` and `setFavorite`.
2. **One term.** "Star": tick "Starred", button "☆ Star" / "★ Starred", "Star (F)", "(starred, seed …)", "Starred unticked: …". If the user prefers "Favourite" (their commit ad24fe5 says "favourite star"), at least use one spelling everywhere.
3. **Optional.** Drop ↻ from Re-render.
4. **Optional.** Add "⊞ Show run" to the lightbox.

**Rule.** Keep the ⚠ mismatch glyph. The `favorites=` API parameter and the element ids are unchanged.

**Smokes.** `smoke-analysis.json:315` and `smoke-comparison.json:17` check the note's text.

*M77 · from gallery-15, copy-a11y-8 · adjusted.*

#### U105. Generate is 36 Tab presses from the prompt, past 17 size buttons and 7 help badges

**low** · common · effort M · panel · `web/sizes.js:7-35`, `74-95`, `web/index.html:166-171`, `web/app.js:256-259`

**Observed.**
- With real Tab presses from `#prompt`, the 36th press reaches Generate. On the way are 13 aspect buttons, 4 tier buttons, W, H, the badges, the LoRA select, Seed, Batch, Runs and the dice.
- The preset buttons have no `aria-pressed`, and the strips have no role.
- With a roving tabindex simulated in the page, Generate is reached on the 21st press.

**Why it matters.** A keyboard user who tabs to Steps or Seed passes 17 size buttons every time.

**Recommendation.**
- Give `#presets` and `#tiers` `role="group"` and aria-labels.
- Use a roving tabindex: the active button (or the first) at 0, the rest at −1. `syncSize` sets `aria-pressed`.
- Arrow, Home and End keys move focus across `strip.querySelectorAll('button')`; the portrait/landscape pairs are nested, so query buttons rather than children. Enter and Space choose.
- Advertise Ctrl+Enter (U86).

**Rule.** The help badges stay focusable, and `syncSize` still walks the buttons in `ASPECTS` order.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U108. The lightbox arrows are 40 px circles at the stage edges, and a near miss closes the lightbox

**low** · common · effort S · gallery · `web/style.css:880`, `927-946`, `web/lightbox.js`, `web/index.html:488`

**Observed.**
- **Layout at QHD.** Prev is at [28,633,40,40] and next at [2179,633,40,40]. A portrait image spans [630,18,987,1269], leaving about 560 px of backdrop on each side.
- **Near misses close.** Clicks 10 px or 42 px to the right of prev, 20 px above it, or 10 px to the left of next all closed the lightbox.
- **The cursor.** At fit, the stage shows a zoom-in cursor over an area where a click closes.
- **Lost place.** After walking to 42/96, a near miss left the walked-to card at y=1111 at QHD, and below the fold at 125%.

Screenshots: `verify-gallery-3/q-lightbox.png`, `q-lbmain-hover.png`.

**Why it matters.** A slight miss throws the user out of the lightbox, and out of their place in the gallery.

**Recommendation.**
- **Hit strips sized to the gap.**
  - `layoutLightbox` sets `--lb-gutter` to `clamp(48, gap − 8, 160)` at fit, and 48 when zoomed.
  - `.lb-nav { top: 20%; bottom: 20%; width: var(--lb-gutter); border: 0; background: transparent }`.
  - Draw the circle with `::before` at the outer edge.
- **Cursors.** `.lb-stage:not(.zoomed) { cursor: default }`, with `cursor: zoom-in` on the image itself.
- **Place.** On close, scroll the walked-to card into view (U43).

**Rule.** design-notes "The lightbox zooms": the arrows sit outside the stage; a click beside the image closes only at fit; zoomed, the whole stage is a pan surface. A full-height strip would break smoke.json:58's corner close and the pan surface.

**Smokes.** `smoke.json:58-60`: the 20% insets keep the corner close working.

#### U109. Turning off a sweep's sheet leaves the gallery filtered to that job

**low** · common · effort S · refine-flow · `web/panel.js:241-248`, `web/sheet.js:546-553`, `web/gallery.js:131-132`, `web/app.js:228`

**Observed.** Opening the sweep's sheet wrote "job:88b58dc00b80" into the search box, which had been empty. Turning Sheet off kept that search: the count read "(3)" of 6 images. Screenshot: `verify-refine-flow-1/shots/a09-after-sheet-off.png`.

**Why it matters.** A gallery showing 3 of its images looks as if the rest have gone. It costs an extra clear after every sweep.

**Recommendation.**
- **Remember the old search.** `showSheetScope` saves `sheetReturn = { search: previous, wrote }` when nothing is saved yet or the sheet is off.
- **One path.** Route the card's ⊞ through `showSheetScope` too.
- **Restore.** `setSheet(false)` restores the previous search when the box still holds what the page wrote.
- **Forget.** Clear `sheetReturn` when the user types in the search box.
- **Optional.** Show "(3 of 9)" in the count when the gallery is filtered.

**Rule.** `setSheet`'s "real escape hatch" covers layout and pins. Only a search the page itself wrote is restored.

**Smokes.** None affected: consecutive comparisons keep the original pre-sheet search.

**Observed.**

- **The finder's fix fails.** `margin-top` alone, at 1400x900, pushes the dialog 36.5 px past the bottom of the viewport without letting it scroll.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-2/shots/q-03-fold-open.png`, `x-qf-open.png`, `x-orig-refused.png`.

**Why it matters.** Controls move under the pointer, and a second click on the fold's summary misses.

**Recommendation.** `.refine-dialog { margin-top: max(16px, 8vh); margin-bottom: auto; max-height: calc(100vh - max(16px, 8vh) - 16px) }`. With this, the top stays at 104 at QHD in every state; the dialog fits 2048x1040; and at 1400x900 it scrolls, with a 16 px bottom gap. Skip the preview min-height.

**Rule.** `style.css:964-966` ("Scrolls on a short screen") holds, because the top margin is subtracted from `max-height`.

**Smokes.** None.

  - ArrowUp/Down step by 0.05, or 0.01 with Shift;

#### U112. Inserting between chips works only by right-click, and the hint for it is 10 px text that moves around

**low** · common · effort S · tags · `web/app.js:114-120`, `web/tags.js:303-347`, `392`, `432-436`, `web/style.css:252`, `258-262`

**Observed.**
- **Pointer behaviour.** Hovering over a 9 px gap marks nothing, and a left click does nothing. A right click in the gap opens the editor at index 5; after the last chip, at index 18 (the end). On the hint text, which wraps to row 2, it opens at index 0.
- **Keyboard.** The ContextMenu key on a chip inserts after it, and the chip's aria-label doesn't mention this.
- **The hint** is an `::after` at 10 px in `rgb(178,178,192)`. It sits at the start of row 2 with 18 tags, and after the last chip with 70.

Screenshots: `verify-tags-2/shots/v03-tray18.png`, `v13-tray70.png`, `v11-rightclick-gap.png`.

**Why it matters.** Most users will retype in the textarea instead.

**Recommendation.**
1. A real "+ tag" button after the last chip, hidden while the editor is open there.
2. Pointermove on the tray, throttled with `requestAnimationFrame` and only on the tray itself, marks the boundary with the existing `.drop-before`/`.drop-after` classes; a plain click there opens the inserter.
3. Pin the hint (absolute, at the bottom, 11 px) and reword it: "double-click edits · drag or Alt+←/→ moves · right-click a gap, or + tag, inserts".
4. Mention the Menu-key insert in the chip's aria-label.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: right-clicking a gap inserts, all mutations go through `writePromptTags`, and only one editor is open at a time.

**Smokes.** A "+ tag" child would break the steps that assert every tray child is a valid chip: smoke-animagine.json:12, smoke-illustrious.json:15, 23 and 26, and smoke-kohaku.json:14 and 19. Change them to `querySelectorAll('.tag-check')`. smoke-animagine.json:22-23 must still pass.

#### U113. An unknown tag shows only a red "?", and four different reasons look identical

**low** · common · effort S · tags · `web/tags.js:381-384`, `web/style.css:258`, `272-273`, `backend/tags.py:172-192`

**Observed.** Four different reasons all render as the same red "?" chip: "not found", "created after this checkpoint's 2025-01-07 cutoff", "deprecated" and "no posts". The reason is only in the chip's title, and the tray's label gives no count. Screenshot: `verify-tags-2/shots/v14-reasons.png`.

**Why it matters.** "Too new for this checkpoint" and "typo" need different fixes, and the chip doesn't say which it is.

**Recommendation.**
1. **One shared tooltip.** A single `#tag-tip`, outside the tray, positioned under the hovered or focused unknown chip. It shows `result.reason` plus "generation is still allowed", linked by `aria-describedby`.
2. **A reason code from the backend.** One of `not_found`, `deprecated`, `empty`, `no_date` or `after_cutoff`. For `after_cutoff`, show "⏲" with the same red outline.
3. **Counts in the label**, through `data-label`: "Prompt tags · Danbooru · 2 unknown".
4. **No automatic "did you mean".** The prefix search finds nothing for typical typos, and the double-click editor already autocompletes.

**Rule.** "Unknown" stays advisory and never blocks. WAI's cutoff stays None.

#### U116. A lightbox delete gives no acknowledgement beyond the counter and the image changing

Screenshots: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v2-lb-mid-zoomed.png`, `v2-lb-mid-after-del1.png`.

**Why it matters.** On near-identical sweep images, at a zoom carried over from the last image, the swap is easy to miss.

**Recommendation.**
- After `forgetImages` re-renders the lightbox, call `showError('Deleted image N (seed s).', true)`. This mirrors into `#lb-error`, which is role=alert.
- Clear the message on the next step.
- Optionally, add a 180 ms fade, guarded by `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Do not reset `lbView`.

**Rule.** design-notes "The lightbox zooms": the view carries across records of the same size.

#### U119. On SDXL the dialog's only size warning is FLUX's VRAM line, which fires at 2x though the SDXL pipeline was measured at 8.6 MP

**low** · common · effort S · refine on an SDXL checkpoint · `web/refine.js:190`, `web/sizes.js:121-123`, `docs/design-notes.md:633-635`, `docs/verified.md:367-369`, `395`

**Observed.** An 832x1216 Animagine source:
- at 1.5x the line reads "2.3 MP" with no warning;
- at 2x it reads "4.0 MP — 2× the source — may run tight on 16GB VRAM" in amber.

What verified.md records:

| Checkpoint | Measured |
| --- | --- |
| WAI v17 | strength-0.4 passes at 5.5 and 8.6 MP, with the card at 11,363 MiB |
| FLUX | fills the card at 2.4 MP and spills at 4.2 MP |
| Animagine, Kohaku, Illustrious | "Not measured" |

Screenshot: `gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint/shots/qhd-a09-2x.png`.

**Why it matters.** One global line understates the risk on FLUX (U6) and overstates it on SDXL.

**Recommendation.** Add a profile key, `refine_tight_pixels`, to `ModelStatus.defaults`. Null means "not measured", which keeps the 1600² line.
- **WAI v17:** no warning anywhere within the guard.
- **Z-Anime and FLUX.1 Krea dev:** about 2.4 MP.
- **The three unmeasured SDXL profiles:** keep the generic line, or say "WAI v17, the same SDXL pipeline, ran 8.6 MP on 16GB; this checkpoint is unmeasured".

Keep "16GB" in FLUX's wording, add the key to `tests/test_profiles.py`, and rewrite the design-notes sentence.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: "a typo guard, not a VRAM model: what fits is the checkpoint's (docs/verified.md)". Values come only from verified.md.

**Smokes.** smoke-refine.json:28, :31 ("16GB"), :52 and :57.

#### U120. The budget-stop message undercounts cells by one, the ETA ignores the budget, and the banner calls a budget stop "cancelled"

**low** · occasional · effort S · results · `web/generation.js:95-109`, `468`, `web/panel.js:178-188`, `backend/jobs.py:493-509`

**Observed.** A grid of 40 seeds at batch 2, with a 0.3-minute budget:
- **The stop message** reads "Time budget reached after 13/40 cells — kept 28 images." 28 images is 14 cells.
- **`/api/jobs`** reports run 14 and `stopped_by` "budget".
- **The banner** reads "Last run was cancelled … 28 images (14/40 cells)".
- **The ETA.** At 12 s the cell note said "~42s left"; the budget stopped the job about 7 s later.

Screenshots: `verify-results-2/shots/m02-panel-budget.png`, `m03-banner.png`.

**Why it matters.** Two counts for the same job contradict each other, and the ETA promises a finish that the budget will not allow.

**Recommendation.**
1. `generation.js:468` uses `data.run`, which already counts finished cells.
2. `panel.js:182` checks for "stopped at its time budget" before "was cancelled".
3. The ETA shows the smaller of the estimate and the budget left, computed from server timestamps only, marked "(budget)" when the budget is the limit.

**Rule.**
- design-notes "Time budget": a budget stop takes the cancel path and is identified by `stopped_by`.
- "waiting_on and the ETA": use the server clock only.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U125. "Reuse settings" on a refined image opens the Refine dialog instead of filling the panel

**low** · occasional · effort S · refine-flow · `web/gallery.js:162-184`, `368-370`, `409-422`, `web/lightbox.js:119`, `docs/design-notes.md:716-724`

**Observed.**
- **The button.** On pass 5 (a refine of 4), the lightbox button reads "Reuse settings", titled "Load these settings into the panel".
- **The click** opens "Refine image 4" at 1904 wide and leaves the panel unchanged.
- **The design notes** at lines 716-724 still describe the old panel mode.

**Why it matters.** A label that says "panel" and opens a modal dialog teaches the user that labels can't be trusted.

**Recommendation.**
- Pass `rec.refine` into `syncReuseButton`, through a data attribute.
- On a pass, show the following, with the ⚠ variant where it applies:
  - label "Reuse pass…";
  - title "Open the Refine dialog on #4 with this pass's size, strength and seed";
  - aria-label "Reuse this pass in the Refine dialog".
- Rewrite design-notes lines 716-724.

**Rule.** CLAUDE.md: Reuse on a refined record opens the dialog on its source. The checkpoint-mismatch warning still applies.

**Smokes.** smoke-flux.json:40 and smoke.json:115 are unaffected. Add an expectation to smoke-refine 118-123.

**low** · occasional · effort S · refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:108-113`, `148-150`, `web/gallery.js:162-178`, `365-371`

**Observed.** Pass 4 is a refine of 3 at strength 0.2, seed 9.

- The fold is closed, and its summary reads "… as image 4 was made".

- The ↻ button's title still says "Load these settings into the panel".

Screenshot: `verify-refine-dialog-2/shots/r-02-reuse-dialog.png`.

**Why it matters.** Until the fold note is spotted, the dialog looks as if it opened on the wrong image.

**Recommendation.** When the dialog opens with a pass to reuse:
- title it "Refine image 3 as image 4 was made";
- add `<p id="refine-reused">Reusing image 4: 1344×1728 at strength 0.2, seed 9</p>` to the source text, and include it in the dialog's `aria-describedby`.

Fix the ↻ title on refined records as in U125.

**Rule.** Opening on the source is deliberate.

#### U127. A refused Refine press repeats the preview's sentence in red instead of pointing at the field

**low** · occasional · effort S · refine-dialog · `web/refine.js:256-259`, `345-363`, `web/index.html:622-623`

**Observed.**
- **A strength refusal.** With Steps 4 and strength 0.1, the amber preview reads "strength 0.1 of 4 steps is less than one step; raise the strength or the steps". Pressing Refine adds the identical sentence in red.
  - The dialog grows from 833 to 865 px, and its top moves from 236 to 220.
  - Focus stays on Refine, and no field gets `aria-invalid`.
- **A size refusal.** The amber size line sits beside W/H, and the press adds the same clause in red at the bottom.

Screenshots: `verify-refine-dialog-2/shots/q-07-refused-dialog.png`, `q-08-size-refused.png`.

**Why it matters.** It reads as two problems, and it moves the dialog.

**Recommendation.**
- **Preview refusal.** Clear the preview and its warn class, then call `refineFailed(title)`. That leaves one red line, and the dialog doesn't jump. Set `aria-invalid` on Strength and focus it.
- **Size refusal.** Keep `#refine-error`, and set `aria-invalid` on W and focus it.
- Clear `aria-invalid` in `refineChanged`.

**Rule.**
- "A press says it in the dialog", so the press must not be silent.
- Don't open the fold to reach a field, because of the jump in U110.

**Smokes.** smoke-refine.json steps 59 and 84, and smoke-refine-unavailable.json step 18.

*M94 · from refine-dialog-10 · adjusted.*

#### U128. Range-syntax mistakes get misleading errors, and a spaced range on Mu silently becomes three cells

**low** · occasional · effort S · grid · `backend/grid.py:61-66`, `178-198`

**Observed.**

| Box | Input | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CFG | `2 - 5` | "CFG: '-' is only meaningful for mu" |
| any | `6-12 x2` | "'x2' is not a number" |
| any | `6..12` | "is not a number" |
| CFG | `2 -5` | "guidance_scale: Input should be greater than or equal to 0" |
| Mu | `0.8 - 1.2` | three cells: [0.8, None, 1.2] |

**Why it matters.** The first mistake a new user makes gets an error about a field they did not touch.

**Recommendation.**
- Before splitting on whitespace, join digit-dash-digit and digit-x-digit, e.g. `re.sub(r"(?<=\d)\s*-\s*(?=\d)", "-", text)` and the same for `[x*/]`. `_RANGE`'s existing `\s*` allowances then work.
- Inputs using ".." or "to" raise "<label>: write a range as 6-12 (or 6-12x2)".
- Drop the finder's proposed static hint line.

**Rule.**
- There is one server-side parser, so the preview and the run agree.
- A standalone "-" stays Mu's "none" token.

**Smokes.** None. `tests/test_grid.py:44-47` still pass; add rows for the new cases.

*M96 · from grid-13 · adjusted.*

**low** · occasional · effort S · analysis · `web/sheet.js:44-84`, `396-403`, `491`, `web/analysis.js:1022`

**Observed.**
- **The sheet.** The row heads read "variant original", "variant reordered", "variant rewrite" and "variant yours". Their title is empty, they are 90x17 at 11 px, and the rows are 518 px apart.

Screenshot: `verify-analysis-2/s-10-sheet-rows.png`.

**Recommendation.**
- **Display names**, on screen and in the export: original → Original, reordered → Reordered, rewrite → Rewrite, yours → Your version.
- **Axis label:** "Version".
- **What changed.** Each non-original row gets a diff excerpt of up to 80 characters from `promptChanges`, clamped to 4 lines.
- **Row title:** the full prompt.
- **Preview:** "version" instead of "variant".

**Rule.**
- The stored labels, and `stats --by variant`, are unchanged.
- Where the labels are ambiguous, Prompt stays a sheet axis.

**Smokes.**

**low** · occasional · effort S · analysis · `web/state.js:318-329`, `web/analysis.js:286`, `621`, `636`, `644`

**Observed.**

#### U131. The critic's wait shows no elapsed time, and the first-use download shows no progress

**low** · occasional · effort S · analysis · `web/analysis.js:561-588`, `590-660`, `web/index.html:99-104`

**Observed.**
- **First use.** The button reads "Thinking…", with the line "Downloading llama.cpp b11206 and fake critic (about 4.5 GB) once, then thinking…".
- **Later uses.** "Thinking… about 15 seconds on the GPU."
- **While it runs,** Generate, Run grid and Run comparison are held, and nothing on the page changes until the answer arrives.
- **Measured.** verified.md gives 114.2 s for the first `prepare()`.

Screenshot: `verify-analysis-2/q-02-inflight.png`.

**Why it matters.** "Slow" can't be told from "stuck" while every generation button is blocked.

**Recommendation.**
- After `#critic-line`, add `<span class="critic-clock hint" aria-hidden="true">`, updated every second ("· 12 s").
- Change the first-use line to "…once - this can take a few minutes - then thinking…".

There is no real download progress to show: `prepare()` exposes none.

**Rule.** `#critic-line` is a live region, so the clock must not go inside it. The timing promise applies only to the pinned model pair.

#### U132. "Checking" and "Danbooru unreachable" look identical, and a prompt over 64 items greys every chip indefinitely

**low** · occasional · effort S · tags · `web/tags.js:385-389`, `462-480`, `503`, `web/style.css:258`, `274`, `backend/schemas.py:840`

**Observed.**
- **Pending and offline.** For ordinary tags, the pending tray and the offline tray are byte-identical. Model tags do turn green offline.
- **Wrong message.** The pending tray's title reads "Lookup unavailable; …: Checking Danbooru…", and offline the label still says "Prompt tags · Danbooru".
- **Over 64 items.** With 65 items, every chip shows "…" indefinitely. The tray's title is "…tags: List should have at most 64 items after validation, not 65".

Screenshots: `verify-tags-2/shots/v19-pending-nomodel.png`, `v20-offline-nomodel.png`.

**Why it matters.** Users wait for ticks that will never come.

**Recommendation.**
- **State.** Add `data-state`: pending, checked or offline.
- **Pending.** The title reads "Checking Danbooru…".
- **Offline.** Show "–" on unchecked chips and an amber label, "Danbooru unreachable, not checked". Never red.
- **Over the cap.** Send the first 64 items and render the rest as unchecked, "past the 64-item lookup cap", with "· first 64 of N checked" in the label.

**Rule.**
- A remote failure means unchecked, never invalid.
- The 64-item cap is documented.

#### U134. Escape from the Examples menu drops focus to `<body>`, and the menu stays open over the prompt after focus leaves it

**low** · occasional · effort S · copy-a11y · `web/app.js:19-56`, `100`, `151`, `264`, `web/index.html:35-39`, `web/tags.js:695-700`

**Observed.**
- **Opening and arrows.** Enter opens the menu. ArrowDown does nothing; Tab reaches the first item.
- **Escape** closes the menu and leaves focus on `<body>`. Edge resumes Tab from the menu's position, so the finder's "must Tab from the top" does not hold.
- **Tabbing out** leaves the menu open, covering 71% of the prompt box on Animagine.
- **The Tags helper** drops focus the same way.

Screenshots: `verify-copy-a11y-2/m107-prompt-covered-crop.png`, `m107-panel-after-tabbing-out.png`.

**Why it matters.** A keyboard user loses the focus ring, and a menu stays stuck over the prompt.

**Recommendation.**
- **Escape.** On close, return focus to the button if focus was inside the menu.
- **Leaving.** Close the menu on focusout to anywhere outside `#examples`.
- **Arrow keys.** Use a roving tabindex with ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Home and End; ArrowDown on the button opens the menu.
- **Tags helper.** The same focus return for `showTagHelper`.

Keep each item the clickable `<li>`.

**Rule.** Picking an example still updates the preview, the panel and tag validation, and focuses the prompt.

**Smokes.** The Examples steps in smoke-anima, -animagine, -illustrious, -kohaku, -netayume, -newbie and -flux click `children[n]`, so the items must stay `<li>` elements.

#### U135. During an outage, every action says only "Failed to fetch", and the message outlives the outage

**low** · occasional · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `web/state.js:293-301`, `318-329`, `web/gallery.js:65-69`, `79-89`, `web/lightbox.js:95-96`, `web/status.js:230`

**Observed.**
- **Generate,** pressed while the page showed "reconnecting…", gave "Failed to fetch".
- **Check,** after the stream had given up, replaced "Lost the generation stream…" with the same message.
- **After recovery,** the message stayed on screen 18 s after the server was answering again.
- **The lightbox** showed a broken image (naturalWidth 0), with `#lb-error` empty.

Screenshots: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/blong-01-after-gen.png`, `blong-04-late.png`.

**Why it matters.** The browser's wording doesn't say that the app's server is gone, and a red error that stays after recovery suggests something is still broken.

**Recommendation.**
- `api()` wraps the fetch rejection as an Error, "No answer from the Local Diffusion server — is it still running?", with `offline: true`.
- `showError` remembers that an offline message is showing, and the first good status poll clears it.
- The lightbox image's `onerror` shows "This image could not be loaded — the server is not answering."
- Don't re-arm Load more.

**Rule.** After a failed page, Load more is the retry. `readableDetail` still formats the server's own errors.

**Smokes.** None.

*G1.5 · from gap-failure-and-recovery-5 · adjusted (medium → low). Implement it with U73.*

#### U136. After a lost stream, the blurred live preview of an unfinished cell stays in the viewer

**low** · occasional · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `web/generation.js:387-390`, `445-449`, `486-500`

**Observed.**
- After the stream gives up, `#viewer` holds the finished image plus a 320 px preview titled "live preview · step 11/28".
- `viewerRecords` holds only the finished image.
- The preview stayed through the server restart.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/blong-04-late.png`.

**Why it matters.** Next to "the run carries on", it reads as work still in progress.

**Recommendation.** Call `dropPreview()` in `onerror`'s give-up branch, before `setBusy(false)`. Keep the preview during the five reconnect attempts.

**Rule.** The preview is never a record.

**Smokes.** smoke-preview.json:8 and :10 are unaffected.
