#### U137. Delete unstarred on a scope that a running job is writing to can return 409, and the page then still shows the old count

**low** · occasional · effort S · destructive-actions · `web/gallery.js:278-327`, `backend/main.py:1416-1450`, `backend/storage.py:802-815`

**Observed.**
- **The setup.** A job landed an image every 3 s, with the search set to `run:<id>`.
- **The 409.** The dialog asked about 1 image and was answered after 4.5 s. The reply: "The selection changed: 2 unstarred images match now, not the 1 confirmed. Nothing was deleted; look again."
- **The stale count.** The count still read "(1)" with 1 card, while the server had 3; 3 s later it was unchanged.
- **The retry.** An immediate retry succeeded.
- **On the real card.** Images land every 10-43 s, so a collision would hit about 12% (FLUX) to 40% (WAI) of 5 s dialogs.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end/shots/v7-race-409.png`.

**Why it matters.** The guard works and nothing wrong is deleted, but the user can't tell why the delete failed, and the page doesn't show the new count.

**Recommendation.**
- **While a job is running,** add to the dialog: "A job is still running; if it adds an image here before you confirm, nothing is deleted."
- **On a 409 "selection changed",** call `resetGallery()` and `loadRuns()`. While a job is running, add: "A running job is still adding images here: press Delete unstarred again, or let it finish first."

**Rule.** design-notes "Delete unstarred": the confirmed count is checked under the write lock, and a sweep that gains images meanwhile is refused.

**Smokes.** None.

*G2.7 · from gap-destructive-actions-end-to-end-7 · adjusted: "fails every time" was refuted.*

- **Weight box placeholder.** "<adapter>: 0-1x0.25" when an adapter is picked.
- **Sheet heads.** "LoRA <adapter> <weight>" when every record shares one adapter. The export follows.

**Rule.** The LoRA weight is a grid axis only beside an adapter.

#### U139. Once clicked, the Size help tip covers the scale buttons and swallows clicks on 1.25×, 1.5× and 2×

**low** · occasional · effort S · refine on an SDXL checkpoint · `web/style.css:144-158`, `web/index.html:557-566`

**Observed.**
- **The tip stays open.** A real click on the Size `?` (1110,526) leaves its tip open at [1098,539,300,130].
- **Clicks are swallowed.** At the 1.25×, 1.5× and 2× buttons and at W, the element under the pointer is the tip. Real clicks on 2× and 1.25× left the size at 1248x1824, with focus on the badge.
- **Hover is enough.** With the pointer only hovering onto the tip, a click on 1.5× was also swallowed. The finder's own sweep ran at 4 MP instead of the intended 2.3 MP because of this.
- **Strength.** Its tip covers Strength, Versions and Seed.
- **The fix, tested.** Injecting `.tip { pointer-events: none }` let the clicks through, and the tip closed.
- **Text.** The pre-line breaks in the two dialog tips render ragged.

Screenshot: `verify-gap-refine-on-sdxl-tag-checkpoint/shots/qhd-t01-tip-after-click.png`.

**Why it matters.** One swallowed click leaves a 4 MP pass where 2.3 MP was intended.

**Recommendation.**
- Add `.tip { pointer-events: none }`, which fixes every `?` on the page.
- Optionally, open the Size tip upward in the dialog. Not the Strength tip: upward it would cover W/H.
- Write the two dialog tips on one source line each, or drop `pre-line` for them.

Do not add a `:focus-visible` rule: it would also hide tips that are tapped or clicked to read.

**Rule.** Tips stay reachable by keyboard: `.help` is focusable, and its tip shows on focus.

**Smokes.** None.

#### U140. A critic failure leaves the Critic block as if it was never asked, and its message leads with the exception's class name

**low** · rare · effort S · failure-and-recovery · `web/analysis.js:590-660`, `backend/main.py:814-831`

**Observed.**
- **A CriticError** put "CriticError: llama-server exited with code 1 before it was ready" in `#error` at [35,1003], while:
  - `#critic-result` stayed empty;
  - the line under Ask was unchanged;
  - focus stayed on Ask.
- **Other failures.** A download 502 quotes the Hub URL. A 503 says "CriticUnavailable: … sudo apt install libgomp1".

Screenshot: `verify-gap-failure-and-recovery/shots/d-error.png`.

**Why it matters.** After a 15 s wait the Critic block looks untouched, so the user asks again without knowing anything failed.

**Recommendation.**
- **Keep the failure apart.** Store it in a separate `critiqueFailure`, not in `critiqueResult`, set only by the `/api/prompt/critique` call's own try.
- **Show it in the block.** `renderCritic` shows one muted row: "The critic did not answer — llama-server stopped before it was ready. Ask again, or check the server log." Focus moves to `#critic-result` under the same rule as for an answer.
- **Clear it** on the next Ask, on clear, and when a new check replaces the text.
- **Server.** Drop the "CriticError: " and "CriticUnavailable: " prefixes from the HTTP detail, keeping them in the log.

**Rule.**
- A failed critique must leave nothing that Use or Apply could act on.
- The meanings of 422, 409, 502 and 503 are unchanged.

**Smokes.**
- smoke-analysis.json:98, 278 and 281 (`critiqueResult` null), and :286 ("check boom").
- tests/test_api_analysis.py:1017-1028.

- **All models off:** "Delete 199 unstarred images in the gallery (FLUX.1-Krea-dev only), keeping 3 starred?"
- **All models on:** "Delete 200 unstarred images in the gallery, keeping 4 starred?", though the preview's models were two checkpoints.
- **Samples.** The sample prompts are the first three alphabetically, out of about 50 runs.

- **Say how many runs.** `matching_preview` also returns the run count and the first and last creation times, for "… from 23 runs, 4 Oct 04:32–04:40 pm".

---

## 5. Set aside

One of the 142 candidates was set aside. None was left without a verdict.

- **G4.3**, "Check's tag-order fix and Use reordered can only change the panel's prompt; a pass's prompt can't be checked or fixed". **Duplicate of U65 (G4.1).** Both say the Refine dialog has no prompt check, and both are fixed by the same dialog-side analyze call. The order Apply would not have helped the example user either: an added detail tag that is moved into its documented slot, just before the quality suffix, is still past the 77-token cut on a 41-tag prompt. The cut warning, which U65 adds, is what helps.

Some claims inside the kept clusters were refuted while the rest stood. The entries above give the details; they are collected here so nobody relies on them:

- **U1:** walkthrough-8 said focusing a grid axis scrolled the page by 311 px; this did not reproduce. panel-1 said the smokes run with a static panel at 1400x900; this is false, because the panel is sticky there too.
- **U38:** the 223 px page scroll after Use or Apply did not reproduce.
- **U122:** the version texts were said to need a scroll; at QHD the critic's boxes are on screen beside the Compare labels.
- **U87:** raising CFG on FLUX was said to fail silently to engage the negative prompt. The negative box stays greyed, and its note names True CFG.
- **U88:** renaming the gallery picker "Grid runs…" would be wrong, because `/api/runs` lists every submission, plain Generate included.
- **U93:** the proposed wording "checks order only in comma-separated tag prompts" is false in both directions.
- **U95:** the group headings are a matter of taste, with no observed cost.
- **U137:** "fails every time at human speed" came from the fake's 3 s cadence; an immediate retry succeeded.
- **U79:** the lightbox counter's "6/96 of 144" follows from the code but did not reproduce.
- **U27:** "Unload enabled throughout the load" is wrong; it is disabled during a load the page is watching.
- **U101:** the card's ⤢ refusal lands on screen, not off it. Only the distance and the inconsistency stand.
- **Fixes rejected on rule grounds:**
  - an `overflow-y: auto` panel (U1);
  - order slots worked out in the client (U53, U85);
  - a server-side refusal of braces (U37);
  - 0.7 opacity for disabled controls (U24);
  - a full-height arrow strip in the lightbox (U108);
  - a `<kbd>` element inside Generate (U86).
- **Fixes rejected as broken:**
  - analysis-1's CSS as first written (U5);
  - the finders' aspect-ratio keys for the lightbox zoom (U3);
  - selecting before focusing (U50).

---

## 6. Strengths to keep

These were reported by the finders and confirmed in passing by the verifiers. A redesign should preserve each one.

**Generation and feedback**
- **Queueing never blocks.** Generate becomes "Generate (queue)" and `watchNext` attaches to the next job. A reload during a run reattaches with the finished image, the preview and the progress restored.
- **Accurate, specific status.**
  - The ETA is the job's own pace.
  - Grid cells name themselves: "cell 6/24 · 8 steps, CFG 5, mu off · ~7s left".
  - `waiting_on` separates loading from queueing.
  - The outcome lines are calm and specific: "Cancelled — kept 1 image.", "Finished 8 images in 10s. Only 4 distinct."
- **The live preview** appears from the first step and is visibly provisional. The eight-column `.many` tier fits QHD, and `VIEWER_MAX` keeps the DOM bounded.

**Panel and guidance**
- **The help is the checkpoint's own,** from its profile and authors' guides, not generic definitions. Controls that do not apply are greyed with a reason rather than hidden, so the panel keeps its shape across checkpoints.
- **The panel shows its arithmetic.**
  - The run hint turns Batch and Runs into numbers.
  - The grid preview is the server's own expansion: count, resolved values, prompts, ETA, disk space and duplicate warnings.
  - Resume's preview says what it will skip.
  - The size line quotes the guide's own word and adds the VRAM caveat.
- **Examples and persistence.** Examples are the authors' own prompts, built when the menu opens. Panel persistence tracks which controls the user owns and never restores Resume.

**Refine**
- **The dialog leaves the panel untouched.** Every change asks the server for a preview, so refusals arrive in the dialog, in the server's words, before anything is submitted.
- **The cost is stated before running:** "about 3 of 28 steps run", "2.3 MP — 1.5× the source", "seed 31, from the source's". Scale buttons past the guard disable themselves, and a sweep runs as one job that opens its sheet with Strength as the column axis.
- **Edge cases are handled honestly.**
  - Another checkpoint's image opens with the fold already open, naming the substitution.
  - Reuse on a pass reopens it as it ran.
  - When the source is gone, Reuse falls back with an explanation.
- **It fits the secondary screen.** At 2048x1040 the dialog fits with the fold open.

**Gallery, lightbox and sheet**
- **The lightbox:**
  - it zooms about the pointer, with explicit layout and no transform blur;
  - 1:1 is exact to the device pixel ratio, and the view carries across cells of the same size;
  - it has a full keyboard model, and it is a real `<dialog>` with a focus trap and focus restore.
- **Browsing.** The gallery pages itself as the user scrolls, smoothly, with 384 px lazy WebP thumbnails.
- **Delete unstarred is well guarded.** The count must be typed back, the scope is stated with the checkpoint and sample prompts, and the server pins the expected count. The per-card confirm names the seed and the prompt.
- **Small things done right.** Starred cards stand out at a glance. The ⚠ Reuse warning appears before the click. Re-render states its scope and size.
- **The sheet** picks its axes well, labels variables by name in declared order, walks the lightbox in reading order and stars in place. Its export burns in 14 px labels and a caption. The Runs picker works as a table of contents.

**Prompt check**
- **Traceability.** Every finding names what it rests on and links it. The critic cites numbered sources and says whose words they are.
- **It is fast and clear.** The stale state is clear and safe, Check runs instantly and cold, the encoder bars show the cut, and the rewrite diff is exact.
- **The comparison** states its cost before running, pairs seeds across versions and keeps versions in rows. The server says what it set aside and why. Use and Apply write through `replaceBoxText`, so Ctrl+Z works.

**Tags**
- **The chips are a true round-trip editor** through `writePromptTags`. In-place editing is well built, and right-click insertion lands at the exact boundary.
- **Readable at QHD.** Chips are 12 px light text on charcoal; unknown tags are advisory, never blocking.
- **Grounded in the guide.** The Tags helper is guide data rather than a hard-coded cloud. The size guidance is accurate. Autocomplete shows the category and the post count.

**Accessibility and copy**
- **Focus is handled.** Submit buttons use `aria-disabled`, so they keep focus and can explain a refusal. Focus returns correctly after Refine and Check.
- **Structure.** Images are focusable `role=button` elements with names, and the live regions are deliberate.
- **Copy.** Muted text was chosen for contrast (5.52:1, and 7.35:1 for `#a2a2b0`), and error copy is specific and actionable.
- **Keyboard.** Ctrl+Enter generates from anywhere, and the lightbox keys cover walking, starring, deleting and zooming.

---

## 7. Coverage

**The critic's assessment.** The finders covered the happy paths at QHD well. The eleven areas between them reached:
- the panel, the results, the gallery and the lightbox;
- Refine twice, on FLUX;
- grid and sheet, the prompt check and critic, and tag prompting on Animagine;
- copy and accessibility, and a task walkthrough.

Most of these also ran at 2048x1040 @1.25. The 108 merged clusters are well grounded.

Four kinds of state stood out as unreached:
1. **Failure and recovery.** A gated load, failed cells, a dead server, a critic error.
2. **Destructive actions taken to completion.** Every delete had been stubbed to capture its confirm.
3. **klein's references and LoRA.** The catalogue's only conditioning workflow.
4. **Refine on an SDXL tag checkpoint.** Every Refine observation had been on FLUX.

The critic briefed four gap finders on these areas. Their 34 findings became G1-G4 (U9, U10, U35, U36, U56-U67, U73-U78, U116-U119, U135-U141). They added the highs U9 and U10, and the two states that mislead the user outright: a job reported as still running after the server died (U9), and a green topbar over a dead server (U73).

**What remains unexamined.**
- **ERNIE's Expand and translation field.** The fake's expander answers instantly with "Expanded: <prompt> (WxH)", so judging it needs a wrapper.
- **The other guides' prompt shapes.** NewBie's XML in the 296 px prompt box, Z-Anime, and Anima's artist note.
- **The one-column layout below 901 px.** Not the target screen.
- **Real assistive technology.** No screen reader was run; the accessibility findings come from the DOM and Chrome's accessibility tree.
- **Two tabs on one server.**
- **The layout fixes together.** Whether U5, U12, U14, U19, U23 and U28 compose when applied together was not tested. Each was injected alone. Only the three directions combined several, and none of them was run against the smokes.
- **Real GPU behaviour.** All runs used fake servers. Sigma arithmetic (U6) and VRAM figures (U119) rest on `docs/verified.md`. Animagine, Kohaku and Illustrious refine passes past 4 MP are unmeasured on the card. The fake sends preview frames even when it claims an SDXL checkpoint, so U67 was reproduced by dropping frames in the page.
- **Inside the directions.** The Studio direction's tag menu clipping on tag checkpoints was not exercised, and the Loop direction's keyboard layer was not tested for conflicts with typing in text boxes.
- **The tooling.** One verifier's driver collided with another agent's DevTools port and was hijacked mid-run (U83). That run was discarded and redone, but other long-wait runs could have been affected the same way. `scratchpad/ux/find-analysis/drive.mjs` chooses a free port and avoids this.
